=== AGENTS.md ===
# AGENTS.md â€” OMNI stack & operating notes

- What: Python 3.14 desktop-agent prototype (FastAPI + pydantic + SQLite) extended into a Hyprland-native research system.
- Not a git repo (as of Phase 0). Record source SHA-256 manifest if git hash is unavailable.
- Entry point: `python3 -m omni.main <cmd>` (`status`, `inspect-desktop`, `submit-task`, `run-demo`, `dashboard`, `self-test`).
- Package: `omni/` â€” `config.py`, `orchestrator/`, `planner/`, `adapters/`, `safety/`, `verification/`, `storage/`, `api/`, `schemas/`.
- UI today: local web dashboard in `dashboard/` served at `/static/index.html`. Quickshell 0.3.2 is installed; use it for the Hyprland HUD (no new UI framework).
- Core logic lives in `omni/orchestrator/engine.py` (action registry + verification); extend it, do not rewrite.
- New work (planned): `omni/context/` (Hyprland/AT-SPI collector), `omni/routing/` (A1 IARS), `omni/modules/` (A3 AMPL), `omni/scheduler/` (A2 KRLS), `hud/` (Quickshell), `bench/` (tasks, adapters, runner, report).
- LLM access must go through one client with a disk cache keyed by hash(model+prompt+params). Set `OMNI_LLM_BACKEND=ollama|openai|mock`; `--mock-llm` tags every log record `"mock": true`.
- Mock/cached-replay runs are excluded from paper figures. Missing data produces no figure and a printed list of missing runs.
- Environment: Hyprland 0.56.2 live (`HYPRLAND_INSTANCE_SIGNATURE` set), kernel 7.0.0-42-generic with `CONFIG_SCHED_CLASS_EXT=y`, cgroup v2, `grim`, `bpftrace`, `hyprctl`, `quickshell`, `ags`, `gjs`.
- Installed agent CLIs: OpenCode only. No `ollama` server (11434 closed), no PyAT-SPI (use `gdbus`/AT-SPI DBus fallback), no Claude/Aider/Interpreter (bench records "not available").
- Run tests: `python3 -m pytest -q` (48 passing; do not add slow tests to this command).
- Run demo: `python3 -m omni.main run-demo --workspace ./workspace/demo`.
- Run dashboard: `python3 -m omni.main dashboard --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8000`.
- Routing: `python3 -m omni.main route --once` (AMPL on; `--no-modules`, `--budget-mb N`); scheduler: `python3 -m omni.main schedule --once` (`--apply`, `--restore`, `--simulate S1|S2|S3|all`); HUD: `python3 -m omni.main hud` (Quickshell reads `~/.cache/omni/hud/state.json` or `OMNI_HUD_STATE`).
- Bench: `python3 -m omni.main bench --list`; smoke `bench --smoke --adapter mock --results DIR --workspaces DIR`; full mock `bench --tasks all --adapter mock ...`; report `bench --report --results DIR --tasks ... --adapters opencode --seeds 0`. Mock/cached records are tagged and excluded from figures; missing real runs suppress figures and are printed.
- Privacy: never persist raw keystrokes; screenshots are encoded locally and not stored unless `OMNI_DEBUG_FRAMES=1`.
- Safety: no kernel modules; scheduling actuation via cgroup v2 + nice/ionice, optional sched_ext behind the same interface; kill switch restores defaults. Scheduler actuation is dry-run unless `--apply`; it never moves a process without an explicit pid.
- Honest stubs are marked `STUB:` in code and listed in `STATUS.md`. No invented measurements anywhere.
- Phase discipline: stop after each phase, report 5 lines, wait for "continue".
- Status/resume file: `STATUS.md` (update at the end of every phase).

=== README.md ===
# OMNI

OMNI is a lightweight desktop orchestration prototype for local task execution and verification. It focuses on a safe, workspace-bound planner, a small action registry, a CLI, a local dashboard, and a deterministic end-to-end demo that can complete without a remote model.

## Quick start

```bash
python3 -m pip install -e .
python3 -m omni.main status
python3 -m omni.main run-demo --workspace ./workspace/demo
python3 -m omni.main dashboard --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8000
```

## Features

- Task intake and structured plan generation.
- Safe action registry with permission checks.
- Dependency-aware orchestrator with retries and cancellation.
- Local verification engine for file, process, and test outcomes.
- Hyprland-aware desktop adapter with graceful fallback.
- Web dashboard and FastAPI API.
- Demo mode for deterministic delivery.

## Architecture

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    User --> CLI
    User --> Dashboard
    CLI --> API
    Dashboard --> API
    API --> Orchestrator
    Orchestrator --> Registry
    Registry --> Safety
    Registry --> Desktop
    Orchestrator --> Verification
    Orchestrator --> Storage
    Planner --> Orchestrator
```

## Safety model

- All filesystem actions are limited to the configured workspace.
- No arbitrary shell commands are allowed.
- Only registered action names can be executed.
- User approval is required for consequential actions.
- Verification is mandatory before a step is marked successful.

## Testing

```bash
pytest -q
```

=== STATUS.md ===
# OMNI status â€” resume here

Updated: Phase 4 (bench harness + mock smoke) â€” 2026-11-09

## Done
- Phase 0 baseline repairs: planner multi-line strings, API static dir, TestClient lifespan; added CLI `__main__` guard (`python3 -m omni.main` works).
- Phase 1: `omni/llm/` cached client (SHA-256 backend+model+prompt+params), telemetry JSONL; `omni/context/` Hyprland/AT-SPI collector, 198-dim feature map, clipboard *types* only, optional low-res `grim`; A1 IARS (Bayesian linear models, Thompson sampling, embedding priors, cost/load penalties, softmax confidence, top-2 lexical verifier, implicit reward); HUD routing panel; CLI `inspect-context`, `route`, `hud`.
- Phase 2: `omni/modules/` capability units, 11-module library, app descriptor â†’ unit demand, real memory pressure/budget, AMPL greedy selection vs best single module, capability-miss boost/eviction, residency accounting; HUD modules panel; CLI `route --no-modules`, `route --budget-mb`.
- Phase 3:
  - `omni/scheduler/krls.py` â€” online Kernel Recursive Least Squares, RBF kernel, ALD sparsification, block Shermanâ€“Morrison inverse updates, fixed dictionary budget.
  - `omni/scheduler/context.py` â€” real `/proc/loadavg`, `/proc/pressure/cpu`, memory pressure, focused-window interactivity/focus age â†’ 6 bounded features.
  - `omni/scheduler/policy.py` â€” per-action KRLS reward models (`boost` / `balanced` / `throttle`), contextual argmax, design-prior fallback while untrained.
  - `omni/scheduler/actuator.py` â€” cgroup v2 backend (cpu.weight / io.weight); dry-run by default; writes only when `--apply`; optional pid assignment and nice/ionice commands; `restore()` kill switch.
  - `omni/scheduler/simulator.py` â€” S1 interactive, S2 memory-pressure batch, S3 idle; simulated only, explicitly labelled.
  - `omni/scheduler/service.py` + `RoutingService` integration â†’ scheduler decision, features, applied/dry-run, actuator status in HUD state and `scheduler.jsonl`.
  - HUD scheduler panel: action + policy, cpu/io/nice, dry-run/applied, cgroup availability, kill-switch readiness.
  - CLI: `schedule --once`, `schedule --apply`, `schedule --restore`, `schedule --simulate S1|S2|S3|all`; `route --no-scheduler`, `route --apply-scheduler`.
  - Tests: **48 passing** (`python3 -m pytest -q`).
- Phase 4:
  - `bench/tasks.py` â€” 30 deterministic offline tasks across files/documents/code/data/terminal/planning, each with declared initial files, a fixture for the mock adapter and a programmatic verifier (file content, imports, CLI output, pytest, make, sqlite, JSON/JSONL).
  - `bench/adapters.py` â€” `mock` (fixture, always `mock=true`), `opencode` (installed CLI, `run --auto --format json`), and explicit unavailable adapters (`claude`, `aider`, `interpreter`) that record the reason instead of fabricating runs.
  - `bench/runner.py` â€” per-run workspace materialisation, environment capture (load/cpu/memory), JSONL run log (`runs.jsonl`), statuses `success|failure|error|timeout|unavailable`.
  - `bench/report.py` â€” strict real-run aggregation; excludes `mock` and `cached_replay`; lists unavailable and missing runs; refuses figures if any expected real run is missing; writes `report.json`, `report.md`, `runs.csv`, and matplotlib PNG/SVG.
  - `tests/test_bench.py` â€” suite integrity, materialisation, 3-task mock runner, mock exclusion/no-figure behavior, complete-matrix figure generation, missing-run refusal, CLI smoke.
  - Mock validation: 30/30 tasks pass via the mock adapter; 3-task smoke report has `real_records=0`, `excluded.mock=3`, no `figures/` directory, and prints the 3 missing real runs. **Mock smoke is excluded from paper figures.**
  - Tests: **48 passing** (`python3 -m pytest -q`).
- Live checks (real Hyprland, mock-tagged):
  - `schedule --once` â†’ `boost` / `heuristic-prior`; features `load 0.2358`, `cpu pressure 0.0482`, `mem pressure 0.0`, `interactive 1.0`; dry-run, no writes.
  - Detected cgroup v2 base `.../user@1000.service` (cpu delegated); kill switch `schedule --restore` returns `restored`.
  - Simulated learning: S1 `0.705` vs balanced `0.4685` (oracle `0.705`, 100% action accuracy); S2 `1.2399` vs `0.2230` (oracle `1.2399`, 100%); S3 `0.4667` vs `0.4291` (oracle `0.4689`, 90%). **Simulated, not paper data.**
  - `route --once` combines routing + modules + scheduler in HUD state; Quickshell loads with no QML errors; crop shows accent/ink/warn pixels.

## Run
```bash
python3 -m pytest -q
python3 -m omni.main inspect-context
python3 -m omni.main route --once --mock-llm --hud-state /tmp/omni_hud_state.json
python3 -m omni.main route --once --no-modules --no-scheduler
python3 -m omni.main schedule --once
python3 -m omni.main schedule --simulate all --steps 400
python3 -m omni.main schedule --restore          # kill switch
python3 -m omni.main hud                         # Quickshell; OMNI_HUD_STATE overrides state path
OMNI_SCREENSHOT=1 python3 -m omni.main inspect-context
python3 -m omni.main bench --list
python3 -m omni.main bench --smoke --adapter mock --results /tmp/omni_bench --workspaces /tmp/omni_bench_ws
python3 -m omni.main bench --tasks all --adapter mock --results /tmp/omni_bench --workspaces /tmp/omni_bench_ws
python3 -m omni.main bench --report --results /tmp/omni_bench --tasks files-create-notes,files-rename,files-move --adapters opencode --seeds 0
```
LLM env: `OMNI_LLM_BACKEND=mock|ollama|openai`, `OMNI_LLM_MODEL`, `OMNI_OLLAMA_URL`, `OMNI_LLM_BASE_URL`, `OMNI_LLM_API_KEY`, `OMNI_CACHE_DIR`; `OMNI_LLM_MOCK=1` or `--mock-llm` forces mock. Routing/AMPL/scheduler make no LLM calls today.

## Stubs / honest limits
- **Live KRLS is untrained:** no reward signal is observed yet, so live decisions report `policy="heuristic-prior"`. Phase 4 bench must feed real outcomes via `SchedulerService.observe`.
- **Simulator rewards are synthetic** and must never be reported as measurements.
- **Module sizes/params/coverage are hand-authored design priors**, not measured; no real LoRA/tool-schema artifacts are loaded.
- cgroup actuation is dry-run unless `--apply`; it writes weights at the group level but does not move any process without an explicit pid. No sched_ext backend yet (interface allows one later).
- Memory-pressure mapping is a documented design choice: `psi_some_avg10 / 50` and available-below-35% ramp; budget = max Ã— max(0.15, 1 âˆ’ pressure).
- IARS priors use a curated lexical embedding (`omni/context/lexicon.py`), not a neural embedding; `LLMClient.embed` is available but not wired into priors.
- Top-2 decision is returned (`explored`, `candidates`) but there is no agent executor yet; Phase 4 bench must execute both when `explored=true`.
- AT-SPI histogram is global (not attributed to the active window) and bounded; no PyAT-SPI installed, gdbus fallback used.
- Screenshot capture off by default (`OMNI_SCREENSHOT=1`); only brightness/edge stats, no storage unless `OMNI_DEBUG_FRAMES=1`.
- HUD validated by Quickshell load + pixel inspection; screenshot figure capture is deferred.
- **Bench Phase 4 smoke used the mock fixture adapter only.** It validates the harness/verifiers/report, not agent capability; every mock record is tagged `mock=true` and excluded from figures.
- No real OpenCode bench run has been executed yet; the `opencode` adapter path (`run --auto --format json`) still needs validation against the installed CLI and a chosen model.
- `claude`, `aider`, `interpreter` are recorded `unavailable` (not installed); no numbers are produced for them.
- Bench task verifiers are deterministic and workspace-local; verifier subprocesses run with the workspace as cwd, no network or host writes.
- Scheduler KRLS is not yet fed by bench outcomes; wiring `SchedulerService.observe` to real task results remains Phase 5.

=== bench/adapters.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import shutil
import subprocess
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from bench.tasks import BenchTask


@dataclass
class AdapterResult:
    ok: bool
    duration_s: float
    mock: bool = False
    cached_replay: bool = False
    returncode: int | None = None
    output: str = ""
    error: str | None = None
    timed_out: bool = False
    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "ok": self.ok,
            "duration_s": round(self.duration_s, 4),
            "mock": self.mock,
            "cached_replay": self.cached_replay,
            "returncode": self.returncode,
            "output_tail": self.output[-800:],
            "error": self.error,
            "timed_out": self.timed_out,
            "metadata": self.metadata,
        }


class Adapter:
    name = "base"
    mock = False

    def available(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        return True, ""

    def run(self, task: BenchTask, workspace: Path) -> AdapterResult:
        raise NotImplementedError


class MockAdapter(Adapter):
    """Deterministic fixture adapter. Always tagged mock; excluded from figures."""

    name = "mock"
    mock = True

    def run(self, task: BenchTask, workspace: Path) -> AdapterResult:
        started = time.monotonic()
        if task.fixture is None:
            return AdapterResult(False, time.monotonic() - started, mock=True, error="task has no fixture")
        try:
            task.fixture(workspace)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            return AdapterResult(False, time.monotonic() - started, mock=True, error=f"fixture failed: {exc}")
        return AdapterResult(
            True,
            time.monotonic() - started,
            mock=True,
            output="fixture applied",
            metadata={"adapter": "fixture", "excluded_reason": "mock run"},
        )


class OpenCodeAdapter(Adapter):
    """Run the installed OpenCode CLI non-interactively in the task workspace."""

    name = "opencode"
    mock = False

    def __init__(self, model: str | None = None, timeout_pad_s: float = 10.0) -> None:
        self.model = model
        self.timeout_pad_s = timeout_pad_s

    def available(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        binary = shutil.which("opencode")
        if not binary:
            return False, "opencode CLI not installed"
        return True, binary

    def run(self, task: BenchTask, workspace: Path) -> AdapterResult:
        available, detail = self.available()
        started = time.monotonic()
        if not available:
            return AdapterResult(False, 0.0, error=detail)
        prompt = (
            f"{task.goal}\n\n"
            "Work only inside the current directory. Do not ask clarifying questions. "
            "Make the required file changes directly, then stop."
        )
        command = [
            detail,
            "run",
            "--auto",
            "--format",
            "json",
            "--title",
            f"OMNI bench {task.id}",
        ]
        if self.model:
            command.extend(["--model", self.model])
        command.append(prompt)
        try:
            proc = subprocess.run(
                command,
                cwd=workspace,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=task.timeout_s + self.timeout_pad_s,
                check=False,
            )
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
            return AdapterResult(
                False,
                time.monotonic() - started,
                timed_out=True,
                error=f"timed out after {exc.timeout}s",
            )
        except OSError as exc:
            return AdapterResult(False, time.monotonic() - started, error=str(exc))
        output = (proc.stdout or "") + (proc.stderr or "")
        metadata = _parse_usage(proc.stdout or "")
        metadata["adapter"] = "opencode"
        metadata["command"] = command[:7]
        return AdapterResult(
            ok=proc.returncode == 0,
            duration_s=time.monotonic() - started,
            returncode=proc.returncode,
            output=output,
            error=None if proc.returncode == 0 else f"opencode exited {proc.returncode}",
            metadata=metadata,
        )


class UnavailableAdapter(Adapter):
    def __init__(self, name: str, reason: str) -> None:
        self.name = name
        self.reason = reason

    def available(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        return False, self.reason


def _parse_usage(stdout: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    metadata: dict[str, Any] = {}
    events = 0
    for line in stdout.splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if not line.startswith("{"):
            continue
        try:
            event = json.loads(line)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            continue
        events += 1
        if isinstance(event, dict):
            for key in ("sessionID", "session_id"):
                if key in event:
                    metadata["session_id"] = event[key]
            usage = event.get("usage")
            if isinstance(usage, dict):
                metadata["usage"] = usage
            tokens = event.get("tokens")
            if isinstance(tokens, dict):
                metadata["tokens"] = tokens
    metadata["json_events"] = events
    return metadata


def get_adapter(name: str) -> Adapter:
    if name == "mock":
        return MockAdapter()
    if name == "opencode":
        return OpenCodeAdapter()
    if name == "claude":
        return UnavailableAdapter("claude", "Claude CLI not installed")
    if name == "aider":
        return UnavailableAdapter("aider", "Aider CLI not installed")
    if name == "interpreter":
        return UnavailableAdapter("interpreter", "Open Interpreter not installed")
    raise KeyError(f"unknown adapter: {name}")


ADAPTER_NAMES: tuple[str, ...] = ("opencode", "claude", "aider", "interpreter")

=== bench/report.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable

from bench.tasks import TASKS_BY_ID


def _rate(successes: int, attempts: int) -> float | None:
    return round(successes / attempts, 4) if attempts else None


def _aggregate(records: Iterable[dict[str, Any]], key: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    buckets: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    for record in records:
        buckets.setdefault(str(record.get(key, "unknown")), []).append(record)
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for name, items in sorted(buckets.items()):
        attempts = [item for item in items if item.get("status") != "unavailable"]
        successes = [item for item in attempts if item.get("status") == "success"]
        durations = [float(item.get("duration_s") or 0.0) for item in attempts]
        rows.append(
            {
                key: name,
                "records": len(items),
                "attempts": len(attempts),
                "successes": len(successes),
                "success_rate": _rate(len(successes), len(attempts)),
                "mean_duration_s": round(sum(durations) / len(durations), 4) if durations else None,
                "unavailable": len(items) - len(attempts),
            }
        )
    return rows


def _missing(task_ids: list[str], adapters: list[str], seeds: list[int], records: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    present = {(record.get("task_id"), record.get("adapter"), int(record.get("seed", 0))) for record in records}
    missing: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for task_id in task_ids:
        for adapter in adapters:
            for seed in seeds:
                if (task_id, adapter, seed) not in present:
                    missing.append({"task_id": task_id, "adapter": adapter, "seed": seed})
    return missing


def _write_figures(rows: list[dict[str, Any]], figures_dir: Path, key: str, value: str, filename: str, title: str) -> list[str]:
    usable = [row for row in rows if row.get(value) is not None]
    if not usable:
        return []
    try:
        import matplotlib

        matplotlib.use("Agg")
        import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        return []
    figures_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    labels = [str(row[key]) for row in usable]
    values = [float(row[value]) for row in usable]
    figure, axis = plt.subplots(figsize=(max(5.0, 1.1 * len(labels) + 2.0), 3.6))
    bars = axis.bar(labels, values, color="#4c78a8")
    axis.set_title(title)
    axis.set_ylabel(value.replace("_", " "))
    axis.spines[["top", "right"]].set_visible(False)
    axis.tick_params(axis="x", rotation=20)
    for bar, value_item in zip(bars, values):
        axis.text(bar.get_x() + bar.get_width() / 2, bar.get_height(), f"{value_item:.2f}", ha="center", va="bottom", fontsize=8)
    figure.tight_layout()
    paths = [figures_dir / f"{filename}.png", figures_dir / f"{filename}.svg"]
    for path in paths:
        figure.savefig(path, dpi=160)
    plt.close(figure)
    return [str(path) for path in paths]


def build_report(
    records: list[dict[str, Any]],
    task_ids: list[str],
    adapters: list[str],
    seeds: list[int],
    results_dir: str | Path,
    figures_dir: str | Path | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    results_dir = Path(results_dir)
    figures_dir = Path(figures_dir) if figures_dir else results_dir / "figures"
    task_ids = [task_id for task_id in task_ids if task_id in TASKS_BY_ID]
    real = [record for record in records if not record.get("mock") and not record.get("cached_replay")]
    excluded = {
        "mock": sum(1 for record in records if record.get("mock")),
        "cached_replay": sum(1 for record in records if record.get("cached_replay") and not record.get("mock")),
    }
    unavailable = [record for record in real if record.get("status") == "unavailable"]
    attempts = [record for record in real if record.get("status") != "unavailable"]
    missing = _missing(task_ids, adapters, seeds, real)

    by_adapter = _aggregate(real, "adapter")
    by_category = _aggregate(attempts, "category")

    figure_paths: list[str] = []
    figure_status: str
    if missing:
        figure_status = f"not generated: {len(missing)} missing run(s); see missing_runs"
    elif not attempts:
        figure_status = "not generated: no real completed runs"
    else:
        figure_paths.extend(_write_figures(by_adapter, figures_dir, "adapter", "success_rate", "success_by_adapter", "Success rate by adapter (real runs)"))
        figure_paths.extend(_write_figures(by_category, figures_dir, "category", "mean_duration_s", "duration_by_category", "Mean duration by category (s)"))
        figure_status = "generated" if figure_paths else "not generated: no plotting backend"

    report = {
        "schema": 1,
        "generated": time.time(),
        "results_dir": str(results_dir),
        "task_ids": task_ids,
        "adapters": adapters,
        "seeds": seeds,
        "records_total": len(records),
        "real_records": len(real),
        "excluded": excluded,
        "unavailable_runs": [
            {"task_id": record.get("task_id"), "adapter": record.get("adapter"), "seed": record.get("seed"), "reason": record.get("verification")}
            for record in unavailable
        ],
        "missing_runs": missing,
        "by_adapter": by_adapter,
        "by_category": by_category,
        "figures": figure_paths,
        "figure_status": figure_status,
        "paper_rule": "Mock and cached-replay runs are excluded from paper figures.",
    }

    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (results_dir / "report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    _write_csv(attempts, results_dir / "runs.csv")
    (results_dir / "report.md").write_text(_markdown(report), encoding="utf-8")
    return report


def _write_csv(records: list[dict[str, Any]], path: Path) -> None:
    columns = ["run_id", "task_id", "category", "adapter", "seed", "status", "duration_s", "mock", "cached_replay", "verification"]
    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=columns, extrasaction="ignore")
        writer.writeheader()
        for record in records:
            writer.writerow({key: record.get(key) for key in columns})


def _markdown(report: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    lines = [
        "# OMNI bench report",
        "",
        f"- Generated: {time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S', time.localtime(report['generated']))}",
        f"- Real records: {report['real_records']} (excluded mock: {report['excluded']['mock']}, cached replay: {report['excluded']['cached_replay']})",
        f"- Results: `{report['results_dir']}`",
        f"- Figure status: {report['figure_status']}",
        "",
        "## By adapter",
        "",
        "| adapter | attempts | successes | success rate | mean s | unavailable |",
        "|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|",
    ]
    for row in report["by_adapter"]:
        rate = "--" if row["success_rate"] is None else f"{row['success_rate']:.3f}"
        mean = "--" if row["mean_duration_s"] is None else f"{row['mean_duration_s']:.2f}"
        lines.append(f"| {row['adapter']} | {row['attempts']} | {row['successes']} | {rate} | {mean} | {row['unavailable']} |")
    lines += ["", "## By category", "", "| category | attempts | successes | success rate | mean s |", "|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|"]
    for row in report["by_category"]:
        rate = "--" if row["success_rate"] is None else f"{row['success_rate']:.3f}"
        mean = "--" if row["mean_duration_s"] is None else f"{row['mean_duration_s']:.2f}"
        lines.append(f"| {row['category']} | {row['attempts']} | {row['successes']} | {rate} | {mean} |")
    lines += ["", "## Exclusions", "", report["paper_rule"], ""]
    if report["unavailable_runs"]:
        lines += ["## Unavailable", ""]
        for item in report["unavailable_runs"]:
            lines.append(f"- {item['task_id']} / {item['adapter']} / seed {item['seed']}: {item['reason']}")
        lines.append("")
    if report["missing_runs"]:
        lines += ["## Missing runs", ""]
        for item in report["missing_runs"]:
            lines.append(f"- {item['task_id']} / {item['adapter']} / seed {item['seed']}")
        lines.append("")
    figures = report["figures"]
    if figures:
        lines += ["## Figures", ""]
        lines += [f"- `{path}`" for path in figures]
    else:
        lines += ["## Figures", "", f"No figure generated. {report['figure_status']}"]
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"

=== bench/runner.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable

from bench.adapters import Adapter
from bench.tasks import BenchTask, materialise
from omni.context.features import ContextSnapshot
from omni.modules.memory import measure_memory
from omni.scheduler.context import collect_features


def _safe(value: str) -> str:
    return "".join(ch if ch.isalnum() or ch in {"-", "_"} else "-" for ch in value)


class BenchRunner:
    def __init__(self, results_dir: str | Path, workspaces_dir: str | Path) -> None:
        self.results_dir = Path(results_dir)
        self.workspaces_dir = Path(workspaces_dir)
        self.results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.workspaces_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.log_path = self.results_dir / "runs.jsonl"

    def _environment(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        snapshot = ContextSnapshot(timestamp=time.time(), app_class="bench", title="benchmark")
        features = collect_features(snapshot)
        memory = measure_memory()
        return {
            "load_norm": round(features.load_norm, 4),
            "cpu_pressure": round(features.cpu_pressure, 4),
            "mem_pressure": round(memory.pressure, 4),
            "mem_available_mb": round(memory.mem_available_mb, 1),
            "cpu_count": os.cpu_count() or 1,
        }

    def run_one(self, task: BenchTask, adapter: Adapter, seed: int = 0) -> dict[str, Any]:
        stamp = int(time.time() * 1000)
        run_id = f"{_safe(task.id)}--{_safe(adapter.name)}--s{seed}--{stamp}"
        workspace = self.workspaces_dir / run_id
        materialise(task, workspace)
        environment = self._environment()
        record: dict[str, Any] = {
            "schema": 1,
            "run_id": run_id,
            "task_id": task.id,
            "category": task.category,
            "adapter": adapter.name,
            "seed": seed,
            "mock": bool(adapter.mock),
            "cached_replay": False,
            "started": time.time(),
            "environment": environment,
            "workspace": str(workspace),
        }

        available, detail = adapter.available()
        if not available:
            record.update(
                {
                    "status": "unavailable",
                    "duration_s": 0.0,
                    "verification": detail,
                    "adapter_detail": detail,
                }
            )
            self._append(record)
            return record

        result = adapter.run(task, workspace)
        record.update(
            {
                "duration_s": result.duration_s,
                "adapter_result": result.as_dict(),
                "mock": bool(adapter.mock or result.mock),
                "cached_replay": bool(result.cached_replay),
            }
        )
        if result.timed_out:
            record["status"] = "timeout"
            record["verification"] = result.error or "timed out"
        elif not result.ok:
            record["status"] = "error"
            record["verification"] = result.error or "adapter failed"
        elif task.verify is None:
            record["status"] = "error"
            record["verification"] = "task has no verifier"
        else:
            try:
                passed, verification = task.verify(workspace)
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                passed, verification = False, f"verifier raised: {exc}"
            record["status"] = "success" if passed else "failure"
            record["verification"] = verification

        record["finished"] = time.time()
        self._append(record)
        return record

    def run(self, tasks: Iterable[BenchTask], adapter: Adapter, seeds: Iterable[int] = (0,)) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for task in tasks:
            for seed in seeds:
                records.append(self.run_one(task, adapter, seed))
        return records

    def _append(self, record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        with self.log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            handle.write(json.dumps(record, sort_keys=True) + "\n")


def load_records(results_dir: str | Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    path = Path(results_dir) / "runs.jsonl"
    if not path.exists():
        return []
    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        if line.strip():
            records.append(json.loads(line))
    return records

=== bench/tasks.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import re
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable

Verifier = Callable[[Path], tuple[bool, str]]
Fixture = Callable[[Path], None]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BenchTask:
    id: str
    category: str
    goal: str
    files: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    verify: Verifier | None = None
    fixture: Fixture | None = None
    timeout_s: float = 120.0

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "category": self.category,
            "goal": self.goal,
            "files": sorted(self.files),
            "timeout_s": self.timeout_s,
            "has_fixture": self.fixture is not None,
        }


def _write(root: Path, files: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    for name, content in files.items():
        path = root / name
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")


def _read(root: Path, name: str) -> str | None:
    try:
        return (root / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    except OSError:
        return None


def _run(root: Path, command: list[str], timeout: float = 60.0) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(command, cwd=root, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout, check=False)
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
        return False, str(exc)
    return proc.returncode == 0, (proc.stdout + proc.stderr)[-800:]


def _load(root: Path, relative: str, module_name: str):
    path = root / relative
    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(relative)
    # Drop workspace-local modules cached from a previous run (seeds reuse names).
    for cached in list(sys.modules):
        top = cached.split(".")[0]
        if (root / top).exists() or (root / f"{top}.py").exists():
            sys.modules.pop(cached, None)
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(module_name, path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError(relative)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[module_name] = module
    inserted = str(root) not in sys.path
    if inserted:
        sys.path.insert(0, str(root))
    try:
        spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    finally:
        if inserted:
            sys.path.remove(str(root))
    return module


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- verifiers
def v_notes(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    text = _read(root, "notes.md")
    ok = bool(text and "# OMNI Notes" in text and "benchmark" in text)
    return ok, "notes.md exists with heading and benchmark line" if ok else "notes.md missing required heading/line"


def v_rename(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    ok = (root / "final.txt").exists() and not (root / "draft.txt").exists()
    content = _read(root, "final.txt") or ""
    ok = ok and content.strip() == "draft content"
    return ok, "draft.txt renamed to final.txt with content preserved" if ok else "rename incomplete"


def v_move(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    ok = (root / "archive" / "data.txt").exists() and not (root / "src" / "data.txt").exists()
    return ok, "src/data.txt moved to archive/data.txt" if ok else "move incomplete"


def v_delete_dupes(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    ok = (root / "keep.txt").exists() and not (root / "copy1.txt").exists() and not (root / "copy2.txt").exists()
    return ok, "duplicates removed, keep.txt retained" if ok else "duplicates remain"


def v_manifest(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    text = _read(root, "manifest.txt")
    expected = ["a.txt", "b.txt", "c.txt"]
    got = [line.strip() for line in (text or "").splitlines() if line.strip()]
    ok = got == expected
    return ok, f"manifest {got}" if ok else f"expected {expected}, got {got}"


def v_heading(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    text = _read(root, "README.md") or ""
    ok = text.lstrip().startswith("# Project")
    return ok, "README starts with # Project" if ok else "missing # Project heading"


def v_insert_section(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    text = _read(root, "README.md") or ""
    ok = text.find("## Usage") != -1 and text.find("## Usage") < text.find("## License")
    return ok, "## Usage inserted before ## License" if ok else "section order wrong"


def v_typo(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    text = (_read(root, "notes.txt") or "").lower()
    ok = "teh" not in text and "the" in text
    return ok, "typo fixed" if ok else "typo remains"


def v_bullets(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    text = _read(root, "todo.md") or ""
    lines = [line.strip() for line in text.splitlines()]
    bullets = [line for line in lines if line.startswith("- ")]
    ok = [item.lower() for item in bullets] == ["- write tests", "- fix bug", "- ship"]
    return ok, f"bullets {bullets}" if ok else f"unexpected bullets {bullets}"


def v_summary(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    text = (_read(root, "summary.md") or "").lower()
    bullets = [line for line in text.splitlines() if line.strip().startswith("- ")]
    ok = len(bullets) == 2 and "renewable" in text and "storage" in text
    return ok, "two-point summary present" if ok else "summary needs two bullets covering renewable/storage"


def _math(root: Path):
    return _load(root, "math_utils.py", f"bench_math_{abs(hash(str(root))) % 10**8}")


def v_add(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        module = _math(root)
        ok = module.add(2, 3) == 5 and module.add(-1, 1) == 0
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return False, f"import failed: {exc}"
    return ok, "add() works" if ok else "add() returned wrong value"


def v_square(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        module = _load(root, "calc.py", f"bench_calc_{abs(hash(str(root))) % 10**8}")
        ok = module.square(5) == 25 and module.square(3) == 9
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return False, f"import failed: {exc}"
    return ok, "square() fixed" if ok else "square() wrong"


def v_cli(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    ok, out = _run(root, [sys.executable, "greet.py", "--name", "Ada"])
    return ok and "Hello, Ada" in out, out.strip()[:200]


def v_config(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        module = _load(root, "config_io.py", f"bench_cfg_{abs(hash(str(root))) % 10**8}")
        value = module.load_config(root / "config.json")
        ok = value == {"debug": True, "port": 8000}
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return False, f"import failed: {exc}"
    return ok, "load_config returns parsed JSON" if ok else "load_config returned wrong value"


def v_refactor(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    text = (_read(root, "service.py") or "") + (_read(root, "app.py") or "")
    try:
        module = _load(root, "service.py", f"bench_svc_{abs(hash(str(root))) % 10**8}")
        ok = module.new_name() == "ok" and "old_name" not in text
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return False, f"import failed: {exc}"
    return ok, "old_name renamed and callers updated" if ok else "refactor incomplete"


def v_dataclass(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        module = _load(root, "geometry.py", f"bench_geo_{abs(hash(str(root))) % 10**8}")
        point = module.Point(3, 4)
        ok = point.x == 3 and point.y == 4 and abs(point.dist() - 5.0) < 1e-9
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return False, f"import failed: {exc}"
    return ok, "Point dataclass works" if ok else "Point API wrong"


def v_emails(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        module = _load(root, "extract.py", f"bench_ext_{abs(hash(str(root))) % 10**8}")
        got = module.extract_emails("a@b.com and x.y@z.org, not-this")
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return False, f"import failed: {exc}"
    ok = got == ["a@b.com", "x.y@z.org"]
    return ok, f"emails {got}" if ok else f"unexpected emails {got}"


def v_top_n(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        module = _load(root, "ranking.py", f"bench_rank_{abs(hash(str(root))) % 10**8}")
        got = module.top_n([1, 5, 3, 4], 2)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return False, f"import failed: {exc}"
    ok = got == [5, 4]
    return ok, f"top_n {got}" if ok else f"unexpected {got}"


def v_format_user(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        module = _load(root, "users.py", f"bench_users_{abs(hash(str(root))) % 10**8}")
        ok = module.format_user({}) == "anonymous" and module.format_user({"name": "Ada"}) == "Ada"
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return False, f"import failed: {exc}"
    return ok, "missing name defaults correctly" if ok else "format_user wrong"


def v_csv_summary(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        data = json.loads(_read(root, "summary.json") or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return False, "summary.json is not valid JSON"
    ok = data.get("total") == 60 and data.get("count") == 3 and data.get("max") == 30
    return ok, f"summary {data}" if ok else f"unexpected summary {data}"


def v_dedupe(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    text = _read(root, "unique.jsonl") or ""
    ids = []
    for line in text.splitlines():
        if line.strip():
            try:
                ids.append(json.loads(line)["id"])
            except (json.JSONDecodeError, KeyError):
                return False, "invalid JSONL"
    return ids == [1, 2], f"ids {ids}"


def v_sqlite(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        connection = sqlite3.connect(root / "db.sqlite")
        rows = connection.execute("SELECT name FROM items ORDER BY name").fetchall()
        connection.close()
    except sqlite3.Error as exc:
        return False, f"sqlite error: {exc}"
    names = [row[0] for row in rows]
    return names == ["alpha", "beta"], f"rows {names}"


def v_groupby(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        data = json.loads(_read(root, "by_region.json") or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return False, "by_region.json is not valid JSON"
    ok = data == {"east": 30, "west": 40}
    return ok, f"groups {data}" if ok else f"unexpected groups {data}"


def v_pytest(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    return _run(root, [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q"])


def v_shell(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    path = root / "run.sh"
    if not path.exists():
        return False, "run.sh missing"
    if not (path.stat().st_mode & 0o111):
        return False, "run.sh is not executable"
    ok, out = _run(root, ["sh", "run.sh"])
    return ok and out.strip() == "ok", out.strip()[:120]


def v_makefile(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    return _run(root, ["make", "test"])


def v_version(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    text = (_read(root, "version.py") or "") + (_read(root, "package/__init__.py") or "")
    try:
        module = _load(root, "package/__init__.py", f"bench_pkg_{abs(hash(str(root))) % 10**8}")
        ok = module.VERSION == "1.0" and 'VERSION = "1.0"' in text
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return False, f"import failed: {exc}"
    return ok, "VERSION exported from package" if ok else "VERSION not exported"


def v_consistency(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    config = _read(root, "config.py") or ""
    guide = _read(root, "docs/guide.md") or ""
    ok = "8000" in config and "8000" in guide and "9000" not in config and "9000" not in guide
    return ok, "both files use port 8000" if ok else "port mismatch remains"


def v_migration(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    schema = (_read(root, "schema.sql") or "").lower()
    query = (_read(root, "query.sql") or "").lower()
    ok = "accounts" in schema and "accounts" in query and not re.search(r"\busers\b", schema + query)
    return ok, "schema and query use accounts" if ok else "users table still referenced"


def v_slug(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        module = _load(root, "app.py", f"bench_app_{abs(hash(str(root))) % 10**8}")
        ok = module.slugify("Hello World!") == "hello-world" and (root / "text" / "slug.py").exists()
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return False, f"import failed: {exc}"
    return ok, "slugify moved to text/slug.py and app imports it" if ok else "slugify split incomplete"


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- fixtures
def f_notes(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "notes.md").write_text("# OMNI Notes\n\n- benchmark\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_rename(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "draft.txt").rename(root / "final.txt")


def f_move(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "archive").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (root / "src" / "data.txt").rename(root / "archive" / "data.txt")


def f_delete_dupes(root: Path) -> None:
    for name in ("copy1.txt", "copy2.txt"):
        (root / name).unlink()


def f_manifest(root: Path) -> None:
    names = sorted(path.name for path in (root / "data").iterdir())
    (root / "manifest.txt").write_text("\n".join(names) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_heading(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "README.md").write_text("# Project\n\nA sample project.\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_insert_section(root: Path) -> None:
    text = _read(root, "README.md") or ""
    (root / "README.md").write_text(text.replace("## License", "## Usage\n\nRun it.\n\n## License"), encoding="utf-8")


def f_typo(root: Path) -> None:
    text = _read(root, "notes.txt") or ""
    (root / "notes.txt").write_text(text.replace("teh", "the"), encoding="utf-8")


def f_bullets(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "todo.md").write_text("# Todo\n\n- write tests\n- fix bug\n- ship\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_summary(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "summary.md").write_text("- Renewable generation grew.\n- Storage capacity expanded.\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_add(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "math_utils.py").write_text("def add(a, b):\n    return a + b\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_square(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "calc.py").write_text("def square(x):\n    return x * x\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_cli(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "greet.py").write_text(
        "import argparse\n\nparser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\nparser.add_argument('--name', default='World')\nargs = parser.parse_args()\nprint(f'Hello, {args.name}')\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )


def f_config(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "config_io.py").write_text("import json\n\ndef load_config(path):\n    return json.loads(open(path, encoding='utf-8').read())\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_refactor(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "service.py").write_text("def new_name():\n    return 'ok'\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (root / "app.py").write_text("from service import new_name\n\nprint(new_name())\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_dataclass(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "geometry.py").write_text(
        "from dataclasses import dataclass\nimport math\n\n@dataclass\nclass Point:\n    x: float\n    y: float\n\n    def dist(self):\n        return math.hypot(self.x, self.y)\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )


def f_emails(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "extract.py").write_text(
        "import re\n\ndef extract_emails(text):\n    return re.findall(r'[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\\.[A-Za-z]{2,}', text)\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )


def f_top_n(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "ranking.py").write_text("def top_n(items, n):\n    return sorted(items, reverse=True)[:n]\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_format_user(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "users.py").write_text("def format_user(user):\n    return user.get('name', 'anonymous')\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_csv_summary(root: Path) -> None:
    import csv

    rows = list(csv.DictReader((root / "sales.csv").open(encoding="utf-8")))
    amounts = [int(row["amount"]) for row in rows]
    (root / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps({"total": sum(amounts), "count": len(amounts), "max": max(amounts)}), encoding="utf-8")


def f_dedupe(root: Path) -> None:
    seen = set()
    output = []
    for line in (_read(root, "events.jsonl") or "").splitlines():
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        item = json.loads(line)
        if item["id"] not in seen:
            seen.add(item["id"])
            output.append(json.dumps(item))
    (root / "unique.jsonl").write_text("\n".join(output) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_sqlite(root: Path) -> None:
    connection = sqlite3.connect(root / "db.sqlite")
    connection.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS items (name TEXT)")
    connection.executemany("INSERT INTO items (name) VALUES (?)", [("alpha",), ("beta",)])
    connection.commit()
    connection.close()


def f_groupby(root: Path) -> None:
    import csv

    totals: dict[str, int] = {}
    for row in csv.DictReader((root / "orders.csv").open(encoding="utf-8")):
        totals[row["region"]] = totals.get(row["region"], 0) + int(row["amount"])
    (root / "by_region.json").write_text(json.dumps(totals), encoding="utf-8")


def f_pytest(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "calc.py").write_text("def add(a, b):\n    return a + b\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_shell(root: Path) -> None:
    path = root / "run.sh"
    path.write_text("#!/bin/sh\necho ok\n", encoding="utf-8")
    path.chmod(0o755)


def f_makefile(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "Makefile").write_text("test:\n\tpython3 -m pytest -q\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_version(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "version.py").write_text('VERSION = "1.0"\n', encoding="utf-8")
    (root / "package" / "__init__.py").write_text("from version import VERSION\n\n__all__ = ['VERSION']\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_consistency(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "config.py").write_text('PORT = 8000\n', encoding="utf-8")
    (root / "docs" / "guide.md").write_text("# Guide\n\nThe default port is 8000.\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_migration(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "schema.sql").write_text("CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER, name TEXT);\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (root / "query.sql").write_text("SELECT * FROM accounts;\n", encoding="utf-8")


def f_slug(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "text").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (root / "text" / "__init__.py").write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    (root / "text" / "slug.py").write_text(
        "import re\n\ndef slugify(value):\n    return re.sub(r'[^a-z0-9]+', '-', value.lower()).strip('-')\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    (root / "app.py").write_text("from text.slug import slugify\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    print(slugify('Hello World!'))\n", encoding="utf-8")

    BenchTask("data-jsonl-dedupe", "data", "Deduplicate events.jsonl by id, keeping the first occurrence, into unique.jsonl.", {"events.jsonl": '{"id": 1, "v": "a"}\n{"id": 2, "v": "b"}\n{"id": 1, "v": "c"}\n'}, v_dedupe, f_dedupe, 60),

    BenchTask("term-shell-script", "terminal", "Create an executable run.sh that prints 'ok'.", {}, v_shell, f_shell, 60),
    BenchTask("term-makefile", "terminal", "Add a Makefile target named test that runs the Python test suite.", {"test_ok.py": "def test_ok():\n    assert True\n"}, v_makefile, f_makefile, 90),

    BenchTask("plan-consistency", "planning", "Make docs/guide.md and config.py agree on the default port 8000.", {"config.py": "PORT = 9000\n", "docs/guide.md": "# Guide\n\nThe default port is 9000.\n"}, v_consistency, f_consistency, 60),
    BenchTask("plan-migration", "planning", "Rename the users table to accounts in schema.sql and query.sql.", {"schema.sql": "CREATE TABLE users (id INTEGER, name TEXT);\n", "query.sql": "SELECT * FROM users;\n"}, v_migration, f_migration, 60),
    BenchTask("plan-cross-module", "planning", "Move slugify from utils.py into text/slug.py and update app.py to import it.", {"utils.py": "import re\n\ndef slugify(value):\n    return re.sub(r'[^a-z0-9]+', '-', value.lower()).strip('-')\n", "app.py": "from utils import slugify\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    print(slugify('Hello World!'))\n"}, v_slug, f_slug, 60),
)

TASKS_BY_ID: dict[str, BenchTask] = {task.id: task for task in TASKS}
assert len(TASKS) == 30, f"expected 30 tasks, found {len(TASKS)}"


def select_tasks(spec: str | None) -> list[BenchTask]:
    if not spec or spec == "all":
        return list(TASKS)
    ids = [item.strip() for item in spec.split(",") if item.strip()]
    missing = [item for item in ids if item not in TASKS_BY_ID]
    if missing:
        raise KeyError(f"unknown task ids: {missing}")
    return [TASKS_BY_ID[item] for item in ids]


def materialise(task: BenchTask, root: Path) -> None:
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    _write(root, task.files)

=== dashboard/app.js ===
const state = { taskId: null, plan: [] };
const eventStream = document.getElementById('event-stream');
const planList = document.getElementById('plan-list');
const taskStatus = document.getElementById('task-status');
const report = document.getElementById('task-report');
const modePill = document.getElementById('mode-pill');

function addEvent(text) {
  const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
  eventStream.textContent = `${timestamp}: ${text}
` + eventStream.textContent;
}

async function submitTask() {
  const goal = document.getElementById('goal-input').value;
  addEvent('Submitting task');
  const response = await fetch('/tasks', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ goal })
  });
  const payload = await response.json();
  state.taskId = payload.task_id;
  state.plan = payload.plan;
  taskStatus.textContent = payload.status;
  renderPlan();
  addEvent(`Task ${state.taskId} created`);
  modePill.textContent = 'demo';
}

function renderPlan() {
  planList.innerHTML = '';
  for (const step of state.plan) {
    const li = document.createElement('li');
    li.textContent = `${step.id}: ${step.name} (${step.status || 'PENDING'})`;
    planList.appendChild(li);
  }
}

document.getElementById('submit-btn').addEventListener('click', submitTask);

=== docs/architecture.md ===
# OMNI architecture

The project is organized as a compact orchestrator with these responsibilities:

- planner: turns a user goal into dependency-aware task steps
- safety: validates workspace and action requests
- adapters: exposes desktop metadata and safe launch access
- verification: independently checks postconditions
- orchestrator: runs ready steps and manages retries
- api: exposes local HTTP and WebSocket interfaces
- dashboard: renders live status information

=== docs/demo-guide.md ===
# Demo guide

Run the demo with:

```bash
python3 -m omni.main run-demo --workspace ./workspace/demo
```

=== docs/research.md ===
# Research roadmap

OMNI is positioned as a userspace desktop orchestration prototype. It is suited for research on planning, verification, safety, and local execution loops, but it is not a kernel-level operating system.

=== docs/security.md ===
# Security model

- Filesystem actions are restricted to a configured workspace root.
- Disallowed actions are rejected at validation time.
- Web requests are bound to localhost by default.
- The dashboard is a local interface only.
- No arbitrary shell construction is permitted.

=== hud/shell.qml ===
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io

ShellRoot {
    id: root

    property var state: ({})
    property color accent: "#7aa2f7"
    property color muted: "#8b93a7"
    property color ink: "#d7dcec"
    property color warn: "#e0af68"

    function text(value, fallback) {
        if (value === undefined || value === null || value === "") return fallback;
        return String(value);
    }
    function pct(value) {
        if (value === undefined || value === null) return "--";
        return Math.round(Number(value) * 100) + "%";
    }
    function routingField(key, fallback) {
        if (!root.state || !root.state.routing) return fallback;
        return root.text(root.state.routing[key], fallback);
    }

    Process {
        id: reader
        command: ["sh", "-c", "cat \"${OMNI_HUD_STATE:-$HOME/.cache/omni/hud/state.json}\" 2>/dev/null"]
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: {
                try {

RR R lltRtR# )ÚModelGatewayaÆ  Single entry point for every hosted-model call.

Roles are routed independently to the best available model from the live
registry (capability, cost, measured latency). On error / rate limit /
timeout the call falls back to the next best model for the same role.
API keys come from env vars or the system keyring and are never logged.
All non-mock calls are logged to JSONL (text is not stored unless
OMNI_DEBUG_FRAMES-style opt-in; prompts are hashed).

=== omni/adapters/desktop.py ===
from __future__ import annotations
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any


class DesktopAdapter:
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str | None = None):
        self.workspace_root = Path(workspace_root) if workspace_root else Path.cwd()

    def inspect_desktop(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        result: dict[str, Any] = {"desktop": "unknown", "hyprland": False}
        hyprctl = shutil.which("hyprctl")
        if hyprctl:
            try:
                monitors = subprocess.run([hyprctl, "monitors", "-j"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
                if monitors.returncode == 0:
                    result["hyprland"] = True
                    result["desktop"] = "Hyprland"
                    try:
                        result["monitors"] = json.loads(monitors.stdout or "[]")
                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
                        result["monitors"] = []
            except Exception:
                result["hyprland"] = False
        else:
            result["desktop"] = "unsupported"
        return result

    def launch_application(self, app_name: str, args: list[str] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if shutil.which(app_name) is None:
            return {"success": False, "error": f"Application not installed: {app_name}"}
        cmd = [app_name] + (args or [])
        try:
            proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)
            return {"success": True, "pid": proc.pid, "command": cmd}
        except Exception as exc:
            return {"success": False, "error": str(exc)}

=== omni/api/app.py ===
from __future__ import annotations
import json
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from omni.config import settings
from omni.orchestrator.engine import Orchestrator
from omni.planner.demo import DemoPlanner
from omni.storage.sqlite_store import SQLiteStore


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    app.state.store = SQLiteStore(Path("./omni_data.db"))
    app.state.orchestrator = Orchestrator(workspace_root=str(settings.workspace_root), store=app.state.store)
    yield


app = FastAPI(title="OMNI API", lifespan=lifespan)
app.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=["http://127.0.0.1:8000", "http://localhost:8000"], allow_methods=["*"], allow_headers=["*"])

static_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "dashboard"
app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=str(static_dir)), name="static")

@app.get("/health")
def health() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"status": "ok", "mode": settings.mode, "workspace": str(settings.workspace_root)}


@app.get("/")
def root() -> Any:
    return {
        "message": "OMNI API",
        "dashboard": "/static/index.html",
        "docs": "/docs",
    }


@app.post("/tasks")
def create_task(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    goal = str(payload.get("goal", "")).strip()
    if not goal:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Goal is required")
    task = app.state.orchestrator.create_task(goal)
    return {"task_id": task.task_id, "status": task.status, "plan": [step.model_dump() for step in task.plan.steps]}


@app.get("/tasks")
def list_tasks() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return app.state.store.list_tasks()


@app.get("/tasks/{task_id}")
def get_task(task_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    task = app.state.orchestrator.get_task(task_id)
    if task is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Task not found")
    return {"task_id": task.task_id, "status": task.status, "plan": [step.model_dump() for step in task.plan.steps], "final_report": task.final_report}


@app.post("/tasks/{task_id}/approve")
def approve_task(task_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    task = app.state.orchestrator.get_task(task_id)
    if task is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Task not found")
    task.approved = True
    task.status = "READY"
    return {"task_id": task_id, "status": "approved"}


@app.post("/tasks/{task_id}/cancel")
def cancel_task(task_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    task = app.state.orchestrator.get_task(task_id)
    if task is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Task not found")
    task.status = "CANCELLED"
    return {"task_id": task_id, "status": "CANCELLED"}


@app.websocket("/ws")
async def websocket_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket) -> None:
    await websocket.accept()
    try:
        while True:
            await websocket.receive_text()
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        pass

=== omni/config.py ===
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
import os


@dataclass
class Settings:
    workspace_root: Path = field(default_factory=lambda: Path(os.environ.get("OMNI_WORKSPACE", "./workspace")).resolve())
    mode: str = os.environ.get("OMNI_MODE", "demo")
    host: str = os.environ.get("OMNI_HOST", "127.0.0.1")
    port: int = int(os.environ.get("OMNI_PORT", "8000"))
    demo_project_name: str = "omni_demo_project"
    model_timeout: float = 15.0
    action_timeout: float = 30.0
    max_retries: int = 2

    def ensure_workspace(self) -> Path:
        self.workspace_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return self.workspace_root


settings = Settings()
