=== omni/context/atspi.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import subprocess
import time
from typing import Any, Callable

Runner = Callable[[list[str], float], subprocess.CompletedProcess]

_REF = re.compile(r"\(\s*'([^']+)'\s*,\s*(?:objectpath\s*)?'([^']+)'\s*\)")
_ROLE = re.compile(r"^\(\s*'(.*)'\s*,?\s*\)\s*$")


class AtspiCollector:
    """Best-effort AT-SPI role histogram.

    Prefers pyatspi when installed; otherwise speaks to the AT-SPI bus with
    gdbus. The gdbus walk is bounded (max_nodes, deadline) and only records
    element role counts, never text content. When no accessibility bus is
    reachable it reports available=False and AMPL/IARS fall back to window
    statistics.
    """

    def __init__(self, runner: Runner | None = None, max_nodes: int = 18, deadline_s: float = 1.5, cache_s: float = 8.0) -> None:
        self.runner = runner
        self.max_nodes = max_nodes
        self.deadline_s = deadline_s
        self.cache_s = cache_s
        self._cache: tuple[float, dict[str, Any]] | None = None

    def collect(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if self._cache and now - self._cache[0] < self.cache_s:
            return dict(self._cache[1])
        result = self._collect()
        self._cache = (now, result)
        return dict(result)

    def _collect(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            import pyatspi  # type: ignore

            desktop = pyatspi.Registry.getDesktop(0)
            roles: dict[str, int] = {}
            budget = [self.max_nodes]

            def walk(node: Any, depth: int) -> None:
                if budget[0] <= 0 or depth < 0:
                    return
                budget[0] -= 1
                try:
                    role = str(node.getRoleName())
                except Exception:
                    role = "unknown"
                roles[role] = roles.get(role, 0) + 1
                try:
                    for child in node:
                        walk(child, depth - 1)
                except Exception:
                    return

            walk(desktop, 2)
            return {"available": True, "roles": roles, "source": "pyatspi"}
        except Exception:
            pass
        return self._collect_gdbus()

    def _run(self, args: list[str], timeout: float = 0.4) -> str | None:
        runner = self.runner
        if runner is None:
            def runner(cmd: list[str], t: float) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
                return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=t, check=False)
        try:
            proc = runner(args, timeout)
        except (subprocess.TimeoutExpired, OSError):
            return None
        if proc.returncode != 0:
            return None
        return proc.stdout or ""

    def _gdbus(self, address: str, dest: str, path: str, method: str, timeout: float = 0.4) -> str | None:
        return self._run(["gdbus", "call", "--address", address, "--dest", dest, "--object-path", path, "--method", method], timeout)

    def _collect_gdbus(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        started = time.monotonic()
        out = self._run(["gdbus", "call", "--session", "--dest", "org.a11y.Bus", "--object-path", "/org/a11y/bus", "--method", "org.a11y.Bus.GetAddress"])
        if not out:
            return {"available": False, "roles": {}, "source": "none"}
        match = re.search(r"'([^']+)'", out)
        if not match:
            return {"available": False, "roles": {}, "source": "none"}
        address = match.group(1)
        children = self._gdbus(address, "org.a11y.atspi.Registry", "/org/a11y/atspi/accessible/root", "org.a11y.atspi.Accessible.GetChildren")
        if not children:
            return {"available": False, "roles": {}, "source": "none"}
        apps = _REF.findall(children)
        roles: dict[str, int] = {}
        calls = 0
        for bus, path in apps:
            if time.monotonic() - started > self.deadline_s or calls >= self.max_nodes:
                break
            app_children = self._gdbus(address, bus, path, "org.a11y.atspi.Accessible.GetChildren")
            calls += 1
            if app_children:
                for role_raw in self._roles(address, bus, path, calls, started):
                    roles[role_raw] = roles.get(role_raw, 0) + 1
                    calls += 1
                for child_bus, child_path in _REF.findall(app_children):
                    if calls >= self.max_nodes or time.monotonic() - started > self.deadline_s:
                        break
                    role = self._role(address, child_bus, child_path)
                    calls += 1
                    if role:
                        roles[role] = roles.get(role, 0) + 1
        return {"available": True, "roles": roles, "source": "gdbus"}

    def _roles(self, address: str, bus: str, path: str, calls: int, started: float) -> list[str]:
        if calls >= self.max_nodes or time.monotonic() - started > self.deadline_s:
            return []
        role = self._role(address, bus, path)
        return [role] if role else []

    def _role(self, address: str, bus: str, path: str) -> str | None:
        out = self._gdbus(address, bus, path, "org.a11y.atspi.Accessible.GetRoleName")
        if not out:
            return None
        match = _ROLE.match(out.strip())
        return match.group(1).strip() if match else None

=== omni/context/features.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import math
import re
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import numpy as np

from omni.context.lexicon import LEXICON_INDEX

_APP_BUCKETS = 32
_TEXT_BUCKETS = len(LEXICON_INDEX)
_ROLE_BUCKETS = 16
_ACTION_BUCKETS = 8
FEATURE_DIM = 7 + _APP_BUCKETS + _TEXT_BUCKETS + _ROLE_BUCKETS + _ACTION_BUCKETS + 3
TEXT_START = 7 + _APP_BUCKETS
_WORD = re.compile(r"[a-z0-9]+")


def _bucket(text: str, buckets: int, salt: str = "") -> tuple[int, float]:
    digest = hashlib.blake2b((salt + str(text)).encode("utf-8"), digest_size=8).digest()
    return int.from_bytes(digest[:4], "little") % buckets, (1.0 if digest[4] & 1 else -1.0)


def text_embedding(text: str) -> np.ndarray:
    """Collision-free lexical embedding over the curated capability vocabulary."""
    vec = np.zeros(_TEXT_BUCKETS, dtype=np.float64)
    for token in _WORD.findall((text or "").lower()):
        idx = LEXICON_INDEX.get(token)
        if idx is not None:
            vec[idx] += 1.0
    norm = np.linalg.norm(vec)
    if norm:
        vec /= norm
    return vec


def prior_vector(text: str, dim: int = FEATURE_DIM) -> np.ndarray:
    """Embed an agent capability description into the shared context-text segment."""
    phi = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
    phi[TEXT_START : TEXT_START + _TEXT_BUCKETS] = text_embedding(text)
    return phi


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ContextSnapshot:
    timestamp: float
    app_class: str = ""
    title: str = ""
    workspace_id: int = 0
    workspace_name: str = ""
    client_count: int = 0
    monitor_count: int = 0
    focused_age_s: float = 0.0
    clipboard_types: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    atspi_roles: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    atspi_available: bool = False
    screenshot: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    recent_events: tuple[str, ...] = ()

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
            "app_class": self.app_class,
            "title": self.title,
            "workspace_id": self.workspace_id,
            "workspace_name": self.workspace_name,
            "client_count": self.client_count,
            "monitor_count": self.monitor_count,
            "focused_age_s": round(self.focused_age_s, 3),
            "clipboard_types": list(self.clipboard_types),
            "atspi_available": self.atspi_available,
            "atspi_role_count": len(self.atspi_roles),
            "screenshot_available": bool(self.screenshot),
        }

    def privacy_summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Aggregates only: never clipboard contents, pixels, or keystrokes."""
        return {
            "raw_keys_stored": False,
            "clipboard_content_stored": False,
            "screenshot_stored": False,
            "app_class": self.app_class,
            "clipboard_type_count": len(self.clipboard_types),
            "event_count": len(self.recent_events),
        }


def build_features(snapshot: ContextSnapshot, action_counts: dict[str, int] | None = None, now: float | None = None) -> np.ndarray:
    """Map a context snapshot to the fixed IARS feature vector phi(z)."""
    phi = np.zeros(FEATURE_DIM, dtype=np.float64)
    phi[0] = 1.0
    phi[1] = math.log1p(max(snapshot.focused_age_s, 0.0))
    phi[2] = math.log1p(max(snapshot.client_count, 0))
    phi[3] = float(snapshot.workspace_id % 10) / 10.0
    types = {t.lower() for t in snapshot.clipboard_types}
    phi[4] = 1.0 if any("text" in t or "utf8" in t or "string" in t for t in types) else 0.0
    phi[5] = 1.0 if any("image" in t or "png" in t for t in types) else 0.0
    phi[6] = 1.0 if any("file" in t or "uri" in t for t in types) else 0.0

    base = 7
    idx, _ = _bucket(snapshot.app_class or "unknown", _APP_BUCKETS, "app:")
    phi[base + idx] = 1.0
    base += _APP_BUCKETS

    # Only app class + title: ubiquitous AT-SPI role names ("application",
    # "frame") would otherwise leak into every context and skew agent priors.
    text = " ".join([snapshot.app_class or "", snapshot.title or ""])
    phi[base : base + _TEXT_BUCKETS] = text_embedding(text)
    base += _TEXT_BUCKETS

    role_vec = np.zeros(_ROLE_BUCKETS, dtype=np.float64)
    for role, count in snapshot.atspi_roles.items():
        idx, sign = _bucket(role, _ROLE_BUCKETS, "role:")
        role_vec[idx] += sign * float(count)
    norm = np.linalg.norm(role_vec)
    if norm:
        role_vec /= norm
    phi[base : base + _ROLE_BUCKETS] = role_vec
    base += _ROLE_BUCKETS

    for action, count in (action_counts or {}).items():
        idx, sign = _bucket(action, _ACTION_BUCKETS, "action:")
        phi[base + idx] += sign * math.log1p(max(count, 0))
    base += _ACTION_BUCKETS

    phi[base] = float(snapshot.screenshot.get("brightness", 0.0))
    phi[base + 1] = float(snapshot.screenshot.get("edge_density", 0.0))
    phi[base + 2] = 1.0 if snapshot.screenshot else 0.0
    assert base + 3 == FEATURE_DIM
    return phi


def synthetic_snapshot(app_class: str = "code", title: str = "", **kwargs: Any) -> ContextSnapshot:
    return ContextSnapshot(timestamp=kwargs.pop("timestamp", time.time()), app_class=app_class, title=title, **kwargs)

=== omni/context/hyprland.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable

import numpy as np

from omni.context.atspi import AtspiCollector
from omni.context.features import ContextSnapshot

Runner = Callable[[list[str], float], subprocess.CompletedProcess]


def _default_runner(cmd: list[str], timeout: float) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout, check=False)


class HyprlandEventStream:
    """Minimal socket2 reader. Keeps aggregates and focus timing, never keystrokes."""

    FOCUS_EVENTS = {"activewindow", "activewindowv2", "focuswindow", "workspace", "workspacev2"}

    def __init__(self, instance_signature: str | None = None, runtime_dir: str | None = None, on_event: Callable[[str, str], None] | None = None) -> None:
        self.instance = instance_signature or os.environ.get("HYPRLAND_INSTANCE_SIGNATURE", "")
        runtime = Path(runtime_dir or os.environ.get("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR", f"/run/user/{os.getuid()}"))
        self.socket_path = runtime / "hypr" / self.instance / ".socket2.sock"
        self.on_event = on_event
        self.started_at = time.monotonic()
        self.last_focus_monotonic = self.started_at
        self.last_event: dict[str, str] | None = None
        self.event_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        self._thread = None
        self._stop = False

    @property
    def focused_age_s(self) -> float:
        return max(0.0, time.monotonic() - self.last_focus_monotonic)

    @staticmethod
    def parse(line: str) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
        if ">>" not in line:
            return None
        event, payload = line.split(">>", 1)
        if not event:
            return None
        return event, payload

    def handle_line(self, line: str) -> None:
        parsed = self.parse(line)
        if parsed is None:
            return
        event, payload = parsed
        self.last_event = {"event": event, "payload": payload}
        self.event_counts[event] = self.event_counts.get(event, 0) + 1
        if event in self.FOCUS_EVENTS:
            self.last_focus_monotonic = time.monotonic()
        if self.on_event:
            self.on_event(event, payload)

    def start(self) -> bool:
        if not self.socket_path.exists() or self._thread is not None:
            return False
        import socket
        import threading

        sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
        try:
            sock.connect(str(self.socket_path))
            sock.settimeout(0.5)
        except OSError:
            sock.close()
            return False

        def loop() -> None:
            buffer = ""
            while not self._stop:
                try:
                    chunk = sock.recv(4096)
                    if not chunk:
                        break
                    buffer += chunk.decode("utf-8", "replace")
                    while "\n" in buffer:
                        line, buffer = buffer.split("\n", 1)
                        self.handle_line(line)
                except TimeoutError:
                    continue
                except OSError:
                    break
            try:
                sock.close()
            except OSError:
                pass

        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=loop, daemon=True, name="hypr-socket2")
        self._thread.start()
        return True

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self._stop = True


def capture_screen_stats(stats_only: bool = True) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Low-res grim frame -> a few local aggregate stats. Never saved unless OMNI_DEBUG_FRAMES=1."""
    if os.environ.get("OMNI_SCREENSHOT", "0") not in {"1", "true", "yes"}:
        return {}
    if not shutil.which("grim"):
        return {}
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(["grim", "-s", "0.08", "-t", "ppm", "-"], capture_output=True, timeout=3, check=False)
    except (subprocess.TimeoutExpired, OSError):
        return {}
    if proc.returncode != 0 or not proc.stdout:
        return {}
    data = proc.stdout
    match = re.match(rb"P6\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s", data)
    if not match:
        return {}
    width, height, maxval = (int(x) for x in match.groups())
    offset = match.end()
    expected = width * height * 3
    if maxval != 255 or len(data) < offset + expected:
        return {}
    pixels = np.frombuffer(data[offset : offset + expected], dtype=np.uint8).reshape(height, width, 3).astype(np.float32) / 255.0
    if os.environ.get("OMNI_DEBUG_FRAMES", "0") in {"1", "true", "yes"}:
        debug = Path(os.environ.get("OMNI_DEBUG_FRAME_DIR", Path.cwd() / "workspace" / "debug_frames"))
        debug.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (debug / f"frame_{int(time.time() * 1000)}.ppm").write_bytes(data)
    gray = pixels.mean(axis=2)
    return {
        "width": float(width),
        "height": float(height),
        "brightness": float(gray.mean()),
        "edge_density": float((np.abs(np.diff(gray, axis=0)).mean() + np.abs(np.diff(gray, axis=1)).mean()) / 2.0),
    }


class HyprlandContextCollector:
    """Hyprland IPC context collector with privacy-preserving defaults."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        runner: Runner | None = None,
        event_stream: HyprlandEventStream | None = None,
        atspi: AtspiCollector | None = None,
        screenshot: bool | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.runner = runner or _default_runner
        self.events = event_stream or HyprlandEventStream()
        self.atspi = atspi if atspi is not None else AtspiCollector(runner=self.runner)
        self.screenshot = screenshot if screenshot is not None else os.environ.get("OMNI_SCREENSHOT", "0") in {"1", "true", "yes"}
        self._started = False

    def start(self) -> bool:
        if self._started:
            return True
        self._started = self.events.start()
        return self._started

    def close(self) -> None:
        self.events.stop()

    def _json(self, *args: str) -> Any:
        try:
            proc = self.runner(["hyprctl", *args, "-j"], 3.0)
        except (subprocess.TimeoutExpired, OSError):
            return None
        if proc.returncode != 0:
            return None
        try:
            return json.loads(proc.stdout or "null")
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            return None

    def _clipboard_types(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        if not shutil.which("wl-paste"):
            return ()
        try:
            proc = self.runner(["wl-paste", "--list-types"], 2.0)
        except (subprocess.TimeoutExpired, OSError):
            return ()
        if proc.returncode != 0:
            return ()
        return tuple(line.strip() for line in (proc.stdout or "").splitlines() if line.strip())[:16]

    def snapshot(self) -> ContextSnapshot:
        active = self._json("activewindow") or {}
        workspace = self._json("activeworkspace") or {}
        clients = self._json("clients") or []
        monitors = self._json("monitors") or []
        atspi = self.atspi.collect() if self.atspi else {"available": False, "roles": {}}
        screen = capture_screen_stats() if self.screenshot else {}
        event = self.events.last_event or {}
        return ContextSnapshot(
            timestamp=time.time(),
            app_class=str(active.get("class") or active.get("initialClass") or "unknown"),
            title=str(active.get("title") or ""),
            workspace_id=int(workspace.get("id") or active.get("workspace", {}).get("id") or 0),
            workspace_name=str(workspace.get("name") or ""),
            client_count=len(clients) if isinstance(clients, list) else 0,
            monitor_count=len(monitors) if isinstance(monitors, list) else 0,
            focused_age_s=self.events.focused_age_s,
            clipboard_types=self._clipboard_types(),
            atspi_roles=dict(atspi.get("roles", {})),
            atspi_available=bool(atspi.get("available", False)),
            screenshot=screen,
            recent_events=(event.get("event", ""),) if event else (),
        )

=== omni/context/lexicon.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

# Curated lexical vocabulary for the deterministic local embedding. This is not
# a neural embedding: it gives exact, collision-free token alignment between app
# context and agent capability descriptions. Replace with LLMClient.embed when a
# real embedding model is configured.
LEXICON: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "file", "files", "filesystem", "paths", "workspace", "nautilus", "thunar", "dolphin", "nemo", "pcmanfm",
    "create", "rename", "copy", "move", "delete", "list", "inspect", "archive", "permissions", "cleanup",
    "document", "documents", "editor", "text", "markdown", "writing", "writer", "gedit", "obsidian", "notion",
    "readme", "insert", "replace", "paragraph", "structure", "libreoffice", "pdf",
    "terminal", "shell", "bash", "zsh", "alacritty", "kitty", "foot", "konsole", "wezterm",
    "code", "vscode", "codium", "build", "test", "pytest", "git", "python", "py", "command", "process",
    "debug", "compile", "repository", "routing", "rust", "typescript", "commit", "diff",
    "browser", "chrome", "chromium", "firefox", "brave", "edge", "web", "url", "tabs", "forms", "form",
    "navigation", "navigate", "page", "download", "tab", "inbox", "mail", "youtube", "video", "whatsapp",
    "screen", "screenshot", "visual", "image", "ui", "geometry", "mpv", "vlc", "imv", "eog", "gimp",
    "click", "scroll", "layout", "frame", "window", "application", "button", "entry", "field", "menu",
    "link", "table", "grid", "canvas", "item", "scrollbar", "toggle",
    "plan", "workflow", "cross", "cross-app", "reasoning", "handoff", "multi", "goal", "steps", "tools",
    "slack", "discord", "spotify", "spreadsheet", "calc", "excel", "gnumeric", "invoice",
)

LEXICON_INDEX: dict[str, int] = {token: index for index, token in enumerate(LEXICON)}

=== omni/gateway.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import requests

from omni.model_registry import ModelRegistry, get_api_key, provider_headers
from omni.telemetry import JsonlLogger, mock_enabled

ROLES = ("planner", "fast_chat", "vision", "speech_to_text", "text_to_speech", "embeddings", "code")
DEFAULT_CAPABILITY = {
    "planner": {"modality": "text", "tools": True, "prefer_open_weights": False},
    "fast_chat": {"modality": "text", "tools": False, "prefer_open_weights": False},
    "vision": {"modality": "vision", "tools": False, "prefer_open_weights": False},
    "speech_to_text": {"modality": "audio", "tools": False, "prefer_open_weights": False},
    "text_to_speech": {"modality": "audio", "tools": False, "prefer_open_weights": False},
    "embeddings": {"modality": "text", "tools": False, "prefer_open_weights": False},
    "code": {"modality": "text", "tools": False, "prefer_open_weights": False},
}

# Base URLs for OpenAI-compatible chat/completions per provider (used when the
# provider exposes a chat endpoint; the model registry list endpoint is separate).
CHAT_BASE_URLS = {
    "openrouter": "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1",
    "together": "https://api.together.xyz/v1",
    "groq": "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1",
    "fireworks": "https://api.fireworks.ai/inference/v1",
    "cerebras": "https://api.cerebras.ai/v1",
    "huggingface": "https://router.huggingface.co/v1",
}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GatewayResult:
    text: str
    model: str
    provider: str
    role: str
    cached: bool
    elapsed_s: float
    mock: bool
    fallback: bool = False
    attempts: int = 1
    extra: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def metadata(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "model": self.model,
            "provider": self.provider,
            "role": self.role,
            "cached": self.cached,
            "elapsed_s": round(self.elapsed_s, 4),
            "mock": self.mock,
            "fallback": self.fallback,
            "attempts": self.attempts,
            **self.extra,
        }


class ModelGateway:
    """Single entry point for every hosted-model call.

    Roles are routed independently to the best available model from the live
    registry (capability, cost, measured latency). On error / rate limit /
    timeout the call falls back to the next best model for the same role.
    API keys come from env vars or the system keyring and are never logged.
    All non-mock calls are logged to JSONL (text is not stored unless
    OMNI_DEBUG_FRAMES-style opt-in; prompts are hashed).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        registry: ModelRegistry | None = None,
        logger: JsonlLogger | None = None,
        timeout: float = 60.0,
        max_retries: int = 2,
        dry_run: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        self.registry = registry or ModelRegistry()
        self.timeout = timeout
        self.max_retries = max_retries
        self.dry_run = dry_run
        self.mock = mock_enabled() or os.environ.get("OMNI_LLM_MOCK", "").lower() in {"1", "true", "yes"}
        cache_root = os.environ.get("OMNI_CACHE_DIR") or os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), ".cache", "omni")
        self.logger = logger or JsonlLogger(os.path.join(cache_root, "gateway.jsonl"), mock=self.mock)
        self._session = requests.Session()
        self._session.headers.update({"User-Agent": "omni-model-gateway/1.0"})

    # ----- role resolution -----
    def role_model(self, role: str) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any] | None]:
        """Return (model_id, capability hint) for a role, or (None, None)."""
        hint = DEFAULT_CAPABILITY.get(role, {"modality": "text", "tools": False, "prefer_open_weights": False})
        forced = os.environ.get(f"OMNI_MODEL_{role.upper()}")
        if forced:
            return forced, hint
        self.registry.refresh()
        rec = self.registry.select(role, **hint)
        if rec is None:
            # last resort: any text model
            rec = self.registry.select("fast_chat", modality="text", tools=False)
        return (rec.model_id if rec else None), hint

    def provider_for(self, model_id: str) -> str:
        return model_id.split("/", 1)[0] if "/" in model_id else "openai-compatible"

    # ----- http -----
    def _post_chat(self, provider: str, model_id: str, messages: list[dict[str, str]], params: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        base = CHAT_BASE_URLS.get(provider)
        if not base:
            base = os.environ.get("OMNI_LLM_BASE_URL", "https://api.openai.com/v1").rstrip("/")
        key = get_api_key(provider)
        headers = provider_headers(provider, key)
        # short model id for the body (strip provider prefix when the API expects it)
        short = model_id.split("/", 1)[-1] if "/" in model_id else model_id
        body = {"model": short, "messages": messages, **params}
        url = base + "/chat/completions"
        resp = self._session.post(url, headers=headers, data=json.dumps(body), timeout=self.timeout)
        if resp.status_code in {401, 403}:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{provider}: authentication failed (status {resp.status_code})")
        if resp.status_code == 429:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{provider}: rate limited (status 429)")
        resp.raise_for_status()
        data = resp.json()
        return str(data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"])

    def _attempt(self, role: str, messages: list[dict[str, str]], params: dict[str, Any], tried: set[str]) -> GatewayResult | None:
        model_id, hint = self.role_model(role)
        if not model_id or model_id in tried:
            return None
        tried.add(model_id)
        provider = self.provider_for(model_id)
        started = time.time()
        try:
            if self.dry_run:
                text = f"[dry-run {role}] would call {model_id}"
                ok = True
            else:
                text = self._post_chat(provider, model_id, messages, params)
                ok = bool(text)
            elapsed = time.time() - started
            self.registry.record_outcome(model_id, ok, elapsed * 1000.0)
            return GatewayResult(
                text=text,
                model=model_id,
                provider=provider,
                role=role,
                cached=False,
                elapsed_s=elapsed,
                mock=False,
                fallback=bool(tried) and len(tried) > 1,
                attempts=len(tried),
                extra={"hint": hint},
            )
        except Exception as exc:  # provider error / rate limit / timeout -> fallback
            elapsed = time.time() - started
            self.registry.record_outcome(model_id, False, elapsed * 1000.0)
            self.logger.log(
                "gateway_attempt",
                role=role,
                model=model_id,
                provider=provider,
                ok=False,
                error=type(exc).__name__,
                elapsed_s=round(elapsed, 4),
                mock=self.mock,
            )
            return None

    def complete(
        self,
        messages: list[dict[str, str]],
        role: str = "fast_chat",
        params: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> GatewayResult:
        """Chat completion routed to the best model for ``role`` with fallback."""
        if role not in ROLES:
            role = "fast_chat"
        params = dict(params or {})
        if self.mock:
            started = time.time()
            text = f"[mock-llm {role}] " + __import__("hashlib").sha256(
                json.dumps({"messages": messages, "params": params}, sort_keys=True).encode()
            ).hexdigest()[:24]
            result = GatewayResult(
                text=text, model="mock-model", provider="mock", role=role, cached=False,
                elapsed_s=time.time() - started, mock=True, fallback=False, attempts=1,
            )
            self.logger.log("gateway_complete", **result.metadata())
            return result

        tried: set[str] = set()
        last_error = ""
        for _ in range(max(1, self.max_retries + len(self.registry.records))):
            result = self._attempt(role, messages, params, tried)
            if result is not None:
                self.logger.log("gateway_complete", **result.metadata())
                return result
            # try next model for the role
            model_id, _ = self.role_model(role)
            if not model_id or model_id in tried:
                break
        # nothing available -> deterministic local fallback so the system never deadlocks
        text = f"[local-fallback {role}] no hosted model reachable; {last_error or 'no providers configured'}"
        return GatewayResult(
            text=text,
            model="local-fallback",
            provider="local",
            role=role,
            cached=False,
            elapsed_s=0.0,
            mock=True,
            fallback=True,
            attempts=len(tried),
            extra={"error": last_error},
        )

    def embed(self, texts: list[str], role: str = "embeddings") -> list[list[float]]:
        """Embeddings via the best embedding model, with a local hash fallback."""
        model_id, hint = self.role_model(role)
        provider = self.provider_for(model_id) if model_id else "local"
        key = get_api_key(provider) if model_id else None
        if model_id and key and provider in CHAT_BASE_URLS:
            base = CHAT_BASE_URLS[provider].rstrip("/")
            short = model_id.split("/", 1)[-1]
            try:
                headers = provider_headers(provider, key)
                body = {"model": short, "input": texts}
                resp = self._session.post(base + "/embeddings", headers=headers, data=json.dumps(body), timeout=self.timeout)
                resp.raise_for_status()
                data = resp.json()
                return [item["embedding"] for item in data["data"]]
            except Exception:
                pass
        # local deterministic fallback
        import hashlib

        dim = 64
        return [
            [float(int.from_bytes(hashlib.sha256((t + str(i)).encode()).digest()[:4], "big")) / 2**32 for i in range(dim)]
            for t in texts
        ]

=== omni/hud/state.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any


def default_state_path() -> Path:
    return Path(os.environ.get("OMNI_HUD_STATE", Path.home() / ".cache" / "omni" / "hud" / "state.json"))


def write_hud_state(path: str | Path | None, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> Path:
    target = Path(path) if path else default_state_path()
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = target.with_suffix(".tmp")
    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    tmp.replace(target)
    return target

Backends (selected by OMNI_LLM_BACKEND):
  mock    - deterministic, offline; always tagged mock=True
  ollama  - local OpenAI-ish Ollama server (OMNI_OLLAMA_URL)
  openai  - any OpenAI-compatible endpoint (OMNI_LLM_BASE_URL, OMNI_LLM_API_KEY)

Every request is keyed by hash(backend+model+prompt+params) and cached under
OMNI_CACHE_DIR (default ~/.cache/omni). Cache hits are flagged cached=True so
benchmark runs can exclude them from paper data.

This is a hashing embedding, not a neural embedding. It exists so the first
routing decision needs no user input and no network/model download. Swap in
``LLMClient.embed`` when a real embedding model is configured; the interface
is the same (a fixed-length, L2-normalised list of floats).

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from omni.llm.embeddings import HashEmbedder
from omni.telemetry import JsonlLogger, mock_enabled


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LLMResult:
    text: str
    model: str
    backend: str
    mock: bool
    cached: bool
    elapsed_s: float
    cache_key: str
    extra: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def metadata(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "model": self.model,
            "backend": self.backend,
            "mock": self.mock,
            "cached": self.cached,
            "elapsed_s": round(self.elapsed_s, 4),
            "cache_key": self.cache_key,
            **self.extra,
        }


class LLMClient:
    """Single LLM entry point with an on-disk cache.

    Backends (selected by OMNI_LLM_BACKEND):
      mock    - deterministic, offline; always tagged mock=True
      ollama  - local OpenAI-ish Ollama server (OMNI_OLLAMA_URL)
      openai  - any OpenAI-compatible endpoint (OMNI_LLM_BASE_URL, OMNI_LLM_API_KEY)

    Every request is keyed by hash(backend+model+prompt+params) and cached under
    OMNI_CACHE_DIR (default ~/.cache/omni). Cache hits are flagged cached=True so
    benchmark runs can exclude them from paper data.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        backend: str | None = None,
        model: str | None = None,
        cache_dir: str | Path | None = None,
        mock: bool | None = None,
        logger: JsonlLogger | None = None,
        timeout: float = 120.0,
    ) -> None:
        self.backend = (backend or os.environ.get("OMNI_LLM_BACKEND", "ollama")).lower()
        if mock or os.environ.get("OMNI_LLM_MOCK", "").lower() in {"1", "true", "yes"}:
            self.backend = "mock"
        self.model = model or os.environ.get("OMNI_LLM_MODEL", "")
        self.cache_dir = Path(cache_dir or os.environ.get("OMNI_CACHE_DIR", Path.home() / ".cache" / "omni")) / "llm"
        self.cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.timeout = timeout
        self.logger = logger or JsonlLogger(self.cache_dir.parent / "llm_calls.jsonl", mock=self.backend == "mock")

    @property
    def is_mock(self) -> bool:
        return self.backend == "mock" or mock_enabled()

    def _key(self, kind: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        blob = json.dumps({"kind": kind, "backend": self.backend, "model": self.model, **payload}, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
        return hashlib.sha256(blob.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

    def _cache_path(self, key: str) -> Path:
        return self.cache_dir / f"{key}.json"

    def _read(self, key: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        path = self._cache_path(key)
        if not path.exists():
            return None
        try:
            return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            return None

    def _write(self, key: str, entry: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        path = self._cache_path(key)
        tmp = path.with_suffix(".tmp")
        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(entry, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
        tmp.replace(path)

    def complete(self, prompt: str, params: dict[str, Any] | None = None, model: str | None = None) -> LLMResult:
        params = params or {}
        model_name = model or self.model or ("mock-model" if self.is_mock else "")
        key = self._key("complete", {"prompt": prompt, "params": params, "model": model_name})
        started = time.time()
        cached = self._read(key)
        if cached is not None:
            result = LLMResult(text=cached["text"], model=cached.get("model", model_name), backend=cached.get("backend", self.backend), mock=bool(cached.get("mock", False)), cached=True, elapsed_s=time.time() - started, cache_key=key)
            self.logger.log("llm_complete", **result.metadata())
            return result
        if self.is_mock:
            text = "[mock-llm] " + hashlib.sha256(prompt.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:24]
            entry = {"text": text, "model": model_name, "backend": "mock", "mock": True}
        else:
            text = self._http_complete(prompt, params, model_name)
            entry = {"text": text, "model": model_name, "backend": self.backend, "mock": False}
        self._write(key, entry)
        result = LLMResult(text=text, model=model_name, backend=entry["backend"], mock=bool(entry["mock"]), cached=False, elapsed_s=time.time() - started, cache_key=key)
        self.logger.log("llm_complete", **result.metadata())
        return result

    def embed(self, texts: list[str], params: dict[str, Any] | None = None, model: str | None = None) -> list[list[float]]:
        params = params or {}
        model_name = model or self.model or ("mock-embed" if self.is_mock else "")
        key = self._key("embed", {"texts": texts, "params": params, "model": model_name})
        started = time.time()
        cached = self._read(key)
        if cached is not None:
            self.logger.log("llm_embed", model=model_name, backend=cached.get("backend", self.backend), mock=bool(cached.get("mock", False)), cached=True, elapsed_s=round(time.time() - started, 4), cache_key=key, count=len(texts))
            return cached["vectors"]
        if self.is_mock:
            embedder = HashEmbedder(int(params.get("dim", 64)))
            vectors = [embedder.embed(t) for t in texts]
            entry = {"vectors": vectors, "model": model_name, "backend": "mock", "mock": True}
        else:
            vectors = self._http_embed(texts, model_name)
            entry = {"vectors": vectors, "model": model_name, "backend": self.backend, "mock": False}
        self._write(key, entry)
        self.logger.log("llm_embed", model=model_name, backend=entry["backend"], mock=bool(entry["mock"]), cached=False, elapsed_s=round(time.time() - started, 4), cache_key=key, count=len(texts))
        return vectors

    def _http_complete(self, prompt: str, params: dict[str, Any], model: str) -> str:
        if not model:
            raise RuntimeError("OMNI_LLM_MODEL is required for a non-mock backend")
        if self.backend == "ollama":
            url = os.environ.get("OMNI_OLLAMA_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:11434").rstrip("/") + "/api/generate"
            body = {"model": model, "prompt": prompt, "stream": False, "options": params}
            data = self._post(url, body)
            return str(data.get("response", ""))
        if self.backend in {"openai", "openai-compatible"}:
            base = os.environ.get("OMNI_LLM_BASE_URL", "https://api.openai.com/v1").rstrip("/")
            body = {"model": model, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}], **params}
            data = self._post(base + "/chat/completions", body, auth=True)
            return str(data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"])
        raise RuntimeError(f"Unknown LLM backend: {self.backend}")

    def _http_embed(self, texts: list[str], model: str) -> list[list[float]]:
        if self.backend == "ollama":
            url = os.environ.get("OMNI_OLLAMA_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:11434").rstrip("/") + "/api/embeddings"
            return [self._post(url, {"model": model, "prompt": text}).get("embedding", []) for text in texts]
        if self.backend in {"openai", "openai-compatible"}:
            base = os.environ.get("OMNI_LLM_BASE_URL", "https://api.openai.com/v1").rstrip("/")
            data = self._post(base + "/embeddings", {"model": model, "input": texts}, auth=True)
            return [item["embedding"] for item in data["data"]]
        raise RuntimeError(f"Unknown embedding backend: {self.backend}")

    def _post(self, url: str, body: dict[str, Any], auth: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
        request = urllib.request.Request(url, data=json.dumps(body).encode("utf-8"), headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
        if auth and os.environ.get("OMNI_LLM_API_KEY"):
            request.add_header("Authorization", "Bearer " + os.environ["OMNI_LLM_API_KEY"])
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=self.timeout) as response:
                return json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
            raise RuntimeError(f"LLM backend {self.backend} HTTP {exc.code}") from exc
        except urllib.error.URLError as exc:
            raise RuntimeError(f"LLM backend {self.backend} unreachable: {exc.reason}") from exc

=== omni/llm/embeddings.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import math
import re

_TOKEN = re.compile(r"[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_+.-]*")


class HashEmbedder:
    """Deterministic local bag-of-words embedding.

    This is a hashing embedding, not a neural embedding. It exists so the first
    routing decision needs no user input and no network/model download. Swap in
    ``LLMClient.embed`` when a real embedding model is configured; the interface
    is the same (a fixed-length, L2-normalised list of floats).
    """

    def __init__(self, dim: int = 114, seed: str = "omni-embed-v1") -> None:
        self.dim = dim
        self.seed = seed.encode("utf-8")

    def embed(self, text: str) -> list[float]:
        vec = [0.0] * self.dim
        tokens = _TOKEN.findall((text or "").lower())
        grams = tokens + [f"{a}_{b}" for a, b in zip(tokens, tokens[1:])]
        for token in grams:
            digest = hashlib.blake2b(token.encode("utf-8"), digest_size=16, key=self.seed).digest()
            idx = int.from_bytes(digest[:8], "little") % self.dim
            sign = 1.0 if digest[8] & 1 else -1.0
            vec[idx] += sign
        norm = math.sqrt(sum(v * v for v in vec)) or 1.0
        return [v / norm for v in vec]

=== omni/main.py ===
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from omni.config import settings
from omni.orchestrator.engine import Orchestrator
from omni.planner.demo import DemoPlanner


def _status() -> int:
    print(f"Workspace: {settings.workspace_root}")
    print(f"Mode: {settings.mode}")
    print("Planner: DemoPlanner")
    return 0


def _inspect_desktop() -> int:
    from omni.adapters.desktop import DesktopAdapter
    info = DesktopAdapter().inspect_desktop()
    print(json.dumps(info, indent=2))
    return 0


def _submit_task(goal: str, workspace: str | None = None) -> int:
    orchestrator = Orchestrator(workspace_root=workspace or str(settings.workspace_root))
    task = orchestrator.create_task(goal)
    print(f"Task created: {task.task_id}")
    for step in task.plan.steps:
        print(f"- {step.id}: {step.name}: {step.description}")
    return 0


def _run_demo(workspace: str | None = None) -> int:
    workspace_root = workspace or str(settings.workspace_root)
    root = Path(workspace_root)
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    orchestrator = Orchestrator(workspace_root=str(root))
    task = orchestrator.create_task("Create a Python project in my OMNI workspace, implement a small program, run its tests, detect a deliberate failure, repair it, and verify the final tests.")
    task_result = orchestrator.run_plan(task.task_id)
    print(json.dumps({"task_id": task_result.task_id, "status": task_result.status, "report": task_result.final_report}, indent=2))
    if task_result.status == "SUCCEEDED":
        return 0
    return 1


def _launch_dashboard(host: str, port: int) -> int:
    import uvicorn
    from omni.api.app import app
    uvicorn.run(app, host=host, port=port, reload=False)
    return 0


def _self_test() -> int:
    import pytest
    return pytest.main(["-q", "tests"]) if Path("tests").exists() else 1


def _inspect_context() -> int:
    from omni.context.hyprland import HyprlandContextCollector
    collector = HyprlandContextCollector()
    snapshot = collector.snapshot()
    print(json.dumps({"context": snapshot.as_dict(), "privacy": snapshot.privacy_summary()}, indent=2))
    return 0


def _route(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    from omni.modules.ampl import AMPL, ModuleResidency
    from omni.routing.service import RoutingService
    from omni.scheduler.actuator import CgroupV2Actuator
    from omni.scheduler.service import SchedulerService
    from omni.telemetry import JsonlLogger

    ampl = None
    residency = None
    if not getattr(args, "no_modules", False):
        ampl = AMPL(max_budget_mb=args.budget_mb)
        residency = ModuleResidency(ampl.library)
    scheduler = None
    if not getattr(args, "no_scheduler", False):
        log_dir = Path(os.environ.get("OMNI_ROUTING_LOG", Path.home() / ".cache" / "omni" / "routing" / "decisions.jsonl")).parent
        scheduler = SchedulerService(
            actuator=CgroupV2Actuator(),
            apply=bool(args.apply_scheduler),
            logger=JsonlLogger(log_dir / "scheduler.jsonl", mock=args.mock_llm),
        )
    service = RoutingService(
        hud_path=args.hud_state,
        mock=args.mock_llm,
        ampl=ampl,
        residency=residency,
        scheduler=scheduler,
        scheduler_apply=bool(getattr(args, "apply_scheduler", False)),
    )
    if args.once:
        print(json.dumps(service.step(), indent=2))
        return 0
    service.run(interval=args.interval)
    return 0


def _schedule(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    from omni.context.hyprland import HyprlandContextCollector
    from omni.scheduler.actuator import CgroupV2Actuator
    from omni.scheduler.service import SchedulerService
    from omni.scheduler.simulator import simulate, simulate_all

    if args.restore:
        print(json.dumps(CgroupV2Actuator().restore(), indent=2))
        return 0
    if args.simulate:
        payload = simulate_all(steps=args.steps) if args.simulate == "all" else simulate(args.simulate, steps=args.steps)
        print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2))
        return 0
    collector = HyprlandContextCollector()
    snapshot = collector.snapshot()
    service = SchedulerService(apply=args.apply)
    print(json.dumps(service.step(snapshot), indent=2))
    return 0


def _bench(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    from bench.adapters import get_adapter
    from bench.report import build_report
    from bench.runner import BenchRunner, load_records
    from bench.tasks import TASKS, select_tasks

    results_dir = Path(args.results or os.environ.get("OMNI_BENCH_RESULTS", Path.cwd() / "bench" / "results"))
    workspaces_dir = Path(
        args.workspaces or os.environ.get("OMNI_BENCH_WORKSPACES", Path.home() / ".cache" / "omni" / "bench" / "workspaces")
    )
    if args.list:
        print(json.dumps([task.as_dict() for task in TASKS], indent=2))
        return 0

    seeds = [int(value) for value in str(args.seeds).split(",") if value.strip()]
    if args.report:
        records = load_records(results_dir)
        if args.tasks:
            task_ids = [task.id for task in select_tasks(args.tasks)]
        else:
            task_ids = sorted({record.get("task_id") for record in records if not record.get("mock")}) or [task.id for task in TASKS[:3]]
        if args.adapters:
            adapters = [value.strip() for value in args.adapters.split(",") if value.strip()]
        else:
            adapters = sorted({record.get("adapter") for record in records if not record.get("mock")}) or ["opencode"]
        report_seeds = seeds or sorted({int(record.get("seed", 0)) for record in records if not record.get("mock")}) or [0]
        report = build_report(records, task_ids, adapters, report_seeds, results_dir)
        print(json.dumps({key: report[key] for key in ("real_records", "excluded", "by_adapter", "figure_status", "missing_runs")}, indent=2))
        return 0

    if args.tasks:
        tasks = select_tasks(args.tasks)
    elif args.smoke:
        tasks = list(TASKS[:3])
    else:
        tasks = list(TASKS)
    adapter = get_adapter(args.adapter)
    available, detail = adapter.available()
    if not available and args.adapter != "mock":
        print(json.dumps({"adapter": args.adapter, "available": False, "reason": detail}, indent=2))
    runner = BenchRunner(results_dir, workspaces_dir)
    records = runner.run(tasks, adapter, seeds or [0])
    summary = {
        "adapter": adapter.name,
        "tasks": len(tasks),
        "seeds": seeds or [0],
        "results": str(runner.log_path),
        "statuses": {status: sum(1 for record in records if record["status"] == status) for status in sorted({record["status"] for record in records})},
        "mock": bool(adapter.mock),
    }
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
    return 0


def _launch_hud() -> int:
    qs = shutil.which("qs") or shutil.which("quickshell")
    if not qs:
        print("Quickshell is not installed", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    hud_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "hud"
    return subprocess.run([qs, "-p", str(hud_dir)]).returncode



def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="OMNI CLI")
    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    subparsers.add_parser("status")
    subparsers.add_parser("inspect-desktop")
    submit = subparsers.add_parser("submit-task")
    submit.add_argument("goal")
    submit.add_argument("--workspace", default=None)
    demo = subparsers.add_parser("run-demo")
    demo.add_argument("--workspace", default=None)
    dashboard = subparsers.add_parser("dashboard")
    dashboard.add_argument("--host", default=settings.host)
    dashboard.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=settings.port)
    subparsers.add_parser("self-test")
    subparsers.add_parser("inspect-context")
    route = subparsers.add_parser("route")
    route.add_argument("--once", action="store_true", help="route once and print the decision")
    route.add_argument("--interval", type=float, default=1.0)
    route.add_argument("--hud-state", default=None)
    route.add_argument("--mock-llm", action="store_true", help="tag this run as mock; excluded from paper data")
    route.add_argument("--no-modules", action="store_true", help="disable AMPL module residency")
    route.add_argument("--budget-mb", type=float, default=256.0, help="maximum AMPL module budget in MB")
    route.add_argument("--no-scheduler", action="store_true", help="disable A2 KRLS scheduler decisions")
    route.add_argument("--apply-scheduler", action="store_true", help="actuate cgroup v2 weights (default: dry-run)")
    schedule = subparsers.add_parser("schedule")
    schedule.add_argument("--once", action="store_true", help="one decision (default)")
    schedule.add_argument("--apply", action="store_true", help="actuate cgroup v2 weights (default: dry-run)")
    schedule.add_argument("--restore", action="store_true", help="kill switch: restore original weights")
    schedule.add_argument("--simulate", choices=["S1", "S2", "S3", "all"], default=None, help="run simulated policy learning")
    schedule.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=400)
    bench = subparsers.add_parser("bench")
    bench.add_argument("--list", action="store_true", help="list the 30 tasks")
    bench.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true", help="run the first 3 tasks")
    bench.add_argument("--tasks", default=None, help="comma-separated task ids or 'all'")
    bench.add_argument("--adapter", default="mock", choices=["mock", "opencode", "claude", "aider", "interpreter"])
    bench.add_argument("--adapters", default=None, help="report: comma-separated adapters")
    bench.add_argument("--seeds", default="0")
    bench.add_argument("--results", default=None)
    bench.add_argument("--workspaces", default=None)
    bench.add_argument("--report", action="store_true", help="aggregate results and write report/figures")
    subparsers.add_parser("hud")

    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.command == "status":
        return _status()
    if args.command == "inspect-desktop":
        return _inspect_desktop()
    if args.command == "submit-task":
        return _submit_task(args.goal, args.workspace)
    if args.command == "run-demo":
        return _run_demo(args.workspace)
    if args.command == "dashboard":
        return _launch_dashboard(args.host, args.port)
    if args.command == "self-test":
        return _self_test()
    if args.command == "inspect-context":
        return _inspect_context()
    if args.command == "route":
        return _route(args)
    if args.command == "schedule":
        return _schedule(args)
    if args.command == "bench":
        return _bench(args)
    if args.command == "hud":
        return _launch_hud()
    return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

=== omni/model_registry.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import time
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import requests

try:  # system keyring is optional; env vars always work
    import keyring  # type: ignore

    _HAS_KEYRING = True
except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - keyring not installed
    keyring = None  # type: ignore[assignment]
    _HAS_KEYRING = False

SERVICE_NAME = "omni-model-gateway"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModelRecord:
    """A single normalised model entry from a provider's live model list."""

    model_id: str               # provider-prefixed id, e.g. "openrouter/meta-llama/llama-3.1-8b-instruct"
    provider: str
    context_length: int | None = None
    modalities: frozenset[str] = field(default_factory=lambda: frozenset({"text"}))
    tools: bool = False
    price_input_per_1k: float | None = None      # USD per 1k tokens
    price_output_per_1k: float | None = None     # USD per 1k tokens
    open_weights: str = "unknown"                # "yes" | "no" | "unknown" - never guessed
    latency_ms: float = 0.0                       # measured at runtime
    error_rate: float = 0.0                       # measured at runtime
    successes: int = 0
    failures: int = 0
    last_checked: float = 0.0
    source: str = ""                              # provider endpoint used
    raw: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        d = asdict(self)
        d["modalities"] = sorted(self.modalities)
        return d

    @property
    def cost_score(self) -> float:
        """Normalised cost proxy (input+output per 1k); missing prices treated as unknown/high."""
        if self.price_input_per_1k is None and self.price_output_per_1k is None:
            return 1.0
        inp = self.price_input_per_1k or 0.0
        out = self.price_output_per_1k or 0.0
        return inp + out

    @property
    def reliability(self) -> float:
        total = self.successes + self.failures
        if total == 0:
            return 0.5
        return self.successes / total

    def is_available(self) -> bool:
        return self.error_rate < 1.0 and self.reliability > 0.0


def _cache_dir() -> Path:
    return Path(os.environ.get("OMNI_CACHE_DIR", Path.home() / ".cache" / "omni")) / "registry"


def _record_cache_path(provider: str) -> Path:
    _cache_dir().mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return _cache_dir() / f"{provider}.json"


def provider_headers(provider: str, api_key: str | None) -> dict[str, str]:
    headers = {"Accept": "application/json", "User-Agent": "omni-model-gateway/1.0"}
    if api_key:
        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {api_key}"
    return headers


def get_api_key(provider: str) -> str | None:
    """Resolve a provider API key from env or the system keyring. Never logged."""
    env_map = {
        "openrouter": "OPENROUTER_API_KEY",
        "huggingface": "HF_TOKEN",
        "together": "TOGETHER_API_KEY",
        "groq": "GROQ_API_KEY",
        "fireworks": "FIREWORKS_API_KEY",
        "cerebras": "CEREBRAS_API_KEY",
    }
    env_var = env_map.get(provider)
    if env_var:
        value = os.environ.get(env_var)
        if value:
            return value
    if _HAS_KEYRING:
        try:
            value = keyring.get_password(SERVICE_NAME, provider)
            if value:
                return value
        except Exception:
            pass
    return None


class ModelRegistry:
    """Fetches each provider's live model list and normalises it into one registry.

    Model lists are cached on disk with the provider's ETag and a TTL (default
    6 hours) so startup is fast and rate limits on list endpoints are avoided.
    No static model list lives in code.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        providers: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
        ttl_hours: float = 6.0,
        cache_dir: Path | None = None,
        timeout: float = 10.0,
    ) -> None:
        self.ttl_hours = float(ttl_hours)
        self.timeout = timeout
        self.providers: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = providers or {
            "openrouter": {
                "list_url": "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/models",
                "key_env": "OPENROUTER_API_KEY",
            },
            "huggingface": {
                "list_url": "https://api.endpoints.huggingface.cloud/v1/endpoint",
                "key_env": "HF_TOKEN",
            },
            "together": {
                "list_url": "https://api.together.xyz/v1/models",
                "key_env": "TOGETHER_API_KEY",
            },
            "groq": {
                "list_url": "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/models",
                "key_env": "GROQ_API_KEY",
            },
            "fireworks": {
                "list_url": "https://api.fireworks.ai/inference/v1/models",
                "key_env": "FIREWORKS_API_KEY",
            },
            "cerebras": {
                "list_url": "https://api.cerebras.ai/v1/models",
                "key_env": "CEREBRAS_API_KEY",
            },
        }
        self.records: dict[str, ModelRecord] = {}
        self._load_cache()

    # ----- persistence -----
    def _load_cache(self) -> None:
        for provider in self.providers:
            path = _record_cache_path(provider)
            if not path.exists():
                continue
            try:
                payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                for entry in payload.get("models", []):
                    rec = ModelRecord(**entry)
                    self.records[rec.model_id] = rec
            except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
                continue

    def _save_cache(self, provider: str, models: list[ModelRecord], etag: str | None) -> None:
        path = _record_cache_path(provider)
        payload = {
            "provider": provider,
            "etag": etag,
            "fetched_at": time.time(),
            "models": [m.as_dict() for m in models],
        }
        tmp = path.with_suffix(".tmp")
        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
        tmp.replace(path)

    # ----- fetching -----
    def _normalise(self, provider: str, raw: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[ModelRecord]:
        out: list[ModelRecord] = []
        for item in raw:
            mid = str(item.get("id") or item.get("name") or item.get("model_id") or "")
            if not mid:
                continue
            modalities = set(item.get("modalities") or item.get("modalities", []) or {"text"})
            if "text" not in modalities:
                modalities.add("text")
            # licence metadata: only mark open-weights if the provider explicitly says so.
            licence = str(item.get("licence") or item.get("license") or item.get("open_weights") or "").lower()
            if licence in {"yes", "true", "open", "apache", "mit", "llama", "mixtral"}:
                open_weights = "yes"
            elif licence in {"no", "false", "closed", "proprietary"}:
                open_weights = "no"
            else:
                open_weights = "unknown"
            out.append(
                ModelRecord(
                    model_id=f"{provider}/{mid}",
                    provider=provider,
                    context_length=self._int(item.get("context_length") or item.get("context_window")),
                    modalities=frozenset(modalities),
                    tools=bool(item.get("tools") or item.get("tool_calling") or item.get("function_calling")),
                    price_input_per_1k=self._money(item.get("price_input_per_1k") or item.get("pricing", {}).get("input")),
                    price_output_per_1k=self._money(item.get("price_output_per_1k") or item.get("pricing", {}).get("output")),
                    open_weights=open_weights,
                    last_checked=time.time(),
                    source=provider,
                    raw=item,
                )
            )
        return out

    @staticmethod
    def _int(value: Any) -> int | None:
        try:
            return int(value) if value is not None else None
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return None

    @staticmethod
    def _money(value: Any) -> float | None:
        try:
            return float(value) if value is not None else None
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return None

    def fetch_provider(self, provider: str, force: bool = False) -> list[ModelRecord]:
        config = self.providers.get(provider)
        if not config:
            return []
        now = time.time()
        cached = self.records  # may hold stale entries for this provider
        # TTL check using the newest record timestamp for this provider.
        provider_records = [r for r in self.records.values() if r.provider == provider]
        if not force and provider_records and (now - max(r.last_checked for r in provider_records)) < self.ttl_hours * 3600:
            return provider_records
        api_key = get_api_key(provider)
        list_url = config.get("list_url")
        if not list_url or not api_key:
            return provider_records  # no key/endpoint: keep stale records, if any
        headers = provider_headers(provider, api_key)
        try:
            etag_path = _cache_dir() / f"{provider}.etag"
            etag = etag_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip() if etag_path.exists() else None
            if etag:
                headers["If-None-Match"] = etag
            response = requests.get(list_url, headers=headers, timeout=self.timeout)
            if response.status_code == 304:
                return provider_records
            response.raise_for_status()
            if response.headers.get("ETag"):
                etag_path.write_text(response.headers["ETag"], encoding="utf-8")
            data = response.json()
        except Exception:
            return provider_records
        items = data if isinstance(data, list) else data.get("data") or data.get("models") or []
        records = self._normalise(provider, items)
        for rec in records:
            self.records[rec.model_id] = rec
        self._save_cache(provider, records, response.headers.get("ETag") if hasattr(response, "headers") else None)
        return records

    def refresh(self, force: bool = False) -> dict[str, int]:
        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        for provider in self.providers:
            try:
                records = self.fetch_provider(provider, force=force)
                counts[provider] = len(records)
            except Exception:
                counts[provider] = 0
        return counts

    # ----- selection -----
    def candidates(
        self,
        modality: str = "text",
        tools: bool = False,
        max_cost: float | None = None,
        require_open_weights: bool | None = None,
    ) -> list[ModelRecord]:
        out: list[ModelRecord] = []
        for rec in self.records.values():
            if modality not in rec.modalities:
                continue
            if tools and not rec.tools:
                continue
            if max_cost is not None and rec.cost_score > max_cost:
                continue
            if require_open_weights is True and rec.open_weights != "yes":
                continue
            if require_open_weights is False and rec.open_weights == "yes":
                continue
            out.append(rec)
        return out

    def select(
        self,
        role: str,
        modality: str = "text",
        tools: bool = False,
        max_cost: float | None = None,
        prefer_open_weights: bool = False,
    ) -> ModelRecord | None:
        """Pick the best model for a role: low latency + high reliability + low cost."""
        cands = self.candidates(modality=modality, tools=tools, max_cost=max_cost)
        if not cands:
            return None
        # role hints (env override OMNI_MODEL_<ROLE> takes precedence)
        env_key = f"OMNI_MODEL_{role.upper()}"
        forced = os.environ.get(env_key)
        if forced:
            for rec in cands:
                if rec.model_id == forced or rec.model_id.split("/", 1)[-1] == forced:
                    return rec
        if prefer_open_weights:
            cands = [c for c in cands if c.open_weights == "yes"] or cands
        # score: reliability high, latency low, cost low
        now = time.time()

        def score(rec: ModelRecord) -> float:
            age_penalty = min((now - rec.last_checked) / 3600.0, 5.0)  # stale records penalised
            return (
                2.0 * rec.reliability
                - 0.001 * rec.latency_ms
                - 0.5 * rec.cost_score
                - 0.1 * age_penalty
            )

        return max(cands, key=score)

    def record_outcome(self, model_id: str, ok: bool, latency_ms: float) -> None:
        rec = self.records.get(model_id)
        if rec is None:
            return
        if ok:
            rec.__class__  # dataclass is frozen; rebuild
        # frozen dataclass -> replace
        object.__setattr__(rec, "successes", rec.successes + (1 if ok else 0))
        object.__setattr__(rec, "failures", rec.failures + (0 if ok else 1))
        total = rec.successes + rec.failures
        object.__setattr__(rec, "error_rate", rec.failures / total)
        alpha = 0.3
        object.__setattr__(
            rec,
            "latency_ms",
            (1 - alpha) * rec.latency_ms + alpha * float(latency_ms),
        )

pressure = max(available-pressure, PSI-pressure):
  available-pressure rises linearly as MemAvailable/T MemTotal falls below 0.35
  PSI-pressure = min(psi_some_avg10 / 50, 1)  (design mapping, documented)
budget = max_budget * max(floor_ratio, 1 - pressure)
