=== omni/modules/ampl.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Iterable

from omni.modules.descriptor import AppDescriptor, infer_unit_demand
from omni.modules.library import DEFAULT_MODULES, ModuleLibrary, ModuleSpec
from omni.modules.units import UNITS
from omni.telemetry import JsonlLogger


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModulePlan:
    app_class: str
    source: str
    demand: dict[str, float]
    weights: dict[str, float]
    budget_mb: float
    resident: tuple[str, ...]
    load: tuple[str, ...]
    evict: tuple[str, ...]
    total_size_mb: float
    params_m: float
    objective: float
    best_single_id: str | None
    best_single_objective: float
    strategy: str
    reason: str

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "app_class": self.app_class,
            "source": self.source,
            "demand": {k: round(v, 4) for k, v in self.demand.items()},
            "budget_mb": round(self.budget_mb, 2),
            "resident": list(self.resident),
            "resident_mb": round(self.total_size_mb, 2),
            "params_m": round(self.params_m, 2),
            "load": list(self.load),
            "evict": list(self.evict),
            "objective": round(self.objective, 4),
            "best_single": self.best_single_id,
            "best_single_objective": round(self.best_single_objective, 4),
            "strategy": self.strategy,
            "reason": self.reason,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MissResult:
    unit: str
    best_module: str | None
    boosted_weight: float
    resident: tuple[str, ...]
    evicted: tuple[str, ...]
    resident_mb: float
    loaded: bool
    reason: str


class AMPL:
    """Application-conditioned module loading under a shrinking memory budget."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        library: ModuleLibrary | None = None,
        max_budget_mb: float = 256.0,
        boost_step: float = 0.75,
        min_gain_per_mb: float = 0.005,
        logger: JsonlLogger | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.library = library or ModuleLibrary()
        self.max_budget_mb = float(max_budget_mb)
        self.boost_step = float(boost_step)
        self.min_gain_per_mb = float(min_gain_per_mb)
        self.unit_boost: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.logger = logger

    # ---- objective / coverage -------------------------------------------------
    def effective_weights(self, demand: dict[str, float]) -> dict[str, float]:
        return {unit: weight * (1.0 + self.unit_boost.get(unit, 0.0)) for unit, weight in demand.items()}

    def expected_coverage(self, module_ids: Iterable[str], weights: dict[str, float]) -> float:
        modules = [self.library.get(mid) for mid in module_ids]
        total = 0.0
        for unit, weight in weights.items():
            miss = 1.0
            for module in modules:
                miss *= 1.0 - module.covers.get(unit, 0.0)
            total += weight * (1.0 - miss)
        return total

    def _marginal(self, module: ModuleSpec, current_ids: list[str], weights: dict[str, float]) -> float:
        current = [self.library.get(mid) for mid in current_ids]
        gain = 0.0
        for unit, weight in weights.items():
            p = module.covers.get(unit, 0.0)
            if p <= 0:
                continue
            miss_without = 1.0
            for resident in current:
                miss_without *= 1.0 - resident.covers.get(unit, 0.0)
            gain += weight * miss_without * p
        return gain

    def _individual_utility(self, module: ModuleSpec, weights: dict[str, float]) -> float:
        return sum(weight * module.covers.get(unit, 0.0) for unit, weight in weights.items())

    def _size(self, module_ids: Iterable[str]) -> float:
        return sum(self.library.get(mid).size_mb for mid in module_ids)

    def _evict_to_budget(self, resident: list[str], budget_mb: float, weights: dict[str, float]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
        evicted: list[str] = []
        while resident and self._size(resident) > budget_mb:
            scored = sorted(
                ((self._individual_utility(self.library.get(mid), weights) / max(self.library.get(mid).size_mb, 1e-9), mid) for mid in resident),
            )
            victim = scored[0][1]
            resident.remove(victim)
            evicted.append(victim)
        return resident, evicted

    # ---- selection -------------------------------------------------------------
    def select(self, descriptor: AppDescriptor, budget_mb: float, resident_ids: Iterable[str] = ()) -> ModulePlan:
        demand, source = infer_unit_demand(descriptor)
        weights = self.effective_weights(demand)
        budget = max(float(budget_mb), 0.0)
        resident: list[str] = []
        for mid in resident_ids:
            if mid in self.library.by_id and mid not in resident:
                resident.append(mid)
        resident, evicted = self._evict_to_budget(resident, budget, weights)
        base_objective = self.expected_coverage(resident, weights)

        # Cost-benefit greedy: maximise marginal weighted coverage per MB.
        additions: list[str] = []
        while True:
            best: tuple[float, float, str] | None = None
            for module in self.library.modules:
                if module.id in resident:
                    continue
                if self._size(resident) + module.size_mb > budget + 1e-9:
                    continue
                gain = self._marginal(module, resident, weights)
                if gain <= 1e-9:
                    continue
                ratio = gain / module.size_mb
                if ratio < self.min_gain_per_mb:
                    continue
                candidate = (ratio, gain, module.id)
                if best is None or candidate > best:
                    best = candidate
            if best is None:
                break
            resident.append(best[2])
            additions.append(best[2])

        if not additions:
            best_single_id = None
            best_single_obj = 0.0
            best_single_add_obj = base_objective
            strategy = "resident-only"
            reason = "no positive marginal module fits the current budget"
        else:
            singles = [m for m in self.library.modules if m.size_mb <= budget + 1e-9]
            best_single = max(singles, key=lambda m: self.expected_coverage([m.id], weights)) if singles else None
            best_single_id = best_single.id if best_single else None
            best_single_obj = self.expected_coverage([best_single.id], weights) if best_single else 0.0
            base_set = set(resident) - set(additions)
            add_options = [m for m in self.library.modules if m.id not in base_set and m.size_mb <= budget + 1e-9]
            best_add = max(add_options, key=lambda m: self.expected_coverage(list(base_set) + [m.id], weights)) if add_options else None
            best_single_add_obj = self.expected_coverage(list(base_set) + [best_add.id], weights) if best_add else base_objective
            greedy_obj = self.expected_coverage(resident, weights)
            if len(additions) > 1 and greedy_obj <= best_single_add_obj + 1e-9 and best_add is not None:
                additions = [best_add.id]
                resident = list(base_set) + additions
                strategy = "single"
                reason = f"greedy set did not beat best single module {best_add.id}; chose single"
            else:
                strategy = "greedy"
                reason = f"greedy added {len(additions)} module(s); objective {greedy_obj:.3f} vs best single {best_single_add_obj:.3f}"

        objective = self.expected_coverage(resident, weights)
        params = sum(self.library.get(mid).params_m for mid in resident)
        plan = ModulePlan(
            app_class=descriptor.app_class,
            source=source,
            demand=demand,
            weights=weights,
            budget_mb=budget,
            resident=tuple(resident),
            load=tuple(additions),
            evict=tuple(evicted),
            total_size_mb=self._size(resident),
            params_m=params,
            objective=objective,
            best_single_id=best_single_id,
            best_single_objective=best_single_obj,
            strategy=strategy,
            reason=reason,
        )
        if self.logger:
            self.logger.log("module_plan", **plan.as_dict(), mock=self.logger.mock)
        return plan

    def handle_miss(self, unit: str, descriptor: AppDescriptor, budget_mb: float, resident_ids: Iterable[str]) -> MissResult:
        if unit not in UNITS:
            raise ValueError(f"unknown capability unit: {unit}")
        self.unit_boost[unit] = self.unit_boost.get(unit, 0.0) + self.boost_step
        boosted = self.unit_boost[unit]
        candidates = sorted(
            (m for m in self.library.modules if m.covers.get(unit, 0.0) > 0),
            key=lambda m: (-m.covers.get(unit, 0.0), m.size_mb),
        )
        resident = [mid for mid in resident_ids if mid in self.library.by_id]
        if not candidates:
            return MissResult(unit, None, boosted, tuple(resident), (), self._size(resident), False, "no module covers unit")
        best = candidates[0]
        if best.id in resident:
            return MissResult(unit, best.id, boosted, tuple(resident), (), self._size(resident), True, "best module already resident")
        if best.size_mb > budget_mb + 1e-9:
            return MissResult(unit, best.id, boosted, tuple(resident), (), self._size(resident), False, "best module exceeds budget")
        resident.append(best.id)
        weights = {unit: 1.0 + boosted, **{u: 1.0 for u in best.covers}}
        evicted: list[str] = []
        while self._size(resident) > budget_mb + 1e-9:
            evictable = [mid for mid in resident if mid != best.id]
            if not evictable:
                resident.remove(best.id)
                return MissResult(unit, best.id, boosted, tuple(resident), tuple(evicted), self._size(resident), False, "cannot fit module after eviction")
            victim = min(evictable, key=lambda mid: self._individual_utility(self.library.get(mid), weights) / max(self.library.get(mid).size_mb, 1e-9))
            resident.remove(victim)
            evicted.append(victim)
        result = MissResult(unit, best.id, boosted, tuple(resident), tuple(evicted), self._size(resident), True, "loaded best covering module")
        if self.logger:
            self.logger.log(
                "capability_miss",
                unit=unit,
                module=best.id,
                boosted_weight=boosted,
                resident=list(result.resident),
                evicted=list(result.evicted),
                resident_mb=result.resident_mb,
                app=descriptor.app_class,
                mock=self.logger.mock,
            )
        return result


class ModuleResidency:
    """Tracks which modules are resident and their declared memory/params."""

    def __init__(self, library: ModuleLibrary | None = None, ids: Iterable[str] = ()) -> None:
        self.library = library or ModuleLibrary()
        self.ids: list[str] = [mid for mid in ids if mid in self.library.by_id]

    def apply(self, plan: ModulePlan) -> list[str]:
        self.ids = list(plan.resident)
        return self.ids

    def set(self, ids: Iterable[str]) -> list[str]:
        self.ids = [mid for mid in ids if mid in self.library.by_id]
        return self.ids

    def size_mb(self) -> float:
        return sum(self.library.get(mid).size_mb for mid in self.ids)

    def params_m(self) -> float:
        return sum(self.library.get(mid).params_m for mid in self.ids)

    def state(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "resident": list(self.ids),
            "resident_mb": round(self.size_mb(), 2),
            "params_m": round(self.params_m(), 2),
        }

=== omni/modules/descriptor.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from omni.context.features import ContextSnapshot
from omni.modules.units import UNITS


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AppDescriptor:
    app_class: str
    title: str = ""
    atspi_roles: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    recent_actions: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    clipboard_types: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    workspace_id: int = 0

    @classmethod
    def from_snapshot(cls, snapshot: ContextSnapshot, recent_actions: dict[str, int] | None = None) -> "AppDescriptor":
        return cls(
            app_class=snapshot.app_class,
            title=snapshot.title,
            atspi_roles=dict(snapshot.atspi_roles),
            recent_actions=dict(recent_actions or {}),
            clipboard_types=tuple(snapshot.clipboard_types),
            workspace_id=snapshot.workspace_id,
        )


# Hand-authored priors for common classes. Unknown classes deliberately fall
# through to element-type/action statistics, so no app needs hand configuration.
_APP_PRIORS: tuple[tuple[tuple[str, ...], dict[str, float]], ...] = (
    (("code", "vscode", "codium"), {"code-edit": 1.0, "terminal": 0.55, "git": 0.50, "test-run": 0.45, "file-edit": 0.35}),
    (("chrome", "chromium", "firefox", "brave", "edge"), {"browser-nav": 1.0, "forms": 0.55, "download": 0.45, "search": 0.45, "vision": 0.20}),
    (("alacritty", "kitty", "foot", "konsole", "wezterm", "terminal"), {"terminal": 1.0, "test-run": 0.55, "git": 0.45, "file-edit": 0.30}),
    (("nautilus", "thunar", "dolphin", "nemo", "pcmanfm"), {"file-edit": 0.95, "file-search": 0.85, "clipboard": 0.35}),
    (("libreoffice-writer", "gedit", "obsidian", "notion"), {"doc-edit": 1.0, "file-edit": 0.50, "clipboard": 0.35}),
    (("libreoffice-calc", "gnumeric", "excel"), {"data-table": 0.90, "doc-edit": 0.45, "clipboard": 0.30}),
    (("mpv", "vlc", "imv", "eog", "gimp"), {"vision": 0.80, "window-control": 0.35}),
)

_ROLE_UNITS: tuple[tuple[str, dict[str, float]], ...] = (

    ("canvas", {"vision": 0.6}),

    ("scroll bar", {"window-control": 0.3}),
)

_ACTION_UNITS: tuple[tuple[str, dict[str, float]], ...] = (
    ("write_file", {"file-edit": 1.0}),
    ("create_file", {"file-edit": 1.0}),
    ("read_file", {"file-search": 0.6}),

    ("fill_form", {"forms": 1.0}),

    ("click", {"vision": 0.5, "window-control": 0.5}),
    ("edit_document", {"doc-edit": 1.0}),
    ("plan", {"planning": 1.0}),
)

_TITLE_UNITS: tuple[tuple[str, dict[str, float]], ...] = (
    ("terminal", {"terminal": 0.8}),
    ("shell", {"terminal": 0.7}),
    ("bash", {"terminal": 0.5}),
    ("test", {"test-run": 0.6}),
    ("pytest", {"test-run": 0.9}),
    ("git", {"git": 0.7}),
    ("commit", {"git": 0.6}),

    (".py", {"code-edit": 0.7}),
    (".rs", {"code-edit": 0.7}),
    (".ts", {"code-edit": 0.6}),
    ("readme", {"doc-edit": 0.7}),
    (".md", {"doc-edit": 0.7}),
    (".txt", {"doc-edit": 0.6}),
    ("http", {"browser-nav": 0.7}),
    ("localhost", {"browser-nav": 0.6}),
    ("invoice", {"data-table": 0.6}),
    ("spreadsheet", {"data-table": 0.8}),
)


def _add(demand: dict[str, float], units: dict[str, float], scale: float = 1.0) -> None:
    for unit, weight in units.items():
        if unit in UNITS:
            demand[unit] = demand.get(unit, 0.0) + float(weight) * scale


def infer_unit_demand(descriptor: AppDescriptor) -> tuple[dict[str, float], str]:
    """Infer capability-unit demand from app class, AT-SPI roles, actions, title."""
    demand: dict[str, float] = {}
    app = (descriptor.app_class or "").lower()
    known = False
    for patterns, units in _APP_PRIORS:
        if any(pattern in app for pattern in patterns):
            _add(demand, units, 1.0)
            known = True
            break

    total_roles = sum(max(c, 0) for c in descriptor.atspi_roles.values())
    for role, count in descriptor.atspi_roles.items():
        role_l = role.lower()
        share = (max(count, 0) / total_roles) if total_roles else 0.0
        for pattern, units in _ROLE_UNITS:
            if pattern in role_l:
                _add(demand, units, min(1.0, share * 3.0))
                break

    for action, count in descriptor.recent_actions.items():
        action_l = action.lower()
        scale = min(1.0, math.log1p(max(count, 0)) / 2.0)
        for pattern, units in _ACTION_UNITS:
            if pattern in action_l:
                _add(demand, units, scale)
                break

    title_l = (descriptor.title or "").lower()
    for pattern, units in _TITLE_UNITS:
        if pattern in title_l:
            _add(demand, units, 0.5)

    if any("text" in t.lower() for t in descriptor.clipboard_types):
        _add(demand, {"clipboard": 0.4, "doc-edit": 0.2})
    if any("image" in t.lower() for t in descriptor.clipboard_types):
        _add(demand, {"vision": 0.4})

    if not demand:
        demand = {"window-control": 0.5, "planning": 0.2}
        source = "generic"
    elif known:
        source = "app+roles"
    else:
        source = "roles"

    demand = {unit: round(min(weight, 1.0), 4) for unit, weight in demand.items() if weight > 0}
    return dict(sorted(demand.items(), key=lambda item: item[1], reverse=True)), source

=== omni/modules/library.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from omni.modules.units import UNITS


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModuleSpec:
    id: str
    name: str
    kind: str  # "lora" | "tool-schema" | "prompt-pack"
    size_mb: float
    params_m: float
    covers: dict[str, float]
    description: str = ""
    tags: tuple[str, ...] = field(default_factory=tuple)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.size_mb <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"{self.id}: size_mb must be positive")
        if any(unit not in UNITS for unit in self.covers):
            unknown = sorted(set(self.covers) - set(UNITS))
            raise ValueError(f"{self.id}: unknown capability units {unknown}")
        if any(not 0.0 < float(p) <= 1.0 for p in self.covers.values()):
            raise ValueError(f"{self.id}: coverage probabilities must be in (0, 1]")


# Declared sizes and coverage are hand-authored design priors, not measured
# performance. Phase 4/5 should calibrate them against real runs.
DEFAULT_MODULES: tuple[ModuleSpec, ...] = (
    ModuleSpec("files-core", "Files Core", "tool-schema", 8.0, 0.0, {"file-edit": 0.90, "file-search": 0.60, "clipboard": 0.40}, "Filesystem actions and workspace-scoped path handling.", ("files", "paths")),
    ModuleSpec("editor-lora", "Editor Adapter", "lora", 24.0, 6.0, {"doc-edit": 0.85, "code-edit": 0.35, "clipboard": 0.20}, "Text/document editing adapter.", ("document", "editor")),
    ModuleSpec("terminal-tools", "Terminal Tools", "tool-schema", 14.0, 0.0, {"terminal": 0.90, "test-run": 0.70, "git": 0.55, "file-search": 0.30}, "Shell, build, test and git tool schemas.", ("terminal", "dev")),
    ModuleSpec("browser-tools", "Browser Tools", "tool-schema", 12.0, 0.0, {"browser-nav": 0.85, "forms": 0.60, "download": 0.55, "search": 0.35}, "Tab, URL, form and download controls.", ("browser", "web")),
    ModuleSpec("vision-lora", "Vision Adapter", "lora", 96.0, 24.0, {"vision": 0.90, "window-control": 0.45, "forms": 0.25}, "Visual grounding for screenshots and layout.", ("vision", "screen")),
    ModuleSpec("planner-pack", "Planner Pack", "prompt-pack", 10.0, 0.0, {"planning": 0.80, "cross-app": 0.70, "window-control": 0.20}, "Decomposition and cross-application handoff prompts.", ("planning", "workflow")),
    ModuleSpec("office-tools", "Office Tools", "tool-schema", 18.0, 0.0, {"doc-edit": 0.65, "data-table": 0.55, "forms": 0.45, "clipboard": 0.25}, "Document, spreadsheet and form operations.", ("office", "documents")),
    ModuleSpec("dev-tools", "Developer Adapter", "lora", 22.0, 5.0, {"code-edit": 0.80, "test-run": 0.55, "git": 0.70, "terminal": 0.30}, "Code editing and repository workflows.", ("code", "dev")),
    ModuleSpec("web-scrape", "Web Extract", "tool-schema", 10.0, 0.0, {"search": 0.80, "browser-nav": 0.45, "data-table": 0.35, "download": 0.25}, "Page text/table extraction and search.", ("web", "search")),
    ModuleSpec("clipboard-pack", "Clipboard Pack", "tool-schema", 4.0, 0.0, {"clipboard": 0.75, "file-edit": 0.30}, "Clipboard read/write without storing content.", ("clipboard",)),
    ModuleSpec("accessibility-pack", "Accessibility Pack", "tool-schema", 6.0, 0.0, {"window-control": 0.65, "forms": 0.50, "vision": 0.15}, "AT-SPI element actions and focus control.", ("atspi", "a11y")),
)


class ModuleLibrary:
    def __init__(self, modules: tuple[ModuleSpec, ...] = DEFAULT_MODULES) -> None:
        self.modules = tuple(modules)
        self.by_id = {m.id: m for m in self.modules}
        if len(self.by_id) != len(self.modules):
            raise ValueError("duplicate module id")

    def get(self, module_id: str) -> ModuleSpec:
        return self.by_id[module_id]

    def total_size_mb(self) -> float:
        return sum(m.size_mb for m in self.modules)

=== omni/modules/memory.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MemoryState:
    captured_at: float
    mem_total_mb: float
    mem_available_mb: float
    cgroup_current_mb: float | None
    cgroup_max_mb: float | None
    psi_some_avg10: float | None
    pressure: float
    budget_mb: float

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "captured_at": self.captured_at,
            "mem_total_mb": round(self.mem_total_mb, 1),
            "mem_available_mb": round(self.mem_available_mb, 1),
            "cgroup_current_mb": None if self.cgroup_current_mb is None else round(self.cgroup_current_mb, 1),
            "cgroup_max_mb": None if self.cgroup_max_mb is None else round(self.cgroup_max_mb, 1),
            "psi_some_avg10": self.psi_some_avg10,
            "pressure": round(self.pressure, 4),
            "budget_mb": round(self.budget_mb, 2),
        }


def _read_meminfo(path: Path) -> tuple[float, float] | None:
    try:
        values: dict[str, float] = {}
        for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            key, _, rest = line.partition(":")
            parts = rest.strip().split()
            if parts and parts[0].replace(".", "", 1).isdigit():
                values[key] = float(parts[0]) / 1024.0
        return values.get("MemTotal", 0.0), values.get("MemAvailable", 0.0)
    except OSError:
        return None


def _read_psi(path: Path) -> float | None:
    try:
        for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            if line.startswith("some "):
                for field in line.split():
                    if field.startswith("avg10="):
                        return float(field.split("=", 1)[1])
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None
    return None


def _read_cgroup(directory: Path) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
    def read_mb(name: str) -> float | None:
        try:
            text = (directory / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
        except OSError:
            return None
        if text == "max":
            return None
        try:
            return float(text) / (1024.0 * 1024.0)
        except ValueError:
            return None

    return read_mb("memory.current"), read_mb("memory.max")


def measure_memory(
    max_budget_mb: float = 256.0,
    floor_ratio: float = 0.15,
    meminfo_path: Path | None = None,
    psi_path: Path | None = None,
    cgroup_dir: Path | None = None,
) -> MemoryState:
    """Read real memory pressure and shrink the module budget accordingly.

    pressure = max(available-pressure, PSI-pressure):
      available-pressure rises linearly as MemAvailable/T MemTotal falls below 0.35
      PSI-pressure = min(psi_some_avg10 / 50, 1)  (design mapping, documented)
    budget = max_budget * max(floor_ratio, 1 - pressure)
    """
    mem = _read_meminfo(meminfo_path or Path("/proc/meminfo"))
    if mem is None:
        total, available = 0.0, 0.0
        avail_pressure = 1.0
    else:
        total, available = mem
        ratio = (available / total) if total > 0 else 0.0
        avail_pressure = min(1.0, max(0.0, (0.35 - ratio) / 0.35))
    psi = _read_psi(psi_path or Path("/proc/pressure/memory"))
    psi_pressure = min(1.0, max(0.0, (psi or 0.0) / 50.0))
    pressure = max(avail_pressure, psi_pressure)
    budget = float(max_budget_mb) * max(float(floor_ratio), 1.0 - pressure)
    current, maximum = _read_cgroup(cgroup_dir or Path("/sys/fs/cgroup"))
    return MemoryState(
        captured_at=time.time(),
        mem_total_mb=total,
        mem_available_mb=available,
        cgroup_current_mb=current,
        cgroup_max_mb=maximum,
        psi_some_avg10=psi,
        pressure=pressure,
        budget_mb=budget,
    )

from __future__ import annotations

# Capability units form the shared vocabulary between the application descriptor
# and module coverage maps. They are abstract capabilities, not model names.
UNITS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "file-edit",
    "file-search",
    "clipboard",
    "doc-edit",
    "code-edit",
    "terminal",
    "test-run",
    "git",
    "browser-nav",
    "forms",
    "download",
    "search",
    "vision",
    "window-control",
    "planning",
    "cross-app",
    "data-table",
)

model_dumpr   )r'   rk   r€   ÚpÚapp_namer   Ú	file_pathrO   Úbase_dirÚpatternÚcmdrš   Úactualr›   s   &&            r   r^   ÚOrchestrator.execute_action_   sº  € Ø‰×#Ñ# D§I¡I¨t¯~©~Ô>Ø9‰9Ğ)Ô)Ø˜dŸl™l×:Ñ:Ó<Ğ=Ğ=Ø9‰9˜Ô$Ü˜Ÿ™×*Ñ*¨6°4×3FÑ3FÓGÓHˆDØ´F¸4¿<¹<»>Ô4JÓKÑ4J¨qœs 1§6¡6{Ñ4JÑKĞLĞLØ9‰9Ğ*Ô*Ü˜Ÿ™ vÕ.Ó/ˆDØJ‰J˜t¨dˆJÔ3ØĞ 2°4°&Ğ9Ğ:Ğ:Ø9‰9˜Ô$Ü˜Ÿ™ vÕ.Ó/ˆDØK‰K×'Ñ'¨¯	©	°4·>±>ÔBØK‰K×Ñ d°TĞÔ:ØO‰O˜DŸN™N×.Ñ.¨y¸"Ó=ÈˆOÔPØ  t f˜oĞ.Ğ.Ø9‰9˜Ô#Ü˜Ÿ™ vÕ.Ó/ˆDØ˜dŸn™n°g˜nÓ>Ğ?Ğ?Ø9‰9Ğ,Ô,Ø—~‘~×)Ñ)¨-¸Ó<ˆHØ—<‘<×2Ñ2°8¿^¹^×=OÑ=OĞPVĞXZÓ=[Ó\Ğ\Ø9‰9˜Ô$Ø—^‘^×'Ñ'¨°"Ó5ˆFÜ˜TŸ^™^×/Ñ/°¸×8KÑ8KÓLÓMˆIÜ—^’^ Y°°fĞ$=Ä3ÀyÃ>ĞbfĞmqĞ{}Ô~ˆFØ˜|¨V×->Ñ->ÀÈ&Ï-É-ĞYaĞci×cpÑcpĞqĞrĞrØ9‰9˜Ô#Ü˜DŸN™N×.Ñ.¨v°t×7JÑ7JÓKÓLˆHØ—n‘n×(Ñ(¨Ó8ˆGÚ3ˆCßØ—
‘
˜7Ô#Ü—^’^ C¬S°«]È4ĞVZĞdfÔgˆFØ˜|¨V×->Ñ->ÀÈ&Ï-É-ĞYaĞci×cpÑcpĞqĞrĞrØ9‰9˜Ô'Ø—~‘~×)Ñ)¨*°bÓ9ˆHØ—^‘^×'Ñ'¨°Ó3ˆFØŸ™×+Ñ+¨L¸*ÓEˆJØÔ0×@Ò@ÀĞS]Ó^×iÑiÓkĞlĞlØ9‰9Ğ*Ô*ØĞ1Ğ2Ğ2Ü#Ğ&:¸4¿9¹9¸+Ğ$FÓGĞGùòK Ls   Â:Rc               ó    € V ^8„  d   QhRRRR/# )r   rk   r   r,   r   r   )r   s   "r   r   r   Œ   s   € ÷ Yñ Y *ğ YĞ1Cñ Yr   c                	ó  € VP                   R 8X  d3   \        VP                  R,          4      p\        P                  ! V4      # VP                   R8X  Ed   \        VP                  P                  RV P                  4      4      pVP                  P                  R4      p. ROpV'       d   VP                  V4       \        P                  ! V\        V4      RR^<R7      p\        RRVP                   2VP                  ^ 8H  VP                  R,          VP                  R,          ,           VP                  ^ 8X  d   R	R

=== omni/orchestrator/engine.py ===
from __future__ import annotations
import json
import os
import subprocess
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from omni.adapters.desktop import DesktopAdapter
from omni.config import settings
from omni.planner.demo import DemoPlanner
from omni.safety.policy import SafetyPolicy
from omni.schemas.models import ActionPermission, ActionValidationError, StepStatus, TaskAction, TaskPlan, TaskRecord, VerificationResult
from omni.storage.sqlite_store import SQLiteStore
from omni.verification.engine import VerificationEngine


class Orchestrator:
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str | None = None, store: SQLiteStore | None = None):
        self.workspace_root = str(Path(workspace_root or settings.workspace_root).resolve())
        self.store = store or SQLiteStore(Path(self.workspace_root) / "omni_tasks.db")
        self.safety = SafetyPolicy(self.workspace_root)
        self.desktop = DesktopAdapter(self.workspace_root)
        self.planner = DemoPlanner(self.workspace_root, mode=settings.mode)
        self._tasks: dict[str, TaskRecord] = {}

    def create_task(self, goal: str) -> TaskRecord:
        import uuid
        task_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
        plan = self.planner.generate_plan(goal, task_id)
        task = TaskRecord(task_id=task_id, description=goal, mode=plan.mode, workspace=self.workspace_root, plan=plan, status="PENDING")
        self._tasks[task_id] = task
        self.store.save_task(task_id, goal, plan.mode.value, self.workspace_root, task.status)
        self.store.save_event(task_id, "plan_created", json.dumps({"step_count": len(plan.steps)}))
        return task

    def get_task(self, task_id: str) -> TaskRecord | None:
        return self._tasks.get(task_id)

    def run_plan(self, task_id: str) -> TaskRecord:
        task = self._tasks[task_id]
        task.status = "RUNNING"
        for step in task.plan.steps:
            if task.status == "CANCELLED":
                break
            step.status = StepStatus.READY
            if not self._dependencies_met(task.plan, step):
                step.status = StepStatus.BLOCKED
                continue
            if step.user_approval_required and not step.approved:
                step.status = StepStatus.BLOCKED
                task.status = "WAITING_FOR_APPROVAL"
                break
            step.status = StepStatus.RUNNING
            try:
                result = self.execute_action(step)
                step.result = result.get("result", str(result))
                step.status = StepStatus.VERIFYING
                verification = self.verify_action(step)
                step.verification = verification
                if verification.passed:
                    step.status = StepStatus.SUCCEEDED
                    self.store.save_event(task_id, "step_success", json.dumps({"step_id": step.id, "result": step.result}))
                else:
                    if step.max_retries_used < step.retry_limit:
                        step.max_retries_used += 1
                        step.status = StepStatus.RETRY_PENDING
                        self.store.save_event(task_id, "step_retry", json.dumps({"step_id": step.id, "reason": verification.failure_reason}))
                    else:
                        step.status = StepStatus.FAILED
                        task.status = "FAILED"
                        self.store.save_event(task_id, "step_failed", json.dumps({"step_id": step.id, "error": verification.failure_reason}))
                        break
            except Exception as exc:
                step.status = StepStatus.FAILED
                task.status = "FAILED"
                step.result = str(exc)
                self.store.save_event(task_id, "step_exception", json.dumps({"step_id": step.id, "error": str(exc)}))
                break
        if task.status not in {"FAILED", "CANCELLED", "WAITING_FOR_APPROVAL"}:
            task.status = "SUCCEEDED"
            task.final_report = self._build_final_report(task)
        self.store.save_task(task_id, task.description, task.mode.value, task.workspace, task.status, task.final_report)
        return task

    def _dependencies_met(self, plan: TaskPlan, step: TaskAction) -> bool:
        for dep_id in step.dependencies:
            dep_step = next((candidate for candidate in plan.steps if candidate.id == dep_id), None)
            if dep_step is None:
                raise ActionValidationError(f"Dependency {dep_id} does not exist for step {step.id}")
            if dep_step.status != StepStatus.SUCCEEDED:
                return False
        return True

    def execute_action(self, step: TaskAction) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self.safety.validate_action(step.name, step.arguments)
        if step.name == "inspect_desktop":
            return {"result": self.desktop.inspect_desktop()}
        if step.name == "list_files":
            path = Path(step.arguments.get("path", self.workspace_root))
            return {"result": [str(p.name) for p in sorted(path.iterdir())]}
        if step.name == "create_directory":
            path = Path(step.arguments["path"])
            path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            return {"result": f"created directory {path}"}
        if step.name == "write_file":
            path = Path(step.arguments["path"])
            self.safety.validate_action(step.name, step.arguments)
            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            path.write_text(step.arguments.get("content", ""), encoding="utf-8")
            return {"result": f"wrote {path}"}
        if step.name == "read_file":
            path = Path(step.arguments["path"])
            return {"result": path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")}
        if step.name == "launch_application":
            app_name = step.arguments.get("application", "")
            return self.desktop.launch_application(app_name, step.arguments.get("args", []))
        if step.name == "run_python":
            script = step.arguments.get("script", "")
            file_path = Path(step.arguments.get("path", self.workspace_root))
            result = subprocess.run(["python3", "-c", script], cwd=str(file_path), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
            return {"result": {"returncode": result.returncode, "stdout": result.stdout, "stderr": result.stderr}}
        if step.name == "run_tests":
            base_dir = Path(step.arguments.get("path", self.workspace_root))
            pattern = step.arguments.get("test_pattern")
            cmd = ["python3", "-m", "pytest", "-q"]
            if pattern:
                cmd.append(pattern)
            result = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(base_dir), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
            return {"result": {"returncode": result.returncode, "stdout": result.stdout, "stderr": result.stderr}}
        if step.name == "verify_output":
            expected = step.arguments.get("expected", "")
            actual = step.arguments.get("text", "")
            match_type = step.arguments.get("match_type", "contains")
            return {"result": VerificationEngine.output_contains(expected, actual, match_type).model_dump()}
        if step.name == "request_approval":
            return {"result": "approval recorded"}
        raise ActionValidationError(f"Unsupported action: {step.name}")

    def verify_action(self, step: TaskAction) -> VerificationResult:
        if step.name == "write_file":
            p = Path(step.arguments["path"])
            return VerificationEngine.file_exists(p)
        if step.name == "run_tests":
            base_dir = Path(step.arguments.get("path", self.workspace_root))
            pattern = step.arguments.get("test_pattern")
            cmd = ["python3", "-m", "pytest", "-q"]
            if pattern:
                cmd.append(pattern)
            proc = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(base_dir), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
            return VerificationResult(expected_condition="Pytest command passes", observed_condition=f"returncode={proc.returncode}", passed=proc.returncode == 0, evidence=(proc.stdout[:2000] + proc.stderr[:2000]), failure_reason=None if proc.returncode == 0 else proc.stderr or proc.stdout)
        if step.name == "verify_output":
            expected = step.arguments.get("expected", "")
            actual = step.arguments.get("text", "")
            match_type = step.arguments.get("match_type", "contains")
            return VerificationEngine.output_contains(expected, actual, match_type)
        if step.name == "create_directory":
            return VerificationEngine.file_exists(step.arguments['path'])
        if step.name == "inspect_desktop":
            info = self.desktop.inspect_desktop()
            return VerificationResult(expected_condition="Desktop metadata can be inspected", observed_condition=str(info), passed=True, evidence=str(info))
        return VerificationResult(expected_condition="No explicit verification", observed_condition="completed", passed=True, evidence=str(step.result))

    def _build_final_report(self, task: TaskRecord) -> str:
        successful = [s for s in task.plan.steps if s.status == StepStatus.SUCCEEDED]
        failed = [s for s in task.plan.steps if s.status == StepStatus.FAILED]
        return f"Task {task.task_id}: {len(successful)} successful steps, {len(failed)} failed steps."

from .demo import DemoPlanner, build_planner

=== omni/planner/demo.py ===
from __future__ import annotations
import re
from typing import Any

from omni.config import settings
from omni.schemas.models import ActionPermission, TaskAction, TaskMode, TaskPlan, StepStatus


class DemoPlanner:
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str | None = None, mode: str = "demo") -> None:
        self.workspace_root = workspace_root or str(settings.workspace_root)
        self.mode = mode

    def generate_plan(self, description: str, task_id: str) -> TaskPlan:
        text = (description or "").strip()
        if not text:
            raise ValueError("Task description is required.")
        is_demo = "create a python project" in text.lower() or "run its tests" in text.lower() or "detect" in text.lower() or "repair" in text.lower()
        steps: list[TaskAction] = []
        if is_demo:
            steps = [
                TaskAction(id="step-1", name="inspect_desktop", description="Inspect environment metadata and confirm the desktop is available.", arguments={}, dependencies=[], permission=ActionPermission.SAFE, timeout=10.0, retry_limit=1, user_approval_required=False),
                TaskAction(id="step-2", name="create_directory", description="Create a workspace directory for the demo project.", arguments={"path": self.workspace_root}, dependencies=["step-1"], permission=ActionPermission.MODERATE, timeout=10.0, retry_limit=1),
                TaskAction(id="step-3", name="write_file", description="Write the project source file.", arguments={"path": f"{self.workspace_root}/demo_app.py", "content": """def fibonacci(n):
    if n < 2:
        return n
    return fibonacci(n - 1) + fibonacci(n - 2)
"""}, dependencies=["step-2"], permission=ActionPermission.MODERATE, timeout=10.0, retry_limit=1),
                TaskAction(id="step-4", name="write_file", description="Write a test file that initially fails on purpose.", arguments={"path": f"{self.workspace_root}/test_demo_app.py", "content": """from demo_app import fibonacci


def test_fibonacci():
    assert fibonacci(5) == 10
"""}, dependencies=["step-3"], permission=ActionPermission.MODERATE, timeout=10.0, retry_limit=1),
                TaskAction(id="step-5", name="run_tests", description="Run the demo tests to capture the failing evidence.", arguments={"path": self.workspace_root, "test_pattern": "test_demo_app.py"}, dependencies=["step-4"], permission=ActionPermission.MODERATE, timeout=30.0, retry_limit=1),
                TaskAction(id="step-6", name="verify_output", description="Confirm the test failure is present and record the output.", arguments={"expected": "AssertionError|assert fibonacci\(5\) == 10", "text": "", "match_type": "regex"}, dependencies=["step-5"], permission=ActionPermission.SAFE, timeout=10.0, retry_limit=1),
                TaskAction(id="step-7", name="write_file", description="Repair the faulty implementation in the demo project.", arguments={"path": f"{self.workspace_root}/demo_app.py", "content": """def fibonacci(n):
    if n < 0:
        raise ValueError('n must be non-negative')
    if n < 2:
        return n
    a, b = 0, 1
    for _ in range(2, n + 1):
        a, b = b, a + b
    return b
"""}, dependencies=["step-6"], permission=ActionPermission.MODERATE, timeout=10.0, retry_limit=1, user_approval_required=True, approved=True),
                TaskAction(id="step-8", name="run_tests", description="Verify the repaired implementation passes the tests.", arguments={"path": self.workspace_root, "test_pattern": "test_demo_app.py"}, dependencies=["step-7"], permission=ActionPermission.MODERATE, timeout=30.0, retry_limit=1),
                TaskAction(id="step-9", name="verify_output", description="Confirm the final test evidence passes.", arguments={"expected": "1 passed|passed", "text": "", "match_type": "regex"}, dependencies=["step-8"], permission=ActionPermission.SAFE, timeout=10.0, retry_limit=1),
            ]
        else:
            steps = [
                TaskAction(id="action-1", name="list_files", description="Inspect the workspace root.", arguments={"path": self.workspace_root}, dependencies=[], permission=ActionPermission.SAFE, timeout=10.0),
                TaskAction(id="action-2", name="write_file", description="Create a file based on the task.", arguments={"path": f"{self.workspace_root}/result.txt", "content": f"""Task received: {text}
"""}, dependencies=["action-1"], permission=ActionPermission.MODERATE, timeout=10.0),
            ]
        return TaskPlan(task_id=task_id, description=text, mode=TaskMode.DEMO if self.mode == "demo" else TaskMode.LOCAL_MODEL, steps=steps)


def build_planner(mode: str = "demo", workspace_root: str | None = None) -> DemoPlanner:
    return DemoPlanner(workspace_root=workspace_root, mode=mode)

=== omni/routing/__init__.py ===
from omni.routing.agents import DEFAULT_AGENTS, AgentSpec
from omni.routing.iars import IARS, BayesianLinearModel, RoutingDecision
from omni.routing.service import RoutingService
from omni.routing.verifier import HeuristicVerifier

__all__ = ["DEFAULT_AGENTS", "AgentSpec", "IARS", "BayesianLinearModel", "RoutingDecision", "RoutingService", "HeuristicVerifier"]

=== omni/routing/agents.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AgentSpec:
    id: str
    name: str
    description: str
    tags: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    # Design-time constants (not measurements): used only as a routing penalty.
    cost: float = 0.10


# Capability descriptions are the only input to the prior embedding. Users never
# select an agent; IARS explores and learns from implicit feedback.
DEFAULT_AGENTS: tuple[AgentSpec, ...] = (
    AgentSpec(
        id="file-ops",
        name="File Operator",
        description="Create, rename, move, copy, delete, list and inspect files and directories on disk. Work with paths, archives, permissions and workspace cleanup.",
        tags=("files", "file", "filesystem", "paths", "workspace", "list", "nautilus", "thunar", "dolphin", "nemo", "pcmanfm"),
        cost=0.05,
    ),
    AgentSpec(
        id="document-editor",
        name="Document Editor",
        description="Read and edit text documents, notes and markdown. Insert, replace and reflow paragraphs while preserving structure and avoiding unrelated edits.",
        tags=("document", "editor", "text", "markdown", "writing", "writer", "gedit", "obsidian", "notion", "readme", "libreoffice", "pdf"),
        cost=0.12,
    ),
    AgentSpec(
        id="dev-terminal",
        name="Terminal Developer",
        description="Run terminal commands, inspect processes, build and test code, use git and package managers, and debug command-line failures in a development workspace.",
        tags=("terminal", "shell", "bash", "zsh", "alacritty", "kitty", "foot", "konsole", "wezterm", "code", "vscode", "codium", "build", "test", "pytest", "git", "python", "routing", "py"),
        cost=0.18,
    ),
    AgentSpec(
        id="browser-ops",
        name="Browser Operator",
        description="Inspect and change browser state: tabs, navigation, page text, forms and downloads. Verify URL or page content after interacting with a web application.",
        tags=("browser", "chrome", "chromium", "firefox", "brave", "edge", "web", "url", "tabs", "forms", "form", "link", "page", "inbox", "mail", "youtube", "video"),
        cost=0.22,
    ),
    AgentSpec(
        id="visual-ui",
        name="Visual UI Agent",
        description="Understand what is visible on screen from low-resolution frames and window geometry. Click, scroll and verify visual layout when text and accessibility data are insufficient.",
        tags=("screen", "screenshot", "visual", "image", "ui", "geometry", "button", "window", "mpv", "vlc", "imv", "eog", "gimp", "video"),
        cost=0.30,
    ),
    AgentSpec(
        id="planner",
        name="Multi-step Planner",
        description="Decompose an ambiguous cross-app goal into ordered steps, choose tools and hand off sub-tasks. Useful for multi-step workflows and long-running requests.",
        tags=("plan", "workflow", "cross", "cross-app", "reasoning", "handoff", "multi", "goal", "steps", "tools"),
        cost=0.25,
    ),
)

=== omni/routing/iars.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Iterable

import numpy as np

from omni.context.features import FEATURE_DIM, prior_vector
from omni.llm.embeddings import HashEmbedder
from omni.routing.agents import DEFAULT_AGENTS, AgentSpec
from omni.routing.verifier import HeuristicVerifier


class BayesianLinearModel:
    """Ridge Bayesian linear model with a Sherman-Morrison precision update.

    A_inv is the posterior covariance. b is the precision-weighted reward vector,
    so the posterior mean is A_inv @ b. Sampling uses a Cholesky factor of A_inv.
    """

    def __init__(self, dim: int, ridge: float = 1.0, noise: float = 1.0, prior_mean: np.ndarray | None = None) -> None:
        self.dim = dim
        self.ridge = float(ridge)
        self.noise = float(noise)
        self.A_inv = np.eye(dim, dtype=np.float64) / self.ridge
        self.b = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
        if prior_mean is not None:
            prior = np.asarray(prior_mean, dtype=np.float64).reshape(dim)
            self.b = self.ridge * prior
        self.n_updates = 0

    @property
    def mean(self) -> np.ndarray:
        return self.A_inv @ self.b

    @property
    def covariance(self) -> np.ndarray:
        return self.A_inv

    def sample(self, rng: np.random.Generator) -> np.ndarray:
        mean = self.mean
        try:
            chol = np.linalg.cholesky(self.A_inv + 1e-12 * np.eye(self.dim))
        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:
            chol = np.diag(np.sqrt(np.maximum(np.diag(self.A_inv), 1e-12)))
        return mean + chol @ rng.standard_normal(self.dim)

    def update(self, phi: np.ndarray, reward: float, weight: float = 1.0) -> None:
        x = np.asarray(phi, dtype=np.float64).reshape(self.dim)
        w = float(weight) / self.noise
        a_inv_x = self.A_inv @ x
        denom = 1.0 + w * float(x @ a_inv_x)
        if denom <= 0:
            return
        self.A_inv -= w * np.outer(a_inv_x, a_inv_x) / denom
        self.b += w * float(reward) * x
        self.n_updates += 1


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RoutingDecision:
    agent_id: str
    agent_name: str
    confidence: float
    scores: dict[str, float]
    explored: bool
    candidates: tuple[str, ...]
    reason: str
    verification: str
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    context_app: str = ""

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "agent": self.agent_id,
            "name": self.agent_name,
            "confidence": round(self.confidence, 4),
            "scores": {k: round(v, 4) for k, v in self.scores.items()},
            "explored": self.explored,
            "candidates": list(self.candidates),
            "reason": self.reason,
            "verification": self.verification,
            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
            "context_app": self.context_app,
        }


class IARS:
    """Implicit intent routing with per-agent Thompson sampling."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        agents: Iterable[AgentSpec] = DEFAULT_AGENTS,
        dim: int = FEATURE_DIM,
        tau: float = 0.55,
        lambda_cost: float = 0.10,
        lambda_load: float = 0.20,
        ridge: float = 64.0,
        noise: float = 0.1,
        seed: int = 7,
        embedder: HashEmbedder | None = None,
        verifier: HeuristicVerifier | None = None,
        prior_vectors: dict[str, np.ndarray] | None = None,
        confidence_temperature: float = 0.35,
        prior_strength: float = 8.0,
    ) -> None:
        self.agents = tuple(agents)
        if not self.agents:
            raise ValueError("IARS needs at least one agent")
        self.dim = int(dim)
        self.tau = float(tau)
        self.lambda_cost = float(lambda_cost)
        self.lambda_load = float(lambda_load)
        self.confidence_temperature = float(confidence_temperature)
        self.prior_strength = float(prior_strength)
        self.embedder = embedder
        self.verifier = verifier or HeuristicVerifier()
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.models: dict[str, BayesianLinearModel] = {}
        for spec in self.agents:
            if prior_vectors and spec.id in prior_vectors:
                prior = np.asarray(prior_vectors[spec.id], dtype=np.float64)
                if prior.shape != (self.dim,):
                    raise ValueError(f"prior for {spec.id} must have shape ({self.dim},)")
            elif self.embedder is not None:
                prior = np.asarray(self.embedder.embed(" ".join((spec.description, *spec.tags))), dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                prior = prior_vector(" ".join((spec.description, *spec.tags)), self.dim) * self.prior_strength
            self.models[spec.id] = BayesianLinearModel(dim=self.dim, ridge=ridge, noise=noise, prior_mean=prior)

    def agent(self, agent_id: str) -> AgentSpec:
        for spec in self.agents:
            if spec.id == agent_id:
                return spec
        raise KeyError(agent_id)

    def scores(self, phi: np.ndarray, load_by_agent: dict[str, float] | None = None) -> dict[str, float]:
        x = np.asarray(phi, dtype=np.float64).reshape(self.dim)
        loads = load_by_agent or {}
        out: dict[str, float] = {}
        for spec in self.agents:
            theta = self.models[spec.id].sample(self.rng)
            out[spec.id] = float(theta @ x) - self.lambda_cost * spec.cost - self.lambda_load * float(loads.get(spec.id, 0.0))
        return out

    def _confidence(self, scores: dict[str, float]) -> tuple[str, float]:
        ids = list(scores)
        values = np.array([scores[i] for i in ids], dtype=np.float64)
        temp = max(self.confidence_temperature, 1e-6)
        shifted = (values - values.max()) / temp
        probs = np.exp(shifted)
        probs /= probs.sum()
        top = int(np.argmax(probs))
        return ids[top], float(probs[top])

    def decide(self, phi: np.ndarray, context_text: str = "", load_by_agent: dict[str, float] | None = None, tau: float | None = None) -> RoutingDecision:
        scores = self.scores(phi, load_by_agent)
        ranked = sorted(scores, key=scores.get, reverse=True)
        top_id, confidence = self._confidence(scores)
        assert top_id == ranked[0]
        threshold = self.tau if tau is None else float(tau)
        chosen = top_id
        explored = False
        candidates: tuple[str, ...] = (top_id,)
        verification = "single"
        if confidence < threshold and len(ranked) > 1:
            candidates = (ranked[0], ranked[1])
            chosen = self.verifier.choose(context_text, [self.agent(candidates[0]), self.agent(candidates[1])], scores)
            explored = True
            verification = "heuristic-lexical"
            reason = f"confidence {confidence:.2f} < tau {threshold:.2f}; ran top-2; verifier chose {chosen}"
        else:
            reason = f"highest sampled utility (confidence {confidence:.2f} >= tau {threshold:.2f})"
        spec = self.agent(chosen)
        return RoutingDecision(
            agent_id=chosen,
            agent_name=spec.name,
            confidence=confidence,
            scores=scores,
            explored=explored,
            candidates=candidates,
            reason=reason,
            verification=verification,
            context_app="",
        )

    def observe(self, phi: np.ndarray, agent_id: str, reward: float, weight: float = 1.0) -> None:
        self.models[agent_id].update(phi, reward, weight=weight)

    def observe_many(self, phi: np.ndarray, outcomes: dict[str, float], weight: float = 1.0) -> None:
        for agent_id, reward in outcomes.items():
            self.observe(phi, agent_id, reward, weight=weight)

    @staticmethod
    def implicit_reward(
        accepted: bool,
        reverted: bool = False,
        undo_count: int = 0,
        edit_distance: float = 0.0,
        max_edit_distance: float = 1.0,
        latency_s: float = 0.0,
        latency_budget_s: float = 30.0,
        beta_undo: float = 0.5,
        beta_edit: float = 0.5,
        beta_latency: float = 0.2,
    ) -> float:
        """Implicit reward from observable post-action signals (no user labels)."""
        accepted_flag = 1.0 if accepted and not reverted else 0.0
        edit_norm = min(max(float(edit_distance) / max(float(max_edit_distance), 1e-9), 0.0), 1.0)
        latency_norm = min(max(float(latency_s) / max(float(latency_budget_s), 1e-9), 0.0), 1.0)
        return accepted_flag - beta_undo * float(max(undo_count, 0)) - beta_edit * edit_norm - beta_latency * latency_norm

=== omni/routing/service.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from omni.context.features import build_features
from omni.context.hyprland import HyprlandContextCollector
from omni.hud.state import default_state_path, write_hud_state
from omni.modules.ampl import AMPL, ModuleResidency
from omni.modules.descriptor import AppDescriptor
from omni.modules.memory import measure_memory
from omni.routing.iars import IARS
from omni.telemetry import JsonlLogger


class RoutingService:
    """Collects context, routes once, and publishes a HUD state file + JSONL log."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        collector: HyprlandContextCollector | None = None,
        iars: IARS | None = None,
        hud_path: str | Path | None = None,
        log_path: str | Path | None = None,
        mock: bool | None = None,
        ampl: AMPL | None = None,
        residency: ModuleResidency | None = None,
        scheduler: Any | None = None,
        scheduler_apply: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        self.collector = collector or HyprlandContextCollector()
        self.iars = iars or IARS()
        self.hud_path = Path(hud_path) if hud_path else default_state_path()
        self.log_path = Path(log_path) if log_path else Path(os.environ.get("OMNI_ROUTING_LOG", Path.home() / ".cache" / "omni" / "routing" / "decisions.jsonl"))
        self.logger = JsonlLogger(self.log_path, mock=mock)
        self.ampl = ampl
        self.residency = residency or (ModuleResidency(ampl.library) if ampl else None)
        if self.ampl and self.ampl.logger is None:
            self.ampl.logger = JsonlLogger(self.log_path.parent / "modules.jsonl", mock=self.logger.mock)
        self.scheduler = scheduler
        self.scheduler_apply = bool(scheduler_apply)
        self.recent_actions: dict[str, int] = {}

    def record_action(self, action: str) -> None:
        self.recent_actions[action] = self.recent_actions.get(action, 0) + 1

    def step(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        snapshot = self.collector.snapshot()
        phi = build_features(snapshot)
        decision = self.iars.decide(phi, context_text=f"{snapshot.app_class} {snapshot.title}")
        routing = decision.as_dict()
        routing["context_app"] = snapshot.app_class
        modules: dict[str, Any] = {"status": "pending A3 (AMPL)", "resident": [], "resident_mb": 0.0, "budget_mb": None}
        memory_state = None
        if self.ampl and self.residency is not None:
            memory_state = measure_memory(self.ampl.max_budget_mb)
            descriptor = AppDescriptor.from_snapshot(snapshot, self.recent_actions)
            plan = self.ampl.select(descriptor, memory_state.budget_mb, self.residency.ids)
            self.residency.apply(plan)
            modules = {"status": "active", **plan.as_dict(), "memory": memory_state.as_dict()}
        scheduler_state: dict[str, Any] = {"status": "disabled", "policy": "default", "decisions": []}
        if self.scheduler is not None:
            scheduler_state = {"status": "active", **self.scheduler.step(snapshot, apply=self.scheduler_apply)}
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "schema": 1,
            "updated": time.time(),
            "mock": self.logger.mock,
            "context": snapshot.as_dict(),
            "privacy": snapshot.privacy_summary(),
            "routing": routing,
            "modules": modules,
            "scheduler": scheduler_state,
        }
        write_hud_state(self.hud_path, payload)
        log_fields: dict[str, Any] = {
            "agent": decision.agent_id,
            "confidence": round(decision.confidence, 4),
            "explored": decision.explored,
            "candidates": list(decision.candidates),
            "scores": {k: round(v, 4) for k, v in decision.scores.items()},
            "app": snapshot.app_class,
            "workspace": snapshot.workspace_id,
            "features_dim": int(phi.shape[0]),
            "modules_resident": list(modules.get("resident", [])),
            "module_mb": modules.get("resident_mb", 0.0),
            "module_budget_mb": modules.get("budget_mb"),
            "scheduler_action": scheduler_state.get("action"),
            "scheduler_policy": scheduler_state.get("policy"),
            "mock": self.logger.mock,
        }
        if memory_state is not None:
            log_fields["mem_pressure"] = round(memory_state.pressure, 4)
            log_fields["mem_available_mb"] = round(memory_state.mem_available_mb, 1)
        self.logger.log("routing_decision", **log_fields)
        return payload

    def run(self, once: bool = False, interval: float = 1.0, max_steps: int | None = None) -> None:
        self.collector.start()
        steps = 0
        try:
            while True:
                self.step()
                steps += 1
                if once or (max_steps is not None and steps >= max_steps):
                    break
                time.sleep(max(interval, 0.1))
        finally:
            self.collector.close()

=== omni/routing/verifier.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import re

from omni.routing.agents import AgentSpec

_TOKEN = re.compile(r"[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_+.-]*")


class HeuristicVerifier:
    """Cheap local top-2 verifier: lexical affinity + model score, no LLM call."""

    def choose(self, context_text: str, candidates: list[AgentSpec], scores: dict[str, float]) -> str:
        context = set(_TOKEN.findall(context_text.lower()))
        best_id = candidates[0].id
        best_value = -math.inf
        for spec in candidates:
            words = set(_TOKEN.findall(" ".join((spec.description, *spec.tags)).lower()))
            overlap = len(context & words) / math.sqrt(max(len(context) * len(words), 1))
            value = overlap + 0.01 * scores.get(spec.id, 0.0)
            if value > best_value:
                best_value = value
                best_id = spec.id
        return best_id

=== omni/safety/policy.py ===
from __future__ import annotations
import os
from pathlib import Path

from omni.schemas.models import ActionValidationError, ActionPermission


ALLOWED_ACTIONS = {
    "inspect_desktop",
    "list_files",
    "create_directory",
    "write_file",
    "read_file",
    "launch_application",
    "run_python",
    "run_tests",
    "verify_output",
    "request_approval",
}

ALLOWED_APPLICATIONS = {"firefox", "code", "python3", "gnome-terminal", "alacritty"}


class SafetyPolicy:
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str | Path):
        self.workspace_root = Path(workspace_root).resolve()

    def validate_action(self, action_name: str, arguments: dict | None = None) -> dict:
        args = arguments or {}
        if action_name not in ALLOWED_ACTIONS:
            raise ActionValidationError(f"Unknown action: {action_name}")
        if action_name in {"create_directory", "write_file", "read_file", "run_python", "run_tests", "verify_output", "launch_application"}:
            path = args.get("path")
            if path:
                self._validate_path(path)
        return args

    def _validate_path(self, candidate: str | Path) -> None:
        candidate_path = Path(candidate).expanduser().resolve() if str(candidate) else Path('.')
        workspace_root = self.workspace_root.resolve()
        try:
            candidate_path.relative_to(workspace_root)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise ActionValidationError(f"Path escape attempt: {candidate_path} is outside workspace {workspace_root}") from exc
        if candidate_path.is_symlink():
            raise ActionValidationError(f"Symlink escape attempt: {candidate_path}")

    def allow_write(self, path: str | Path, overwrite: bool = False) -> None:
        target = Path(path).resolve()
        if target.exists() and not overwrite:
            raise ActionValidationError(f"Overwrite refused for existing path: {target}")

    def validate_application(self, app_name: str) -> str:
        if app_name not in ALLOWED_APPLICATIONS:
            raise ActionValidationError(f"Application not allowed: {app_name}")
        return app_name

    def check_output_limit(self, output: str, limit: int = 20000) -> str:
        if len(output) > limit:
            raise ActionValidationError(f"Output exceeds limit of {limit} characters")
        return output

    def permission_for(self, action_name: str) -> ActionPermission:
        mapping = {
            "inspect_desktop": ActionPermission.SAFE,
            "list_files": ActionPermission.SAFE,
            "create_directory": ActionPermission.MODERATE,
            "write_file": ActionPermission.MODERATE,
            "read_file": ActionPermission.SAFE,
            "launch_application": ActionPermission.CONSEQUENTIAL,
            "run_python": ActionPermission.MODERATE,
            "run_tests": ActionPermission.MODERATE,
            "verify_output": ActionPermission.SAFE,
            "request_approval": ActionPermission.SAFE,
        }
        return mapping.get(action_name, ActionPermission.SAFE)
