=== omni/scheduler/actuator.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from omni.scheduler.policy import ScheduleAction


def _detect_cgroup_base() -> Path | None:
    """Find a writable cgroup v2 directory that exposes the cpu controller."""
    override = os.environ.get("OMNI_CGROUP_ROOT")
    if override:
        path = Path(override)
        return path if path.exists() else None
    try:
        relative = Path("/proc/self/cgroup").read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip().split(":")[-1]
    except OSError:
        return None
    current = Path("/sys/fs/cgroup") / relative.lstrip("/")
    while current != Path("/sys/fs/cgroup") and current != current.parent:
        try:
            controllers = (current / "cgroup.controllers").read_text(encoding="utf-8").split()
            delegated = (current / "cgroup.subtree_control").read_text(encoding="utf-8").split()
        except OSError:
            controllers, delegated = [], []
        # A base is useful only if it both exposes cpu and delegates it to
        # children, otherwise a child group has no cpu.weight file.
        if "cpu" in controllers and "cpu" in delegated and os.access(current, os.W_OK):
            return current
        current = current.parent
    return None


@dataclass
class CgroupV2Actuator:
    """CPU/IO scheduling actuation through cgroup v2; dry-run unless requested.

    Never elevates privileges, never loads kernel modules, and never moves
    processes unless a pid is explicitly supplied. ``restore`` is the kill switch.
    """

    base: Path | None = None
    group: str = "omni-sched"
    state_path: Path | None = None
    allow_renice: bool = False
    originals: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        self.base = Path(self.base) if self.base is not None else _detect_cgroup_base()
        self.state_path = Path(self.state_path) if self.state_path else Path(
            os.environ.get("OMNI_SCHEDULER_STATE", Path.home() / ".cache" / "omni" / "scheduler" / "actuator.json")
        )
        self._load_state()

    @property
    def group_path(self) -> Path | None:
        return (self.base / self.group) if self.base is not None else None

    def available(self) -> bool:
        return self.base is not None and self.base.exists() and os.access(self.base, os.W_OK)

    def status(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "backend": "cgroup-v2",
            "available": self.available(),
            "base": str(self.base) if self.base else None,
            "group": str(self.group_path) if self.group_path else None,
            "tracked_files": len(self.originals),
            "kill_switch": True,
        }

    def _load_state(self) -> None:
        if not self.state_path or not self.state_path.exists():
            return
        try:
            payload = json.loads(self.state_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            self.originals = {str(k): str(v) for k, v in payload.get("originals", {}).items()}
        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
            self.originals = {}

    def _save_state(self) -> None:
        if not self.state_path:
            return
        self.state_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.state_path.write_text(
            json.dumps({"updated": time.time(), "originals": self.originals}, indent=2),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )

    def _write(self, path: Path, value: str) -> None:
        key = str(path)
        if key not in self.originals:
            try:
                self.originals[key] = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            except OSError:
                self.originals[key] = "100\n"
        path.write_text(value, encoding="utf-8")

    def _weight_text(self, path: Path, value: int) -> str:
        return f"default {value}\n" if path.name == "io.weight" else f"{value}\n"

    def apply(self, action: ScheduleAction, pid: int | None = None, dry_run: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if dry_run:
            return {
                "applied": False,
                "dry_run": True,
                "backend": "cgroup-v2",
                "reason": "dry-run (pass --apply to actuate)",
                "would_write": {"cpu.weight": action.cpu_weight, "io.weight": action.io_weight},
            }
        if not self.available() or self.group_path is None:
            return {
                "applied": False,
                "dry_run": False,
                "backend": "cgroup-v2",
                "reason": "no writable delegated cgroup v2 with cpu controller",
            }
        written: dict[str, Any] = {}
        try:
            self.group_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            for name, value in (("cpu.weight", action.cpu_weight), ("io.weight", action.io_weight)):
                path = self.group_path / name
                if path.exists():
                    self._write(path, self._weight_text(path, value))
                    written[name] = value
            if pid is not None:
                procs = self.group_path / "cgroup.procs"
                if not procs.exists():
                    raise OSError("cgroup.procs missing")
                procs.write_text(f"{int(pid)}\n", encoding="utf-8")
                written["pid"] = int(pid)
            self._save_state()
        except OSError as exc:
            return {"applied": False, "dry_run": False, "backend": "cgroup-v2", "reason": f"cgroup write failed: {exc}"}
        if pid is not None and self.allow_renice:
            written["renice"] = self.apply_nice(pid, action, dry_run=False)
        return {
            "applied": bool(written),
            "dry_run": False,
            "backend": "cgroup-v2",
            "group": str(self.group_path),
            "written": written,
        }

    def nice_commands(self, pid: int, action: ScheduleAction) -> list[list[str]]:
        commands: list[list[str]] = []
        if shutil.which("renice"):
            commands.append(["renice", "-n", str(action.nice), "-p", str(int(pid))])
        if shutil.which("ionice"):
            commands.append(["ionice", "-c", "2", "-n", "4", "-p", str(int(pid))])
        return commands

    def apply_nice(self, pid: int, action: ScheduleAction, dry_run: bool = True) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for command in self.nice_commands(pid, action):
            if dry_run:
                results.append({"command": command, "dry_run": True})
                continue
            try:
                proc = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=3, check=False)
                results.append({"command": command, "returncode": proc.returncode})
            except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
                results.append({"command": command, "error": str(exc)})
        return results

    def restore(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Kill switch: restore every touched weight file to its original value."""
        restored: dict[str, str] = {}
        errors: list[str] = []
        for key, original in list(self.originals.items()):
            path = Path(key)
            try:
                path.write_text(original, encoding="utf-8")
                restored[key] = original.strip()
            except OSError as exc:
                errors.append(f"{key}: {exc}")
        group_path = self.group_path
        if group_path is not None:
            for name in ("cpu.weight", "io.weight"):
                path = group_path / name
                if path.exists() and str(path) not in self.originals:
                    try:
                        path.write_text(self._weight_text(path, 100), encoding="utf-8")
                        restored[str(path)] = "100"
                    except OSError as exc:
                        errors.append(f"{path}: {exc}")
        self.originals.clear()
        if self.state_path and self.state_path.exists():
            try:
                self.state_path.unlink()
            except OSError as exc:
                errors.append(f"{self.state_path}: {exc}")
        return {"restored": restored, "errors": errors, "kill_switch": "restored"}

=== omni/scheduler/context.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from omni.context.features import ContextSnapshot
from omni.modules.memory import MemoryState, measure_memory

# Hand-authored interactivity prior by app class. This is a design prior used
# only when the KRLS model has no observations; it is not a measurement.
_INTERACTIVE_PATTERNS = (
    "chrome", "chromium", "firefox", "brave", "edge", "browser",
    "code", "codium", "vscode", "editor", "writer", "obsidian", "notion",
    "terminal", "alacritty", "kitty", "foot", "konsole", "wezterm",
    "nautilus", "thunar", "dolphin", "nemo", "slack", "discord",
)

FEATURE_NAMES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "load_norm",
    "cpu_pressure",
    "mem_pressure",
    "client_norm",
    "interactive",
    "focus_age_norm",
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ScheduleFeatures:
    load_norm: float
    cpu_pressure: float
    mem_pressure: float
    client_norm: float
    interactive: float
    focus_age_norm: float

    def as_array(self):
        import numpy as np

        return np.array(
            [
                self.load_norm,
                self.cpu_pressure,
                self.mem_pressure,
                self.client_norm,
                self.interactive,
                self.focus_age_norm,
            ],
            dtype=np.float64,
        )

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, float]:
        return {name: round(float(value), 4) for name, value in zip(FEATURE_NAMES, self.as_array())}


def _read_loadavg(path: Path) -> float:
    try:
        return float(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").split()[0])
    except (OSError, ValueError, IndexError):
        return 0.0


def _read_psi_some(path: Path) -> float | None:
    try:
        for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            if line.startswith("some "):
                for field in line.split():
                    if field.startswith("avg10="):
                        return float(field.split("=", 1)[1])
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None
    return None


def is_interactive_app(app_class: str) -> bool:
    app = (app_class or "").lower()
    return any(pattern in app for pattern in _INTERACTIVE_PATTERNS)


def collect_features(
    snapshot: ContextSnapshot,
    memory: MemoryState | None = None,
    loadavg_path: Path | None = None,
    cpu_psi_path: Path | None = None,
    cpu_count: int | None = None,
) -> ScheduleFeatures:
    """Read real load/PSI/memory and combine with the focused-window context."""
    cpus = max(int(cpu_count if cpu_count is not None else (os.cpu_count() or 1)), 1)
    load_norm = min(1.0, max(0.0, _read_loadavg(loadavg_path or Path("/proc/loadavg")) / cpus))
    psi = _read_psi_some(cpu_psi_path or Path("/proc/pressure/cpu"))
    cpu_pressure = min(1.0, max(0.0, (psi or 0.0) / 50.0))
    memory = memory or measure_memory()
    client_norm = min(1.0, max(0.0, float(snapshot.client_count) / 12.0))
    interactive = 1.0 if is_interactive_app(snapshot.app_class) else 0.0
    focus_age_norm = min(1.0, max(0.0, math.log1p(max(snapshot.focused_age_s, 0.0)) / 5.0))
    return ScheduleFeatures(
        load_norm=load_norm,
        cpu_pressure=cpu_pressure,
        mem_pressure=min(1.0, max(0.0, float(memory.pressure))),
        client_norm=client_norm,
        interactive=interactive,
        focus_age_norm=focus_age_norm,
    )

=== omni/scheduler/krls.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import numpy as np


@dataclass
class KRLS:
    """Kernel Recursive Least Squares with ALD sparsification.

    Online, deterministic regressor: RBF kernel, regularised kernel inverse
    maintained with a block Sherman-Morrison update. The dictionary is capped by
    ``budget``; once full, samples update coefficients without adding centres.
    """

    dim: int
    gamma: float = 1.0
    ridge: float = 0.05
    budget: int = 64
    ald_threshold: float = 1e-3
    centers: list[np.ndarray] = field(default_factory=list)
    alpha: np.ndarray = field(default_factory=lambda: np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64))
    targets: np.ndarray = field(default_factory=lambda: np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64))
    inv: np.ndarray = field(default_factory=lambda: np.zeros((0, 0), dtype=np.float64))
    n_updates: int = 0

    def _kernel(self, a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:
        diff = a - b
        return float(np.exp(-self.gamma * float(diff @ diff)))

    def _kernel_row(self, x: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        if not self.centers:
            return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
        return np.array([self._kernel(x, c) for c in self.centers], dtype=np.float64)

    def predict(self, x: np.ndarray) -> float:
        if not self.centers:
            return 0.0
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(self.dim)
        return float(self._kernel_row(x) @ self.alpha)

    def novelty(self, x: np.ndarray) -> float:
        if not self.centers:
            return 1.0
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(self.dim)
        k = self._kernel_row(x)
        return float(1.0 - k @ self.inv @ k)

    def update(self, x: np.ndarray, y: float) -> None:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(self.dim)
        y = float(y)
        k = self._kernel_row(x)
        if not self.centers:
            self.centers.append(x.copy())
            self.inv = np.array([[1.0 / (1.0 + self.ridge)]], dtype=np.float64)
            self.targets = np.array([y], dtype=np.float64)
            self.alpha = self.inv @ self.targets
            self.n_updates += 1
            return

        ald = self.novelty(x)
        if len(self.centers) < self.budget and ald > self.ald_threshold:
            a = self.inv @ k
            denom = 1.0 + self.ridge - float(k @ a)
            denom = max(denom, 1e-12)
            m = len(self.centers)
            expanded = np.zeros((m + 1, m + 1), dtype=np.float64)
            expanded[:m, :m] = self.inv + np.outer(a, a) / denom
            expanded[:m, m] = -a / denom
            expanded[m, :m] = -a / denom
            expanded[m, m] = 1.0 / denom
            self.inv = expanded
            self.centers.append(x.copy())
            self.targets = np.append(self.targets, y)
            self.alpha = self.inv @ self.targets
        else:
            pred = float(k @ self.alpha)
            a = self.inv @ k
            self.alpha = self.alpha + (y - pred) * a / (1.0 + self.ridge)
        self.n_updates += 1

    def fit(self, xs: np.ndarray, ys: np.ndarray) -> "KRLS":
        for x, y in zip(np.asarray(xs), np.asarray(ys)):
            self.update(x, float(y))
        return self

    def state(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "dim": self.dim,
            "gamma": self.gamma,
            "ridge": self.ridge,
            "budget": self.budget,
            "centers": len(self.centers),
            "updates": self.n_updates,
        }

=== omni/scheduler/policy.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from omni.scheduler.context import FEATURE_NAMES, ScheduleFeatures
from omni.scheduler.krls import KRLS


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ScheduleAction:
    id: str
    label: str
    cpu_weight: int
    io_weight: int
    nice: int
    description: str

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "label": self.label,
            "cpu_weight": self.cpu_weight,
            "io_weight": self.io_weight,
            "nice": self.nice,
        }


ACTIONS: tuple[ScheduleAction, ...] = (
    ScheduleAction("boost", "Interactive boost", 300, 300, 0, "Favour the focused interactive app."),
    ScheduleAction("balanced", "Balanced", 100, 100, 0, "Default CPU/IO share."),
    ScheduleAction("throttle", "Background throttle", 35, 40, 0, "Keep background work from starving the foreground."),
)
ACTIONS_BY_ID: dict[str, ScheduleAction] = {action.id: action for action in ACTIONS}


def heuristic_scores(features: ScheduleFeatures) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Design-prior scores used until the KRLS models have observations.

    STUB: the live service has no reward signal yet, so it reports
    ``policy="heuristic-prior"`` until Phase 4 bench/actuation feeds outcomes.
    """
    x = features
    boost = 0.55 + 0.9 * x.interactive - 0.55 * x.load_norm - 0.7 * x.mem_pressure - 0.15 * x.client_norm
    balanced = 0.35 + 0.2 * x.interactive - 0.25 * abs(x.load_norm - 0.35) - 0.2 * x.mem_pressure
    throttle = 0.20 + 0.7 * (1.0 - x.interactive) + 0.5 * x.load_norm + 0.35 * x.mem_pressure
    return {"boost": boost, "balanced": balanced, "throttle": throttle}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SchedulerDecision:
    action: str
    label: str
    policy: str
    scores: dict[str, float]
    features: dict[str, float]
    reason: str
    cpu_weight: int
    io_weight: int
    nice: int
    updates: int

    def as_dict(self, applied: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "action": self.action,
            "label": self.label,
            "policy": self.policy,
            "scores": {key: round(value, 4) for key, value in self.scores.items()},
            "features": self.features,
            "reason": self.reason,
            "cpu_weight": self.cpu_weight,
            "io_weight": self.io_weight,
            "nice": self.nice,
            "updates": self.updates,
        }
        if applied is not None:
            payload["applied"] = applied
        return payload


class KRLSPolicy:
    """Contextual scheduler: one KRLS reward model per candidate action."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        gamma: float = 1.5,
        ridge: float = 0.05,
        budget: int = 48,
        ald_threshold: float = 0.02,
        actions: tuple[ScheduleAction, ...] = ACTIONS,
    ) -> None:
        self.actions = tuple(actions)
        self.models = {
            action.id: KRLS(
                dim=len(FEATURE_NAMES),
                gamma=gamma,
                ridge=ridge,
                budget=budget,
                ald_threshold=ald_threshold,
            )
            for action in self.actions
        }

    @property
    def updates(self) -> int:
        return sum(model.n_updates for model in self.models.values())

    def scores(self, features: ScheduleFeatures) -> dict[str, float]:
        x = features.as_array()
        return {action.id: float(self.models[action.id].predict(x)) for action in self.actions}

    def decide(self, features: ScheduleFeatures) -> SchedulerDecision:
        if self.updates == 0:
            scores = heuristic_scores(features)
            policy = "heuristic-prior"
        else:
            scores = self.scores(features)
            policy = "krls"
        action_id = max(scores, key=scores.get)
        action = ACTIONS_BY_ID[action_id]
        return SchedulerDecision(
            action=action.id,
            label=action.label,
            policy=policy,
            scores=scores,
            features=features.as_dict(),
            reason=f"{action.description} (predicted reward {scores[action_id]:.3f})",
            cpu_weight=action.cpu_weight,
            io_weight=action.io_weight,
            nice=action.nice,
            updates=self.updates,
        )

    def update(self, features: ScheduleFeatures, action_id: str, reward: float) -> None:
        if action_id not in self.models:
            raise KeyError(action_id)
        self.models[action_id].update(features.as_array(), float(reward))

    def state(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "updates": self.updates,
            "models": {action.id: self.models[action.id].state() for action in self.actions},
        }

=== omni/scheduler/service.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from omni.context.features import ContextSnapshot
from omni.modules.memory import measure_memory
from omni.scheduler.actuator import CgroupV2Actuator
from omni.scheduler.context import ScheduleFeatures, collect_features
from omni.scheduler.policy import ACTIONS_BY_ID, KRLSPolicy
from omni.telemetry import JsonlLogger


class SchedulerService:
    """Context features -> KRLS/heuristic decision -> optional cgroup actuation."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        policy: KRLSPolicy | None = None,
        actuator: CgroupV2Actuator | None = None,
        apply: bool = False,
        logger: JsonlLogger | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.policy = policy or KRLSPolicy()
        self.actuator = actuator or CgroupV2Actuator()
        self.apply = bool(apply)
        self.logger = logger

    def step(self, snapshot: ContextSnapshot, apply: bool | None = None, pid: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        memory = measure_memory()
        features = collect_features(snapshot, memory)
        decision = self.policy.decide(features)
        action = ACTIONS_BY_ID[decision.action]
        actuate = self.apply if apply is None else bool(apply)
        applied = self.actuator.apply(action, pid=pid, dry_run=not actuate)
        payload = decision.as_dict(applied=applied)
        payload["actuator"] = self.actuator.status()
        payload["memory"] = memory.as_dict()
        if self.logger:
            self.logger.log(
                "scheduler_decision",
                action=decision.action,
                policy=decision.policy,
                scores={key: round(value, 4) for key, value in decision.scores.items()},
                features=decision.features,
                applied=applied,
                mock=self.logger.mock,
            )
        return payload

    def observe(self, features: ScheduleFeatures, action_id: str, reward: float) -> None:
        self.policy.update(features, action_id, reward)

    def restore(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.actuator.restore()

=== omni/scheduler/simulator.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

import numpy as np

from omni.scheduler.context import ScheduleFeatures
from omni.scheduler.policy import ACTIONS, KRLSPolicy

SIMULATED_NOTE = "SIMULATED: synthetic reward model for policy tests, not a measurement."


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Scenario:
    id: str
    name: str
    interactive: tuple[float, float]
    load: tuple[float, float]
    memory: tuple[float, float]
    clients: tuple[float, float]
    focus_age: tuple[float, float] = (0.55, 0.12)


SCENARIOS: dict[str, Scenario] = {
    "S1": Scenario("S1", "interactive foreground vs background work", (0.90, 0.03), (0.55, 0.12), (0.15, 0.05), (0.50, 0.10)),
    "S2": Scenario("S2", "memory pressure with background batch", (0.15, 0.05), (0.80, 0.10), (0.75, 0.08), (0.80, 0.10)),
    "S3": Scenario("S3", "idle / low-load mixed use", (0.50, 0.05), (0.20, 0.08), (0.20, 0.05), (0.20, 0.10)),
}


def _clip(value: float) -> float:
    return float(min(1.0, max(0.0, value)))


def sample_state(rng: np.random.Generator, scenario: Scenario) -> ScheduleFeatures:
    def draw(pair: tuple[float, float]) -> float:
        return _clip(rng.normal(pair[0], pair[1]))

    return ScheduleFeatures(
        load_norm=draw(scenario.load),
        cpu_pressure=draw((scenario.load[0] * 0.6, 0.08)),
        mem_pressure=draw(scenario.memory),
        client_norm=draw(scenario.clients),
        interactive=draw(scenario.interactive),
        focus_age_norm=draw(scenario.focus_age),
    )


def true_rewards(features: ScheduleFeatures) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Synthetic reward model. Explicitly simulated, never reported as data."""
    x = features
    boost = 1.0 * x.interactive - 0.5 * x.load_norm - 0.8 * x.mem_pressure + 0.2
    balanced = 0.45 - 0.2 * abs(x.load_norm - 0.35) - 0.2 * x.mem_pressure + 0.1 * x.interactive
    throttle = 0.8 * (1.0 - x.interactive) + 0.5 * x.load_norm + 0.3 * x.mem_pressure - 0.5 * x.interactive
    return {"boost": boost, "balanced": balanced, "throttle": throttle}


def simulate(
    scenario_id: str,
    steps: int = 400,
    test_steps: int = 120,
    seed: int = 0,
    epsilon_start: float = 0.35,
    epsilon_end: float = 0.05,
    noise: float = 0.02,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    scenario = SCENARIOS[scenario_id]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    policy = KRLSPolicy()
    action_counts: dict[str, int] = {action.id: 0 for action in ACTIONS}

    for step in range(max(steps, 1)):
        features = sample_state(rng, scenario)
        rewards = true_rewards(features)
        epsilon = epsilon_start + (epsilon_end - epsilon_start) * (step / max(steps - 1, 1))
        if rng.random() < epsilon:
            action_id = str(rng.choice([action.id for action in ACTIONS]))
        else:
            action_id = policy.decide(features).action
        reward = rewards[action_id] + float(rng.normal(0.0, noise))
        policy.update(features, action_id, reward)
        action_counts[action_id] += 1

    learned: list[float] = []
    baseline: list[float] = []
    oracle: list[float] = []
    correct = 0
    chosen_counts: dict[str, int] = {action.id: 0 for action in ACTIONS}
    for _ in range(max(test_steps, 1)):
        features = sample_state(rng, scenario)
        rewards = true_rewards(features)
        decision = policy.decide(features)
        learned.append(rewards[decision.action])
        baseline.append(rewards["balanced"])
        best = max(rewards, key=rewards.get)
        oracle.append(rewards[best])
        correct += int(decision.action == best)
        chosen_counts[decision.action] += 1

    tests = max(test_steps, 1)
    return {
        "scenario": scenario_id,
        "name": scenario.name,
        "simulated": True,
        "steps": steps,
        "test_steps": test_steps,
        "seed": seed,
        "updates": policy.updates,
        "train_actions": action_counts,
        "eval_actions": chosen_counts,
        "mean_reward": round(float(np.mean(learned)), 4),
        "balanced_reward": round(float(np.mean(baseline)), 4),
        "oracle_reward": round(float(np.mean(oracle)), 4),
        "improvement_over_balanced": round(float(np.mean(learned) - np.mean(baseline)), 4),
        "oracle_accuracy": round(correct / tests, 4),
        "note": SIMULATED_NOTE,
    }


def simulate_all(steps: int = 400, test_steps: int = 120, seed: int = 0) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "simulated": True,
        "note": SIMULATED_NOTE,
        "scenarios": {sid: simulate(sid, steps=steps, test_steps=test_steps, seed=seed + index) for index, sid in enumerate(SCENARIOS)},
    }

=== omni/schemas/models.py ===
from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator


class ActionPermission(str, Enum):
    SAFE = "safe"
    MODERATE = "moderate"
    CONSEQUENTIAL = "consequential"


class StepStatus(str, Enum):
    PENDING = "PENDING"
    READY = "READY"
    RUNNING = "RUNNING"
    VERIFYING = "VERIFYING"
    SUCCEEDED = "SUCCEEDED"
    FAILED = "FAILED"
    RETRY_PENDING = "RETRY_PENDING"
    CANCELLED = "CANCELLED"
    BLOCKED = "BLOCKED"


class TaskMode(str, Enum):
    DEMO = "demo"
    LOCAL_MODEL = "local_model"


class VerificationResult(BaseModel):
    expected_condition: str
    observed_condition: str
    passed: bool
    evidence: str = ""
    timestamp: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    failure_reason: str | None = None


class TaskAction(BaseModel):
    id: str
    name: str
    description: str
    arguments: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    dependencies: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    preconditions: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    expected_postconditions: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    timeout: float = 30.0
    retry_limit: int = 1
    max_retries_used: int = 0
    permission: ActionPermission = ActionPermission.SAFE
    status: StepStatus = StepStatus.PENDING
    result: str | None = None
    verification: VerificationResult | None = None
    user_approval_required: bool = False
    approved: bool = False
    created_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    updated_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))

    @field_validator("name")
    @classmethod
    def validate_name(cls, v: str) -> str:
        if not v or not v.strip():
            raise ValueError("Action name is required.")
        return v


class TaskPlan(BaseModel):
    task_id: str
    description: str
    mode: TaskMode = TaskMode.DEMO
    steps: list[TaskAction]


class TaskRecord(BaseModel):
    task_id: str
    description: str
    mode: TaskMode = TaskMode.DEMO
    workspace: str
    status: str = "PENDING"
    plan: TaskPlan | None = None
    created_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    updated_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    final_report: str | None = None


class ProviderError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, message: str, *, provider: str | None = None):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.provider = provider


class ActionValidationError(ValueError):
    pass

=== omni/storage/sqlite_store.py ===
from __future__ import annotations
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path


class SQLiteStore:
    def __init__(self, db_path: str | Path = "./omni_data.db"):
        self.db_path = str(db_path)
        self._init_db()

    def _init_db(self) -> None:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        conn.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasks (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, description TEXT, mode TEXT, workspace TEXT, status TEXT, final_report TEXT, created_at TEXT, updated_at TEXT)")
        conn.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, task_id TEXT, event_type TEXT, payload TEXT, created_at TEXT)")
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

    def save_task(self, task_id: str, description: str, mode: str, workspace: str, status: str, final_report: str | None = None) -> None:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO tasks (id, description, mode, workspace, status, final_report, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'), datetime('now')) ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET description=excluded.description, mode=excluded.mode, workspace=excluded.workspace, status=excluded.status, final_report=excluded.final_report, updated_at=datetime('now')", (task_id, description, mode, workspace, status, final_report))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

    def save_event(self, task_id: str, event_type: str, payload: str) -> None:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO events (task_id, event_type, payload, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))", (task_id, event_type, payload))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

    def list_tasks(self) -> list[dict]:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        rows = conn.execute("SELECT id, description, mode, workspace, status, final_report, created_at, updated_at FROM tasks ORDER BY created_at DESC").fetchall()
        conn.close()
        return [dict(zip(["id", "description", "mode", "workspace", "status", "final_report", "created_at", "updated_at"], row)) for row in rows]

=== omni/telemetry.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any


def mock_enabled() -> bool:
    """True when the process is running in mock-LLM mode."""
    return os.environ.get("OMNI_LLM_MOCK", "").lower() in {"1", "true", "yes"} or os.environ.get("OMNI_LLM_BACKEND", "").lower() == "mock"


class JsonlLogger:
    """Append-only JSONL logger. Every record carries an explicit mock flag."""

    def __init__(self, path: str | Path, mock: bool | None = None) -> None:
        self.path = Path(path)
        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.mock = mock_enabled() if mock is None else mock

    def log(self, event: str, **fields: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        record: dict[str, Any] = {"ts": time.time(), "event": event, **fields}
        record["mock"] = bool(fields.get("mock", self.mock))
        with self.path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            fh.write(json.dumps(record, sort_keys=True) + "\n")
        return record

=== omni/tools/discovery.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import time
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

DESKTOP_DIRS = [
    Path("/usr/share/applications"),
    Path(os.path.expanduser("~/.local/share/applications")),
    Path(os.path.expanduser("~/.config/autostart")),
]

# Destructive / high-risk operations that always require explicit confirmation.
DESTRUCTIVE_KEYWORDS = (
    "rm", "delete", "trash", "uninstall", "erase", "wipe", "format",
    "send", "pay", "purchase", "install", "upgrade", "sudo", "su",
    "kill", "pkill", "reboot", "poweroff", "shutdown", "post", "deploy",
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ToolSpec:
    id: str                          # stable: "<source>:<name>"
    name: str
    kind: str                        # desktop | executable | dbus | atspi | hyprland | file | mcp | browser
    category: str                    # documents | code | web | system | media | files | communication | general
    description: str = ""
    params: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)   # JSON schema-ish input schema
    executable: str | None = None
    source: str = ""
    safety_tier: str = "read-only"   # read-only | reversible | destructive
    requires_confirmation: bool = False
    available: bool = True
    discovered_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        d = asdict(self)
        return d

    @property
    def destructive(self) -> bool:
        return self.safety_tier == "destructive" or self.requires_confirmation


def _tier(name: str, description: str) -> str:
    blob = f"{name} {description}".lower()
    if any(k in blob for k in DESTRUCTIVE_KEYWORDS):
        return "destructive"
    return "read-only"


def _categorize(name: str, description: str, exe: str | None) -> str:
    blob = f"{name} {description} {exe or ''}".lower()
    tables = {
        "code": ("code", "git", "vim", "nvim", "python", "node", "rustc", "cargo", "gcc", "make", "docker", "podman"),
        "web": ("firefox", "chrome", "chromium", "browser", "curl", "wget", "lynx"),
        "communication": ("thunderbird", "mail", "signal", "telegram", "slack", "discord", "matrix", "irc"),
        "media": ("mpv", "vlc", "gimp", "inkscape", "audacity", "obs", "ffmpeg", "image", "video", "audio"),
        "files": ("nautilus", "dolphin", "thunar", "pcmanfm", "caja", "explorer", "files"),
        "system": ("hyprctl", "systemctl", "nmcli", "pacman", "apt", "dnf", "grim", "slurp", "wtype", "ydotool"),
        "documents": ("libreoffice", "writer", "calc", "impress", "okular", "evince", "pdf", "docx", "odt"),
    }
    for category, keys in tables.items():
        if any(k in blob for k in keys):
            return category
    return "general"


def _run(cmd: list[str], timeout: float = 5.0) -> str:
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout, check=False)
        return proc.stdout
    except Exception:
        return ""


class ToolRegistry:
    """Typed tool registry assembled from runtime discovery. Refreshed periodically."""

    def __init__(self, ttl_seconds: float = 300.0) -> None:
        self.ttl_seconds = ttl_seconds
        self.tools: dict[str, ToolSpec] = {}
        self.last_refresh: float = 0.0

    def refresh(self, force: bool = False) -> dict[str, int]:
        now = time.time()
        if not force and (now - self.last_refresh) < self.ttl_seconds and self.tools:
            return {k: 1 for k in self.tools}
        discovered = discover_all()
        self.tools = {t.id: t for t in discovered}
        self.last_refresh = now
        return {source: sum(1 for t in discovered if t.kind == source) for source in
                {"desktop", "executable", "dbus", "atspi", "hyprland", "file", "mcp", "browser"}}

    def __iter__(self):
        return iter(self.tools.values())

    def get(self, tool_id: str) -> ToolSpec | None:
        return self.tools.get(tool_id)

    def by_category(self, category: str) -> list[ToolSpec]:
        return [t for t in self.tools.values() if t.category == category]

    def destructive(self) -> list[ToolSpec]:
        return [t for t in self.tools.values() if t.destructive]

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {tool_id: t.as_dict() for tool_id, t in self.tools.items()}


# ----- discovery sources -----


def _tool(tool_id, name, kind, category, description, executable=None, source="", tier="read-only",
          confirm=False, available=True, params=None):
    return ToolSpec(
        id=tool_id,
        name=name,
        kind=kind,
        category=category,
        description=description,
        executable=executable,
        source=source,
        params=params or {"type": "object"},
        safety_tier=tier,
        requires_confirmation=confirm,
        available=available,
    )


def _desktop_entries() -> list[ToolSpec]:
    tools: list[ToolSpec] = []
    for ddir in DESKTOP_DIRS:
        if not ddir.exists():
            continue
        for desktop in sorted(ddir.glob("*.desktop")):
            try:
                text = desktop.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
            except OSError:
                continue
            entry: dict[str, str] = {}
            current = ""
            for line in text.splitlines():
                line = line.strip()
                if not line or line.startswith("#"):
                    continue
                if line.startswith("[") and line.endswith("]"):
                    current = line[1:-1]
                    continue
                if "=" in line and current == "Desktop Entry":
                    key, _, value = line.partition("=")
                    entry[key.strip()] = value.strip()
            if entry.get("NoDisplay", "false").lower() == "true":
                continue
            name = entry.get("Name") or desktop.stem
            exec_cmd = entry.get("Exec", "")
            exe = exec_cmd.split()[0] if exec_cmd else ""
            comment = entry.get("Comment", "")
            tools.append(
                _tool(
                    tool_id=f"desktop:{desktop.stem}",
                    name=name,
                    kind="desktop",
                    category=_categorize(name, comment, exe),
                    description=comment,
                    executable=exe,
                    source=str(desktop),
                    tier=_tier(name, comment),
                    confirm=_tier(name, comment) == "destructive",
                    available=shutil.which(exe) is not None if exe else True,
                    params={"type": "object", "properties": {"desktop_file": {"type": "string", "default": str(desktop)}}},
                )
            )
    return tools


def _path_executables(limit: int = 200) -> list[ToolSpec]:
    tools: list[ToolSpec] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    path_dirs = [p for p in os.environ.get("PATH", "").split(os.pathsep) if p]
    count = 0
    for ddir in path_dirs:
        pdir = Path(ddir)
        if not pdir.is_dir():
            continue
        for entry in sorted(pdir.iterdir()):
            if count >= limit:
                break
            if not entry.is_file() or not os.access(entry, os.X_OK):
                continue
            name = entry.name
            if name in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(name)
            count += 1
            help_text = _run([str(entry), "--help"], timeout=2.0)
            desc = ""
            for line in help_text.splitlines()[:5]:
                if line.strip():
                    desc = line.strip()
                    break
            tools.append(
                _tool(
                    tool_id=f"executable:{name}",
                    name=name,
                    kind="executable",
                    category=_categorize(name, desc, str(entry)),
                    description=desc,
                    executable=str(entry),
                    source="PATH",
                    tier=_tier(name, desc),
                    confirm=_tier(name, desc) == "destructive",
                    params={"type": "object", "properties": {"args": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}}},
                )
            )
        if count >= limit:
            break
    return tools


def _dbus_services() -> list[ToolSpec]:
    tools: list[ToolSpec] = []
    if not shutil.which("dbus-send") and not shutil.which("busctl"):
        return tools
    for bus in ("session", "system"):
        out = _run(["busctl", "--" + bus, "list"], timeout=5.0) if shutil.which("busctl") else ""
        for line in out.splitlines():
            parts = line.split()
            if not parts:
                continue
            name = parts[0]
            if name.startswith(":") or "." not in name:
                continue
            category = "communication"
            if name.startswith("org.mpris"):
                category = "media"
            elif name.startswith("org.freedesktop.Notifications"):
                category = "communication"
            elif name.startswith("org.freedesktop.NetworkManager") or name.startswith("org.freedesktop.systemd1"):
                category = "system"
            tier = "reversible" if name.startswith(("org.freedesktop.systemd1", "org.freedesktop.NetworkManager")) else "read-only"
            tools.append(
                _tool(
                    tool_id=f"dbus:{bus}:{name}",
                    name=name,
                    kind="dbus",
                    category=category,
                    description=f"D-Bus {bus} service {name}",
                    source=f"dbus {bus}",
                    tier=tier,
                    params={"type": "object", "properties": {"bus": {"type": "string"}, "destination": {"type": "string"}, "path": {"type": "string"}, "interface": {"type": "string"}, "member": {"type": "string"}}},
                )
            )
    return tools


def _atspi_apps() -> list[ToolSpec]:
    tools: list[ToolSpec] = []
    if not shutil.which("gdbus"):
        return tools
    out = _run(
        ["gdbus", "introspect", "--session", "--dest", "org.a11y.Bus", "--object-path", "/org/a11y/bus"],
        timeout=5.0,
    )
    if "RegisterApplication" not in out:
        return tools
    tools.append(
        _tool(
            tool_id="atspi:bus",
            name="AT-SPI bus",
            kind="atspi",
            category="general",
            description="AT-SPI accessibility tree (gdbus fallback; no PyAT-SPI)",
            source="atspi",
            params={"type": "object", "properties": {"application": {"type": "string"}}},
        )
    )
    return tools


def _hyprland_tools() -> list[ToolSpec]:
    tools: list[ToolSpec] = []
    if not shutil.which("hyprctl"):
        return tools
    for action, desc in (
        ("activewindow", "focus active window"),
        ("clients", "list all windows"),
        ("workspaces", "list workspaces"),
        ("dispatch", "dispatch a Hyprland command"),
    ):
        tools.append(
            _tool(
                tool_id=f"hyprland:{action}",
                name=f"hyprctl {action}",
                kind="hyprland",
                category="system",
                description=desc,
                source="hyprland ipc",
                tier="read-only" if action in ("activewindow", "clients", "workspaces") else "reversible",
                confirm=action == "dispatch",
                params={"type": "object", "properties": {"command": {"type": "string"}}},
            )
        )
    return tools


def _file_tools() -> list[ToolSpec]:
    return [
        _tool(
            tool_id="file:read", name="read file", kind="file", category="files", description="read a text file",
            source="builtin",
            params={"type": "object", "properties": {"path": {"type": "string"}}, "required": ["path"]},
        ),
        _tool(
            tool_id="file:write", name="write file", kind="file", category="files", description="write a text file",
            source="builtin",
            params={"type": "object", "properties": {"path": {"type": "string"}, "content": {"type": "string"}}, "required": ["path", "content"]},
            tier="reversible",
        ),
        _tool(
            tool_id="file:delete", name="delete file", kind="file", category="files", description="delete a file (destructive)",
            source="builtin",
            params={"type": "object", "properties": {"path": {"type": "string"}}, "required": ["path"]},
            tier="destructive",
            confirm=True,
        ),
    ]


def _mcp_servers() -> list[ToolSpec]:
    tools: list[ToolSpec] = []
    config = Path(os.path.expanduser("~/.config/omni/mcp.json"))
    if not config.exists():
        return tools
    try:
        servers = json.loads(config.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("servers", {})
    except Exception:
        return tools
    for name, spec in servers.items():
        tools.append(
            _tool(
                tool_id=f"mcp:{name}", name=name, kind="mcp", category="general",
                description=str(spec.get("description", "MCP server " + name)),
                source="mcp config",
                tier="reversible",
                available=bool(spec.get("command")),
                params={"type": "object", "properties": {"server": {"type": "string", "default": name}, "method": {"type": "string"}, "params": {"type": "object"}}},
            )
        )
    return tools


def _browser_tools() -> list[ToolSpec]:
    tools: list[ToolSpec] = []
    if shutil.which("chromium") or shutil.which("google-chrome") or shutil.which("firefox"):
        tools.append(
            _tool(
                tool_id="browser:cdp", name="browser CDP/Playwright", kind="browser", category="web",
                description="browser automation via Chrome DevTools Protocol / Playwright",
                source="builtin",
                tier="reversible",
                params={"type": "object", "properties": {"url": {"type": "string"}, "action": {"type": "string"}}},
            )
        )
    return tools


def discover_all() -> list[ToolSpec]:
    """Discover every runtime capability source. Each source degrades gracefully."""
    all_tools: list[ToolSpec] = []
    for collector in (
        _desktop_entries,
        _path_executables,
        _dbus_services,
        _atspi_apps,
        _hyprland_tools,
        _file_tools,
        _mcp_servers,
        _browser_tools,
    ):
        try:
            all_tools.extend(collector())
        except Exception:
            continue
    seen: set[str] = set()
    ordered: list[ToolSpec] = []
    for t in all_tools:
        if t.id in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(t.id)
        ordered.append(t)
    return ordered

=== omni/verification/engine.py ===
from __future__ import annotations
import re
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from omni.schemas.models import VerificationResult


class VerificationEngine:
    @staticmethod
    def file_exists(path: str | Path) -> VerificationResult:
        obj = Path(path)
        return VerificationResult(expected_condition=f"File exists: {obj}", observed_condition="exists" if obj.exists() else "missing", passed=obj.exists(), evidence=str(obj.exists()), failure_reason=None if obj.exists() else "file missing")

    @staticmethod
    def output_contains(expected: str, actual: str, match_type: str = "contains") -> VerificationResult:
        if match_type == "regex":
            passed = bool(re.search(expected, actual, flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.MULTILINE))
            observed = actual[:2000]
            return VerificationResult(expected_condition=f"Regex match for: {expected}", observed_condition=observed, passed=passed, evidence=observed, failure_reason=None if passed else "regex did not match")
        passed = expected.lower() in actual.lower()
        return VerificationResult(expected_condition=f"Contains: {expected}", observed_condition=actual[:2000], passed=passed, evidence=actual[:2000], failure_reason=None if passed else "expected output missing")

    @staticmethod
    def python_exit_ok(command: list[str], cwd: str | Path) -> VerificationResult:
        proc = subprocess.run(command, cwd=str(cwd), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
        return VerificationResult(expected_condition="Python command exited successfully", observed_condition=f"returncode={proc.returncode}", passed=proc.returncode == 0, evidence=(proc.stdout[:1500] + proc.stderr[:1500]), failure_reason=None if proc.returncode == 0 else proc.stderr or proc.stdout)

    @staticmethod
    def pytest_ok(path: str | Path, pattern: str | None = None) -> VerificationResult:
        target = Path(path)
        if not target.exists():
            return VerificationResult(expected_condition="Tests directory or file exists", observed_condition="missing", passed=False, evidence=str(target), failure_reason="target missing")
        cmd = ["python3", "-m", "pytest", "-q"]
        if pattern:
            cmd.extend([str(pattern)])
        proc = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(target), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
        return VerificationResult(expected_condition="Pytest exits successfully", observed_condition=f"returncode={proc.returncode}", passed=proc.returncode == 0, evidence=(proc.stdout[:2500] + proc.stderr[:2500]), failure_reason=None if proc.returncode == 0 else proc.stderr or proc.stdout)

=== tests/test_api.py ===
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from omni.api.app import app


def test_health_endpoint():
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        response = client.get('/health')
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()['status'] == 'ok'


def test_task_submission():
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        response = client.post('/tasks', json={'goal': 'Create a demo project.'})
    assert response.status_code == 200
    data = response.json()
    assert 'task_id' in data
    assert len(data['plan']) > 0

=== tests/test_bench.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json

from bench.adapters import MockAdapter, get_adapter
from bench.report import build_report
from bench.runner import BenchRunner, load_records
from bench.tasks import TASKS, TASKS_BY_ID, materialise, select_tasks


def test_task_suite_is_well_formed() -> None:
    assert len(TASKS) == 30
    assert len(TASKS_BY_ID) == 30
    assert {task.category for task in TASKS} == {"files", "documents", "code", "data", "terminal", "planning"}
    for task in TASKS:
        assert task.verify is not None, task.id
        assert task.fixture is not None, task.id
        assert task.timeout_s > 0, task.id
        assert task.goal.strip(), task.id


def test_materialise_writes_declared_files(tmp_path) -> None:
    task = TASKS_BY_ID["files-rename"]
    materialise(task, tmp_path)
    assert (tmp_path / "draft.txt").read_text() == "draft content\n"


def test_mock_runner_three_task_smoke(tmp_path) -> None:
    results = tmp_path / "results"
    workspaces = tmp_path / "workspaces"
    runner = BenchRunner(results, workspaces)
    records = runner.run(select_tasks("files-create-notes,files-rename,files-move"), MockAdapter())

    assert [record["status"] for record in records] == ["success", "success", "success"]
    assert all(record["mock"] is True for record in records)
    assert all(record["cached_replay"] is False for record in records)
    assert len(load_records(results)) == 3


def test_report_excludes_mock_and_lists_missing(tmp_path) -> None:
    results = tmp_path / "results"
    runner = BenchRunner(results, tmp_path / "workspaces")
    runner.run(select_tasks("files-create-notes,files-rename,files-move"), MockAdapter())

    records = load_records(results)
    task_ids = ["files-create-notes", "files-rename", "files-move"]
    report = build_report(records, task_ids, ["opencode"], [0], results)

    assert report["records_total"] == 3
    assert report["real_records"] == 0
    assert report["excluded"]["mock"] == 3
    assert report["figures"] == []
    assert report["figure_status"].startswith("not generated")
    assert len(report["missing_runs"]) == 3
    assert (results / "report.json").exists()
    assert (results / "report.md").exists()


def test_report_generates_figures_for_complete_real_matrix(tmp_path) -> None:
    task_ids = ["files-create-notes", "files-rename"]
    records = [
        {
            "schema": 1,
            "run_id": f"{task_id}--opencode--s0",
            "task_id": task_id,
            "category": TASKS_BY_ID[task_id].category,
            "adapter": "opencode",
            "seed": 0,
            "mock": False,
            "cached_replay": False,
            "status": "success",
            "duration_s": 12.5,
            "verification": "ok",
        }
        for task_id in task_ids
    ]
    results = tmp_path / "results"
    report = build_report(records, task_ids, ["opencode"], [0], results)

    assert report["real_records"] == 2
    assert report["missing_runs"] == []
    assert report["figure_status"] == "generated"
    assert len(report["figures"]) == 4
    for path in report["figures"]:
        assert (results / "figures" / path.split("/")[-1]).exists()


def test_report_withholds_figures_when_a_real_run_is_missing(tmp_path) -> None:
    task_ids = ["files-create-notes", "files-rename"]
    records = [
        {
            "run_id": "files-create-notes--opencode--s0",
            "task_id": "files-create-notes",
            "category": "files",
            "adapter": "opencode",
            "seed": 0,
            "mock": False,
            "cached_replay": False,
            "status": "success",
            "duration_s": 3.0,
            "verification": "ok",
        }
    ]
    report = build_report(records, task_ids, ["opencode"], [0], tmp_path / "results")

    assert report["figures"] == []
    assert report["missing_runs"] == [{"task_id": "files-rename", "adapter": "opencode", "seed": 0}]
    assert "1 missing run" in report["figure_status"]

    try:
        get_adapter("does-not-exist")
    except KeyError:
        return
    raise AssertionError("unknown adapter should raise KeyError")


def test_bench_cli_smoke_writes_jsonl(tmp_path) -> None:
    from omni.main import main

    results = tmp_path / "cli_results"
    code = main(
        [
            "bench",
            "--smoke",
            "--adapter",
            "mock",
            "--results",
            str(results),
            "--workspaces",
            str(tmp_path / "cli_ws"),
        ]
    )
    assert code == 0
    rows = [json.loads(line) for line in (results / "runs.jsonl").read_text().splitlines() if line]
    assert len(rows) == 3
    assert all(row["mock"] for row in rows)

=== tests/test_context_features.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess

import numpy as np

from omni.context.atspi import AtspiCollector
from omni.context.features import FEATURE_DIM, build_features, synthetic_snapshot
from omni.context.hyprland import HyprlandContextCollector, HyprlandEventStream


def completed(stdout: str, code: int = 0) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    return subprocess.CompletedProcess(args=[], returncode=code, stdout=stdout, stderr="")


def test_event_stream_parses_and_tracks_focus() -> None:
    events = HyprlandEventStream(instance_signature="test", runtime_dir="/tmp")
    assert HyprlandEventStream.parse("activewindow>>firefox,Title") == ("activewindow", "firefox,Title")
    assert HyprlandEventStream.parse("garbage") is None
    events.handle_line("activewindow>>code,main.py")
    assert events.last_event["event"] == "activewindow"
    assert events.event_counts["activewindow"] == 1
    assert events.focused_age_s < 1.0


def test_collector_snapshot_and_features() -> None:
    def runner(cmd: list[str], timeout: float) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
        if cmd[:1] != ["hyprctl"]:
            return completed("")
        what = cmd[1]
        if what == "activewindow":
            return completed(json.dumps({"class": "code", "title": "routing.py", "workspace": {"id": 2}}))
        if what == "activeworkspace":
            return completed(json.dumps({"id": 2, "name": "2"}))
        if what == "clients":
            return completed(json.dumps([{"class": "code"}, {"class": "firefox"}]))
        if what == "monitors":
            return completed(json.dumps([{"name": "eDP-1"}]))
        return completed("null")

    events = HyprlandEventStream(instance_signature="test", runtime_dir="/tmp")
    collector = HyprlandContextCollector(runner=runner, event_stream=events, atspi=None, screenshot=False)
    snapshot = collector.snapshot()
    assert snapshot.app_class == "code"
    assert snapshot.workspace_id == 2
    assert snapshot.client_count == 2
    assert snapshot.monitor_count == 1
    phi = build_features(snapshot)
    assert phi.shape == (FEATURE_DIM,)
    assert np.isfinite(phi).all()
    assert np.allclose(phi, build_features(snapshot))
    assert phi[0] == 1.0
    other = build_features(synthetic_snapshot("firefox"))
    assert not np.allclose(phi, other)
    privacy = snapshot.privacy_summary()
    assert privacy["raw_keys_stored"] is False
    assert privacy["clipboard_content_stored"] is False
    assert "clipboard" not in json.dumps(privacy).replace("clipboard_type_count", "").replace("clipboard_content_stored", "")


def test_atspi_gdbus_fallback_histogram() -> None:
    def runner(cmd: list[str], timeout: float) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
        joined = " ".join(cmd)
        if "org.a11y.Bus.GetAddress" in joined:
            return completed("('unix:path=/tmp/atspi-bus',)\n")
        if "org.a11y.atspi.Accessible.GetChildren" in joined and "org.a11y.atspi.Registry" in joined:
            return completed("([(':1.1', objectpath '/org/a11y/atspi/accessible/root'),],)\n")
        if "org.a11y.atspi.Accessible.GetChildren" in joined:
            return completed("([(':1.1', '/child/1'), (':1.1', '/child/2')],)\n")
        if "org.a11y.atspi.Accessible.GetRoleName" in joined:
            return completed("('push button',)\n")
        return completed("", code=1)

    result = AtspiCollector(runner=runner, max_nodes=8).collect()
    assert result["available"] is True
    assert result["roles"].get("push button", 0) >= 1
    assert result["source"] == "gdbus"

=== tests/test_gateway.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from omni.gateway import DEFAULT_CAPABILITY, ModelGateway, ROLES
from omni.model_registry import ModelRegistry, ModelRecord, get_api_key, provider_headers


def _record(**overrides):
    base = dict(
        model_id="prov/model-a",
        provider="prov",
        context_length=4096,
        modalities=frozenset({"text"}),
        tools=True,
        price_input_per_1k=0.5,
        price_output_per_1k=1.5,
        open_weights="yes",
        latency_ms=100.0,
        error_rate=0.1,
        successes=9,
        failures=1,
        last_checked=1000.0,
        source="prov",
    )
    base.update(overrides)
    return ModelRecord(**base)


def test_record_cost_and_reliability():
    r = _record(successes=9, failures=1)
    assert r.cost_score == 2.0
    assert abs(r.reliability - 0.9) < 1e-9
    assert r.is_available()


def test_registry_select_filters_and_scores():
    reg = ModelRegistry()
    reg.records = {
        "p/fast": ModelRecord(
            model_id="p/fast", provider="p", modalities=frozenset({"text"}),
            price_input_per_1k=0.1, price_output_per_1k=0.1,
            successes=10, failures=0, last_checked=1000.0,
        ),
        "p/slow": ModelRecord(
            model_id="p/slow", provider="p", modalities=frozenset({"text"}),
            price_input_per_1k=10.0, price_output_per_1k=10.0,
            successes=5, failures=5, last_checked=1000.0,
        ),
    }
    pick = reg.select("fast_chat", modality="text", tools=False)
    assert pick is not None
    assert pick.model_id == "p/fast"


def test_registry_select_tools_filter():
    reg = ModelRegistry()
    reg.records = {
        "p/vision": ModelRecord(model_id="p/vision", provider="p", modalities=frozenset({"vision"}), tools=True),
        "p/text": ModelRecord(model_id="p/text", provider="p", modalities=frozenset({"text"}), tools=True),
    }
    pick = reg.select("planner", modality="text", tools=True)
    assert pick is not None
    assert pick.model_id == "p/text"


def test_registry_open_weights_never_guessed():
    reg = ModelRegistry()
    reg.records = {
        "p/a": ModelRecord(model_id="p/a", provider="p", modalities=frozenset({"text"}), open_weights="unknown"),
        "p/b": ModelRecord(model_id="p/b", provider="p", modalities=frozenset({"text"}), open_weights="yes"),
    }
    pick = reg.select("fast_chat", modality="text", prefer_open_weights=True)
    assert pick.model_id == "p/b"


def test_gateway_local_fallback_without_network(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("OMNI_LLM_BACKEND", "mock")
    gw = ModelGateway(registry=ModelRegistry(), dry_run=False)
    result = gw.complete([{"role": "user", "content": "hello"}], role="fast_chat")
    assert result.mock is True
    assert result.text
    assert result.role == "fast_chat"


def test_all_roles_have_capability_hints():
    for role in ROLES:
        assert role in DEFAULT_CAPABILITY
        assert "modality" in DEFAULT_CAPABILITY[role]


def test_headers_do_not_leak_key_when_none():
    headers = provider_headers("openrouter", None)
    assert "Authorization" not in headers

    headers = provider_headers("openrouter", "sk-secret")
    assert headers["Authorization"] == "Bearer sk-secret"


def test_get_api_key_env_only(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY", raising=False)
    assert get_api_key("openrouter") is None

=== tests/test_iars.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from omni.context.features import FEATURE_DIM
from omni.routing.agents import AgentSpec
from omni.routing.iars import BayesianLinearModel, IARS


def test_sherman_morrison_matches_direct_ridge() -> None:
    dim = 5
    prior = np.array([0.1, -0.2, 0.3, 0.0, 0.5])
    model = BayesianLinearModel(dim=dim, ridge=1.5, noise=0.7, prior_mean=prior)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
    observations = [(rng.standard_normal(dim), float(rng.standard_normal())) for _ in range(4)]
    for phi, reward in observations:
        model.update(phi, reward)
    precision = 1.5 * np.eye(dim)
    b = 1.5 * prior
    for phi, reward in observations:
        precision += (phi @ phi) / 0.7 * np.eye(dim) * 0.0 + np.outer(phi, phi) / 0.7
        b += reward * phi / 0.7
    direct = np.linalg.solve(precision, b)
    assert np.allclose(model.mean, direct, atol=1e-10)


def test_sampling_is_seeded() -> None:
    a = BayesianLinearModel(4, prior_mean=np.ones(4))
    b = BayesianLinearModel(4, prior_mean=np.ones(4))
    assert np.allclose(a.sample(np.random.default_rng(1)), b.sample(np.random.default_rng(1)))
    assert not np.allclose(a.sample(np.random.default_rng(1)), a.sample(np.random.default_rng(2)))


def test_implicit_reward_formula() -> None:
    reward = IARS.implicit_reward(accepted=True, undo_count=1, edit_distance=0.5, max_edit_distance=1.0, latency_s=15.0, latency_budget_s=30.0)
    assert abs(reward - (1.0 - 0.5 - 0.25 - 0.1)) < 1e-12
    assert IARS.implicit_reward(accepted=True, reverted=True) == 0.0


def _agent(agent_id: str) -> AgentSpec:
    return AgentSpec(id=agent_id, name=agent_id, description=agent_id, tags=(agent_id,), cost=0.0)


def test_prior_chooses_without_observations_and_update_raises_score() -> None:
    agents = [_agent("a"), _agent("b")]
    prior = np.zeros(FEATURE_DIM)
    prior[7] = 2.0
    router = IARS(agents=agents, prior_vectors={"a": prior, "b": np.zeros(FEATURE_DIM)}, tau=0.0, seed=1)
    phi = np.zeros(FEATURE_DIM)
    phi[7] = 1.0
    decision = router.decide(phi)
    assert decision.agent_id == "a"
    assert decision.explored is False
    before = float(router.models["b"].mean @ phi)
    router.observe(phi, "b", reward=1.0)
    after = float(router.models["b"].mean @ phi)
    assert after > before


def test_low_confidence_runs_top_two_verifier() -> None:
    agents = [_agent("a"), _agent("b")]
    router = IARS(agents=agents, tau=1.01, seed=1)
    decision = router.decide(np.ones(FEATURE_DIM), context_text="terminal python code")
    assert decision.explored is True
    assert len(decision.candidates) == 2
    assert decision.agent_id in decision.candidates
    assert decision.verification == "heuristic-lexical"
    assert 0.0 <= decision.confidence <= 1.0


def test_decide_serialises_scores() -> None:
    router = IARS(agents=[_agent("a")], tau=0.0)
    payload = router.decide(np.ones(FEATURE_DIM)).as_dict()
    assert payload["agent"] == "a"
    assert "a" in payload["scores"]

=== tests/test_llm_cache.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import numpy as np

from omni.llm.client import LLMClient
from omni.llm.embeddings import HashEmbedder


def test_hash_embedder_is_deterministic_and_normalised() -> None:
    embedder = HashEmbedder(dim=32)
    a = embedder.embed("edit a markdown document")
    b = embedder.embed("edit a markdown document")
    assert a == b
    assert abs(float(np.linalg.norm(a)) - 1.0) < 1e-9
    assert a != embedder.embed("browser tabs")


def test_mock_llm_is_cached_and_tagged(tmp_path) -> None:
    client = LLMClient(backend="mock", cache_dir=tmp_path)
    first = client.complete("hello", params={"temperature": 0.0})
    second = client.complete("hello", params={"temperature": 0.0})
    assert first.mock is True and second.mock is True
    assert first.cached is False and second.cached is True
    assert first.text == second.text
    assert first.cache_key == second.cache_key
    assert len(list((tmp_path / "llm").glob("*.json"))) == 1
    lines = (tmp_path / "llm_calls.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()
    assert lines and all(json.loads(line)["mock"] is True for line in lines)
    vectors = client.embed(["a", "b"])
    assert len(vectors) == 2 and len(vectors[0]) == 64

=== tests/test_modules.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json

from omni.modules.ampl import AMPL, ModuleResidency
from omni.modules.descriptor import AppDescriptor, infer_unit_demand
from omni.modules.library import DEFAULT_MODULES, ModuleLibrary, ModuleSpec
from omni.modules.memory import measure_memory
from omni.modules.units import UNITS


def test_library_is_valid() -> None:
    assert len(DEFAULT_MODULES) >= 8
    library = ModuleLibrary()
    assert library.total_size_mb() > 0
    for module in library.modules:
        assert module.size_mb > 0
        assert set(module.covers) <= set(UNITS)
        assert all(0 < p <= 1 for p in module.covers.values())


def test_known_and_unknown_app_demand() -> None:
    known = AppDescriptor(app_class="google-chrome", title="Inbox")
    demand, source = infer_unit_demand(known)
    assert source == "app+roles"
    assert max(demand, key=demand.get) == "browser-nav"

    unknown = AppDescriptor(app_class="mystery-app", atspi_roles={"text": 4, "entry": 2})
    demand, source = infer_unit_demand(unknown)
    assert source == "roles"
    assert "doc-edit" in demand and "forms" in demand

    generic = AppDescriptor(app_class="unknown")
    demand, source = infer_unit_demand(generic)
    assert source == "generic"
    assert "window-control" in demand


def test_greedy_respects_budget_and_uses_resident_modules() -> None:
    library = ModuleLibrary(
        (
            ModuleSpec("a", "A", "tool-schema", 10.0, 0.0, {"file-edit": 0.9}),
            ModuleSpec("b", "B", "tool-schema", 10.0, 0.0, {"clipboard": 0.9}),
            ModuleSpec("c", "C", "tool-schema", 25.0, 0.0, {"file-edit": 0.5, "clipboard": 0.5}),
        )
    )
    ampl = AMPL(library=library, max_budget_mb=20.0)
    descriptor = AppDescriptor(app_class="unknown", recent_actions={"write_file": 1}, clipboard_types=("text/plain",))
    plan = ampl.select(descriptor, budget_mb=20.0)
    assert plan.total_size_mb <= 20.0 + 1e-9
    assert plan.resident
    assert plan.params_m == 0.0
    assert plan.objective > 0
    assert plan.source in {"roles", "generic"}

    plan2 = ampl.select(descriptor, budget_mb=10.0, resident_ids=("a",))
    assert plan2.total_size_mb <= 10.0 + 1e-9
    assert "a" in plan2.resident


def test_capability_miss_boosts_weight_and_evicts_lowest_utility_per_byte() -> None:
    library = ModuleLibrary(
        (
            ModuleSpec("other", "Other", "tool-schema", 10.0, 0.0, {"clipboard": 0.9}),
            ModuleSpec("best", "Best", "tool-schema", 30.0, 0.0, {"file-edit": 0.9}),
        )
    )
    ampl = AMPL(library=library, max_budget_mb=30.0)
    descriptor = AppDescriptor(app_class="unknown")
    result = ampl.handle_miss("file-edit", descriptor, budget_mb=30.0, resident_ids=("other",))
    assert result.loaded is True
    assert result.best_module == "best"
    assert result.boosted_weight > 0
    assert result.resident == ("best",)
    assert "other" in result.evicted
    assert result.resident_mb <= 30.0
    assert ampl.unit_boost["file-edit"] == result.boosted_weight

    too_small = AMPL(library=library, max_budget_mb=20.0).handle_miss("file-edit", descriptor, budget_mb=20.0, resident_ids=())
    assert too_small.loaded is False


def test_memory_pressure_shrinks_budget(tmp_path) -> None:
    meminfo = tmp_path / "meminfo"
    meminfo.write_text("MemTotal: 8000000 kB\nMemAvailable: 4000000 kB\n")
    psi = tmp_path / "psi"
    psi.write_text("some avg10=10.00 avg60=1.00 avg300=0.10 total=0\n")
    state = measure_memory(max_budget_mb=256.0, meminfo_path=meminfo, psi_path=psi, cgroup_dir=tmp_path)
    assert state.mem_total_mb > 0
    assert abs(state.pressure - 0.2) < 1e-9
    assert abs(state.budget_mb - 204.8) < 1e-6

    meminfo.write_text("MemTotal: 8000000 kB\nMemAvailable: 100000 kB\n")
    low = measure_memory(max_budget_mb=256.0, meminfo_path=meminfo, psi_path=psi, cgroup_dir=tmp_path)
    assert low.pressure >= state.pressure
    assert low.budget_mb < state.budget_mb

    live = measure_memory()
    assert live.mem_total_mb > 0
    assert 0.0 <= live.pressure <= 1.0
    assert live.budget_mb > 0


def test_residency_accounts_size_and_params() -> None:
    residency = ModuleResidency()
    residency.set(("files-core", "terminal-tools"))
    assert residency.size_mb() == 22.0
    assert residency.params_m() == 0.0
    state = residency.state()
    assert state["resident"] == ["files-core", "terminal-tools"]
    assert state["resident_mb"] == 22.0


def test_ampl_plan_serialises() -> None:
    plan = AMPL().select(AppDescriptor(app_class="code", title="main.py"), budget_mb=100.0)
    payload = plan.as_dict()
    assert payload["resident"]
    assert payload["resident_mb"] <= 100.0
    assert json.dumps(payload)
