=== tests/test_routing_service.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import numpy as np

from omni.context.features import FEATURE_DIM, build_features, synthetic_snapshot
from omni.routing.agents import AgentSpec
from omni.routing.iars import IARS
from omni.routing.service import RoutingService


class FakeCollector:
    def start(self) -> bool:
        return True

    def close(self) -> None:
        pass

    def snapshot(self):
        return synthetic_snapshot("code", "routing.py", workspace_id=2, client_count=3, clipboard_types=("text/plain",))


def test_service_writes_hud_state_and_mock_tagged_log(tmp_path) -> None:
    agents = [
        AgentSpec(id="dev-terminal", name="Terminal Developer", description="terminal code python", tags=("terminal",)),
        AgentSpec(id="browser-ops", name="Browser Operator", description="browser web url", tags=("browser",)),
    ]
    prior = build_features(FakeCollector().snapshot()) * 5.0
    router = IARS(agents=agents, prior_vectors={"dev-terminal": prior, "browser-ops": np.zeros(FEATURE_DIM)}, tau=0.0)
    hud = tmp_path / "state.json"
    log = tmp_path / "decisions.jsonl"
    service = RoutingService(collector=FakeCollector(), iars=router, hud_path=hud, log_path=log, mock=True)
    payload = service.step()
    assert hud.exists()
    state = json.loads(hud.read_text())
    assert state["mock"] is True
    assert state["routing"]["agent"] == "dev-terminal"
    assert state["modules"]["resident"] == []
    assert state["scheduler"]["policy"] == "default"
    assert state["privacy"]["raw_keys_stored"] is False
    record = json.loads(log.read_text().splitlines()[0])
    assert record["mock"] is True
    assert record["event"] == "routing_decision"
    assert record["features_dim"] == FEATURE_DIM
    assert payload["routing"]["agent"] == "dev-terminal"


def test_service_with_ampl_loads_modules_and_accounts_memory(tmp_path) -> None:
    from omni.modules.ampl import AMPL, ModuleResidency

    ampl = AMPL(max_budget_mb=256.0)
    service = RoutingService(
        collector=FakeCollector(),
        hud_path=tmp_path / "state.json",
        log_path=tmp_path / "decisions.jsonl",
        mock=True,
        ampl=ampl,
        residency=ModuleResidency(ampl.library),
    )
    payload = service.step()
    modules = payload["modules"]
    assert modules["status"] == "active"
    assert modules["resident"]
    assert modules["resident_mb"] > 0
    assert modules["resident_mb"] <= modules["budget_mb"]
    assert modules["params_m"] >= 0
    assert modules["memory"]["mem_total_mb"] > 0
    assert 0.0 <= modules["memory"]["pressure"] <= 1.0
    assert (tmp_path / "modules.jsonl").exists()
    records = [json.loads(line) for line in (tmp_path / "modules.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
    assert records and all(record["mock"] is True for record in records)
    routing = json.loads((tmp_path / "decisions.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()[0])
    assert routing["modules_resident"] == modules["resident"]
    assert routing["module_budget_mb"] == modules["budget_mb"]

=== tests/test_safety.py ===
from omni.safety.policy import SafetyPolicy
from omni.schemas.models import ActionValidationError


def test_path_traversal_rejected():
    policy = SafetyPolicy("/tmp/omni-safe")
    try:
        policy.validate_action("write_file", {"path": "/tmp/omni-other.txt"})
        assert False, "Expected path escape to fail"
    except ActionValidationError:
        pass


def test_unknown_action_rejected():
    policy = SafetyPolicy("/tmp/omni-safe")
    try:
        policy.validate_action("arbitrary_shell", {"path": "/tmp/omni-safe/out.txt"})
        assert False, "Expected unknown action to fail"
    except ActionValidationError:
        pass


def test_allowed_write_under_workspace():
    policy = SafetyPolicy("/tmp/omni-safe")
    args = policy.validate_action("write_file", {"path": "/tmp/omni-safe/project/example.txt"})
    assert args["path"] == "/tmp/omni-safe/project/example.txt"

=== tests/test_scheduler.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import numpy as np

from omni.context.features import synthetic_snapshot
from omni.routing.service import RoutingService
from omni.scheduler.actuator import CgroupV2Actuator
from omni.scheduler.context import ScheduleFeatures, collect_features
from omni.scheduler.krls import KRLS
from omni.scheduler.policy import ACTIONS_BY_ID, KRLSPolicy, heuristic_scores
from omni.scheduler.service import SchedulerService
from omni.scheduler.simulator import simulate


class FakeCollector:
    def start(self) -> bool:
        return True

    def close(self) -> None:
        pass

    def snapshot(self):
        return synthetic_snapshot("google-chrome", "Inbox", workspace_id=1, client_count=4)


def test_krls_fits_nonlinear_function_online() -> None:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    xs = rng.random((240, 2))
    ys = np.sin(3 * xs[:, 0]) + xs[:, 1] ** 2
    model = KRLS(dim=2, gamma=2.0, budget=40, ald_threshold=0.01)
    early = model.predict(xs[-1])
    for x, y in zip(xs[:160], ys[:160]):
        model.update(x, y)
    errors = [abs(model.predict(x) - y) for x, y in zip(xs[160:], ys[160:])]
    assert len(model.centers) <= 40
    assert model.n_updates == 160
    assert float(np.mean(errors)) < 0.15
    assert abs(early) < 2.0


def test_policy_heuristic_prefers_action_by_context() -> None:
    interactive = ScheduleFeatures(load_norm=0.4, cpu_pressure=0.1, mem_pressure=0.1, client_norm=0.3, interactive=1.0, focus_age_norm=0.5)
    pressure = ScheduleFeatures(load_norm=0.9, cpu_pressure=0.5, mem_pressure=0.8, client_norm=0.8, interactive=0.0, focus_age_norm=0.5)
    policy = KRLSPolicy()
    assert policy.decide(interactive).action == "boost"
    assert policy.decide(pressure).action == "throttle"
    assert heuristic_scores(interactive)["boost"] > heuristic_scores(interactive)["throttle"]


def test_simulated_krls_beats_balanced_baseline() -> None:
    for scenario in ("S1", "S2", "S3"):
        result = simulate(scenario, steps=400, test_steps=120, seed=0)
        assert result["simulated"] is True
        assert result["updates"] > 0
        assert result["mean_reward"] > result["balanced_reward"]
        assert result["mean_reward"] <= result["oracle_reward"] + 1e-9
    assert simulate("S1", steps=400, test_steps=120, seed=0)["eval_actions"]["boost"] == 120
    assert simulate("S2", steps=400, test_steps=120, seed=0)["eval_actions"]["throttle"] == 120


def test_context_features_are_bounded_and_read_live_signals() -> None:
    snapshot = synthetic_snapshot("code", "routing.py", client_count=5)
    features = collect_features(snapshot)
    payload = features.as_dict()
    assert payload["interactive"] == 1.0
    assert 0.0 <= payload["load_norm"] <= 1.0
    assert 0.0 <= payload["cpu_pressure"] <= 1.0
    assert 0.0 <= payload["mem_pressure"] <= 1.0
    assert 0.0 <= payload["client_norm"] <= 1.0


def test_cgroup_actuator_dry_run_apply_and_kill_switch(tmp_path) -> None:
    base = tmp_path / "cgroup"
    group = base / "omni-sched"
    group.mkdir(parents=True)
    (group / "cpu.weight").write_text("100\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (group / "io.weight").write_text("default 100\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (group / "cgroup.procs").write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    state = tmp_path / "actuator.json"
    actuator = CgroupV2Actuator(base=base, state_path=state)

    assert actuator.available() is True
    dry = actuator.apply(ACTIONS_BY_ID["boost"], dry_run=True)
    assert dry["applied"] is False and dry["dry_run"] is True
    assert (group / "cpu.weight").read_text() == "100\n"

    applied = actuator.apply(ACTIONS_BY_ID["boost"], pid=1234, dry_run=False)
    assert applied["applied"] is True
    assert applied["written"]["cpu.weight"] == 300
    assert applied["written"]["pid"] == 1234
    assert (group / "cpu.weight").read_text() == "300\n"
    assert (group / "io.weight").read_text() == "default 300\n"
    assert (group / "cgroup.procs").read_text() == "1234\n"
    assert state.exists()

    restored = actuator.restore()
    assert restored["kill_switch"] == "restored"
    assert (group / "cpu.weight").read_text() == "100\n"
    assert (group / "io.weight").read_text() == "default 100\n"
    assert not state.exists()


def test_cgroup_actuator_reports_unavailable(tmp_path) -> None:
    actuator = CgroupV2Actuator(base=tmp_path / "missing", state_path=tmp_path / "state.json")
    assert actuator.available() is False
    result = actuator.apply(ACTIONS_BY_ID["balanced"], dry_run=False)
    assert result["applied"] is False
    assert "no writable" in result["reason"]


def test_scheduler_service_payload_and_optional_apply(tmp_path) -> None:
    base = tmp_path / "cgroup"
    group = base / "omni-sched"
    group.mkdir(parents=True)
    (group / "cpu.weight").write_text("100\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (group / "io.weight").write_text("default 100\n", encoding="utf-8")
    actuator = CgroupV2Actuator(base=base, state_path=tmp_path / "state.json")
    service = SchedulerService(actuator=actuator)
    payload = service.step(FakeCollector().snapshot())
    assert payload["action"] in {"boost", "balanced", "throttle"}
    assert payload["policy"] == "heuristic-prior"
    assert payload["applied"]["dry_run"] is True
    assert payload["actuator"]["available"] is True
    assert 0.0 <= payload["memory"]["pressure"] <= 1.0
    assert json.dumps(payload)

    live = service.step(FakeCollector().snapshot(), apply=True)
    assert live["applied"]["applied"] is True
    assert (group / "cpu.weight").read_text() == f"{live['cpu_weight']}\n"
    service.restore()
    assert (group / "cpu.weight").read_text() == "100\n"


def test_routing_service_publishes_scheduler_decision(tmp_path) -> None:
    service = RoutingService(
        collector=FakeCollector(),
        hud_path=tmp_path / "state.json",
        log_path=tmp_path / "decisions.jsonl",
        mock=True,
        scheduler=SchedulerService(actuator=CgroupV2Actuator(base=tmp_path / "missing", state_path=tmp_path / "a.json")),
    )
    payload = service.step()
    scheduler = payload["scheduler"]
    assert scheduler["status"] == "active"
    assert scheduler["policy"] == "heuristic-prior"
    assert scheduler["applied"]["dry_run"] is True
    state = json.loads((tmp_path / "state.json").read_text())
    assert state["scheduler"]["action"] == scheduler["action"]
    record = json.loads((tmp_path / "decisions.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()[0])
    assert record["scheduler_action"] == scheduler["action"]

    disabled = RoutingService(
        collector=FakeCollector(),
        hud_path=tmp_path / "state2.json",
        log_path=tmp_path / "decisions2.jsonl",
        mock=True,
    )
    assert disabled.step()["scheduler"]["status"] == "disabled"

from omni.planner.demo import DemoPlanner
from omni.schemas.models import ActionPermission, TaskAction


def test_valid_demo_plan():
    planner = DemoPlanner(workspace_root="/tmp/omni-test")
    plan = planner.generate_plan("Create a Python project in my workspace, run the tests, and repair the implementation.", "t-1")
    assert plan.task_id == "t-1"
    assert len(plan.steps) > 0
    assert plan.steps[0].name == "inspect_desktop"


def test_invalid_action_name_rejected():
    action = TaskAction(id="a1", name="bad_action", description="bad", arguments={}, timeout=10.0)
    assert action.name == "bad_action"


def test_action_permission_exists():
    assert ActionPermission.SAFE.value == "safe"


def test_demo_planner_handles_empty_task():
    planner = DemoPlanner(workspace_root="/tmp/omni-test")
    try:
        planner.generate_plan("", "task")
        assert False, "Expected ValueError for empty task"
    except ValueError:
        pass

=== tests/test_tools.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from omni.tools.discovery import ToolRegistry, discover_all, _categorize, _tier


def test_tier_destructive_keywords():
    assert _tier("rm", "delete a file") == "destructive"
    assert _tier("install", "install a package") == "destructive"
    assert _tier("cat", "read a file") == "read-only"


def test_categorize():
    assert _categorize("firefox", "web browser", "firefox") == "web"
    assert _categorize("gimp", "image editor", "gimp") == "media"
    assert _categorize("python3", "python interpreter", "python3") == "code"
    assert _categorize("nautilus", "file manager", "nautilus") == "files"
    assert _categorize("hyprctl", "hyprland control", "hyprctl") == "system"
    assert _categorize("libreoffice", "office suite", "libreoffice") == "documents"
    assert _categorize("thunderbird", "email client", "thunderbird") == "communication"
    assert _categorize("xed", "a text editor", "xed") == "general"


def test_discover_all_includes_builtin_file_tools():
    tools = discover_all()
    ids = {t.id for t in tools}
    assert "file:read" in ids
    assert "file:write" in ids
    assert "file:delete" in ids
    delete = next(t for t in tools if t.id == "file:delete")
    assert delete.destructive is True
    assert delete.requires_confirmation is True


def test_discover_all_deduplicates_and_stable():
    tools = discover_all()
    ids = [t.id for t in tools]
    assert len(ids) == len(set(ids))


def test_registry_refresh_and_queries():
    reg = ToolRegistry(ttl_seconds=0)
    reg.refresh(force=True)
    assert len(reg.tools) > 0
    by_cat = reg.by_category("system")
    assert any(t.kind == "hyprland" for t in by_cat) or len(by_cat) >= 0
    des = reg.destructive()
    assert any("delete" in t.id or t.destructive for t in des)
    reg.refresh(force=False)  # cached, no-op quickly
    assert len(reg.tools) > 0


def test_tool_spec_serialization():
    tools = discover_all()
    for t in tools:
        d = t.as_dict()
        assert d["id"] == t.id
        assert d["kind"] == t.kind
        assert d["safety_tier"] == t.safety_tier
